　Evidence-gathering form (Column B; the facts recorded on the evidence cards have not been independently verified): FRET readings are population averages over several hundred cells per field of view, repeated over different cultures, and fall under classes D2 and D1; by 13-E they read back only to support existence and (when M1 \> B₀) the support fraction, while P\_HC^∃ further requires cell-by-cell availability, or FORM\_PROTO together with the availability fraction; the sampling unit is the culture. Site-directed Q/E substitution fixes the modification state and is in form a setting of T; the value deviation in “Q is equivalent to methylated E” falls under class Ca, and the route deviation under class Cb; the Tsr modification sites are a latent side-effect neighbourhood, falling under class B, and near the boundary of the valid domain the legitimate side effects can only be one-sided and are counted through ρ\_i(s); each fixed state is a different strain, so the comparison is across instances and goes through cross-record composition. The cheR and cheB deletion strains are lineage-type removals and by C-1 yield only history-level conclusions, further requiring mechanism correspondence, compensation and Δ\_ent. Mutations of the CheB D56E type rewrite the law of the process; by C-2 this is a read-side setting, which certifies the dependence of the demethylation process on activity but not its dependence on T. The modification level is inferred from activity through a model, so it is a model-mediated reading and falls under C-3.  
　Sticking point: whether “nonzero partial derivative at s” amounts to HistCond support for instances of the continuous class requires an upstream reading (D15 of *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* defines Supp\_act only at the F layer; U9 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts*); until then, a conclusion claiming HistCond requires that bridge, while conditional conclusions in partial-derivative form, and the testable predictions based on them (activity-response differences between fixed Q/E strains exceeding the budget), can be put forward conditionally. L\_N, ζ\_b, β and the activity mapping constant are Column B premises and remain unverified.  
8.3  Evidence-Gathering Forms for Other Candidates (Column B; the facts recorded on the evidence cards have not been independently verified)  
　*Physarum polycephalum*: cutting a tube induces compensation in other tube segments, which falls under class A; the cut itself is an attractive stimulus, so the intervention is itself a signal input, which falls under class Cb and may leave Λ; local cooling is not exclusive, which falls under class B. Memristive nanowire networks: the premise that the reset network is the same as the original network remains to be verified, and resettability for paired histories holds only approximately; this is better treated as an instance-correspondence problem within cross-record composition and does not fall under class C.

9\. Citation Table of Certificate Rules (bound to *The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization* v1.0)  
General discipline: (1) The output is only 1 or ⊥, and ⊥ implies neither 0 nor any direction. (2) The rules are sound when the channel assumptions hold; reading back to a material host further requires IMP, Dom4 and implementation fidelity. (3) The three columns are kept separate. (4) A certificate must state its information domain and is not cited across levels (3.4). (5) Under the statistical reading, several rules used together follow the union bound (D-1), and adaptive selection follows D-4.  
Format of each entry: name (location; status)  domain and antecedents \| information domain \| output and bounds. Entries with status “lemma” are direct generalizations of upstream rules.  
1\. Verdicts of 1 at the atom level  
　13-A (5.1; lemma)  F-class single-beat KO\~, \|κ\| ≤ γ, \|e\| ≤ r₀ \| the KO\~ reading of the process \| \|D\| \> γ + r₀ gives Act = 1; a 1 is guaranteed when \|c\| \> 2(γ + r₀); ⊥ where \|D\| ≤ γ + r₀ (maximal).  
　D-1′ (5.1; lemma)  replica state discrepancy ≤ ξ, linear law \| as above \| for ξ = 0 use 13-A; for ξ \> 0 use \|D\| \> ε + γ + r + Λ\_m ξ.  
　13-B (5.2; theorem)  the three availability items, LOC\~ under Λ and LD, H0 budget b \| I\_pair \| dist(I(y₀), I(y₁)) \> b gives support, which together with the three availability items gives P\_HC = 1; b\_cons = Lβ + ζ\_b is always sound; b\_legal = Lρ\_i(s) + ζ\_b (linear laws with intercept, optional background, I\_pair) is realizable and tight (13-B3); b\_cons − b\_legal = L(β − ρ\_i(s)).  
　13-B1, 13-B1′ (5.2; lemma)  the margin b + 2r is not sound; \|y₁ − y₀\| \> B₀ + 2r is sound, and for b \> 0 it is dominated by RLC.  
　13-B2 (5.2; lemma)  ζ does not enter the budget; ζ\_b is additive and cannot be removed.  
　13-C (5.2; lemma)  intercept-free linear subclass, s\_min \> 0 \| I\_pair \| RLC\* is sound; not sound with an intercept; tight only on SD (13-D(4)).  
　D-5 (5.3; theorem)  bilinear laws with intercept, \|k\| ≤ K, s\_i \> ε ≥ 0 (suprathreshold antecedent; full P\_HC still requires membership in T and the Form record) \| I\_pair \| the budget K·max(V, V\_×) + ζ\_b is sound and maximal; the closed form of the same type for a guaranteed 1 holds only under the effective single-segment antecedent; for linear ∪ bilinear take the max; s\_i = 0 gives no budget.  
　D-3 (5.5; lemma)  shared-channel deviation \|o\| ≤ τ\_ch \| reading pair \| the joint compatible-set criterion is sound and stronger than adding the radii separately.  
　C-2 (5.8; lemma)  law rewriting \| reading pair \| dist \> ζ\_b gives dependence on R; dependence on T is not certified unless R ⊆ T.  
　C-3 (5.8; lemma)  model-mediated readings \| preimage compatible set \| saturation weakens identification but does not by itself give ⊥.  
　E-1, E-2 (5.9; lemma)  Lipschitz laws, H0^box; SD\_i \| reading pair \| budget L\_N·ρ\_i(s) + ζ\_b; under SD\_i, certificate ⇒ nonzero partial derivative at s.  
2\. Identifiability boundaries on the valid domain  
　13-D(1) (5.3; theorem)  linear laws with intercept, L undeclared, a realizable nonzero legitimate side effect exists \| I\_full (without cross-beat law invariance) \| non-identifiable; L is not needed when ρ\_i(s) = 0.  
　13-D(2)(3) (5.3; theorem)  linear laws with intercept, L declared, known baseline, optional background \| I\_pair \| RLC\_{b\_legal} is maximal (open endpoints excepted); under all the effective single-segment antecedents of 13-D(3) (in every legitimate realization, c₀, c₁ and the readings all satisfy \|c\| \> ε + 2r), a 1 is guaranteed if and only if min\|c₁ − c₀\| \> Lρ\_i(s) + ζ\_b + 4r; when subthreshold readings occur, the compatible-set criterion is used instead of this closed form (Example 5.3-3); not transferred to I\_full, SH or the bilinear class.  
　D-6, D-6′ (5.3; theorem)  linear H0 with intercept, laws of the other processes frozen and known, inventory frozen, nonnegative successors \| I\_full \| exact characterization by the true compatible set; computable when the other processes are affine (structural family implemented); corroboration channels can pin down the side effects.  
　D-7 (5.3; theorem)  linear H0 with intercept, optional background, tgt ∉ N\_i \| I\_pair \| sufficient condition for SH to have no effect: \|d\| + b + ε ≤ H\_tgt; under the bilinear cross budget, the cases tgt = i and tgt ∈ N\_i are not inherited unconditionally.  
3\. Populations  
　13-E(1) (5.4; theorem)  \| population readings \| \|ȳ₁ − ȳ₀\| \> B₀ + 2r gives SuppEx = 1; certifies neither availability nor P\_HC.  
　13-E(2), D-10 (5.4; theorem)  M1 \> B₀, B₀ \< d ≤ M1 \| population readings \| f ≥ (d − B₀)/(M1 − B₀), and ⌈N·f\*⌉/N when N is known (tight in the constructible family); no nontrivial lower bound when M1 ≤ B₀; d \> max(M1, B₀) is incompatible and yields no certificate.  
　13-E(3)(4) (5.4; theorem)  linear laws, the same read multiset, 0 ∉ Θ, availability individual by individual \| population readings \| read-back to every individual; not certifiable when Θ contains 0.  
　13-E(5), 13-E′, D-9 (5.4; theorem)  \| population readings and availability \| f\_low + a\_low \> 1 ⇒ P\_HC^∃ = 1; under FORM\_PROTO, a\_low = (m̄ − r\_s − ε)/(hi − ε); for a partial protocol, a\_thr − (1 − p); for spot checks without replacement, â − t\_n.  
　D-8 (5.4; theorem)  structurally homogeneous bilinear, 0 ∉ Θ, hi\_j \> 0 \| state mean \| universal criterion m̄ − r \> (1 − 1/N)·hi\_j (tight); support fraction max(0, (m̄ − r)/hi\_j).  
4\. Statistics and composition  
　13-F (5.5; lemma)  sampling units as in 4.3 of *The Adjudication Form of the Sedimentation-Field* \| statistical reading \| r = γ + min(τ, t\_n).  
　D-1 (5.5; theorem)  \| atomic certificates \| the misjudgment rate of a composite verdict is ≤ the sum of the misjudgment rates of the atoms tested; multi-beat atoms are taken from replica units.  
　D-2 (5.5; lemma)  equal clusters \| statistical reading \| nested radius t₁ + t₂.  
　D-4 (5.5; lemma)  pre-declared candidate family \| statistical reading \| Bonferroni, sample splitting; bounded-reading certificates have zero misjudgment under any selection rule.  
5\. The gating class  
　PA-1, PA-2, R1′–R3′ (3.6; theorem)  closure and unique successor of 𝒦\_PA^δ and 𝒦\_PA^≺; same-beat cycles lie outside the working domain.  
　G-1, G-2 (5.6; theorem)  𝒦\_g at the same beat, side-effect bound Ξ\_j \| gating-input readings \| \|z₁ − z₀\| \> Ξ\_j + 2r\_v; robust threshold crossing ⇒ FD\_𝒟₁(e) = 1, and then η(e) = 1 through Lemma 3.2 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts*.  
　G-3 (5.6; theorem)  𝔇\_g (visible label sequence kept separate from the complete input; pat and I not frozen) \| firing readings \| non-identifiable when an implementable nonzero side effect in the same direction exists; not transferred to protocols with frozen complete input.  
　M-1 (5.7; theorem)  𝒦\_g, 𝒦\_PA^3, 𝒦\_PA^δ, 𝒦\_PA^≺ \| I\_model^m, readings covering all gating units \| all margins hold under the propagation upper bound with weight-difference coefficient 1 − ρ·\[plastic\], and the verdict is 1 under Table 5.7-1 ⇒ FD\_𝒟₁(e) = 1; only 𝒦\_g further obtains η; the zero-channel correspondence is limited to the event families of Table 5.7-1 and is not equivalent to D1; the margin condition is only sufficient.  
　M-2 (5.7; theorem)  single-output 𝒦\_g, λ \> 0, v with a legitimate nonzero side effect, single-flip type \| 𝔇\_g^m \| non-identifiable, only up to t\_e; not a negative determination; citations must carry over the twin's antecedents on parameters, initial state, global labels and pat\_B.  
6\. Comparative observation families and cross-record composition  
　C-1 (5.8; theorem)  shared history and entry state before the deletion start \| instance pair \| difference \> γ\_del + 2r ⇒ Act(m) = 1 at some beat in \[t\_d, t\_e); with t\_d the entry of W the conclusion is within-window, while deletion from the start yields only history-level conclusions; real deletion strains further require Δ\_ent.  
　M2-1, M2-2, M2-3 (5.8; theorem)  V\_Γ: μ and τ conditions \| record and target \| transfer of linear support, Act (c\_low − Δ\_tr \> ε) and bilinear support; formation atoms are not transferred. Cases with the same object, the same T and the same process inventory belong to this paper; the rest belong to Chapter 15 of the Field Concretization Group Form.  
　I-1 (5.9; theorem)  RF, same activation set, corrected injection (\|Î − I\| ≤ τ\_I), SAM\_W′ or γ\_o, n\_P \| corrected read-out z\_corr = z − Î \| \|Δz\_corr\| \> γ\_o + 2r + ε·n\_P (r = τ + ζ\_RF + τ\_I) gives that some event of P is effective; no certificate when only the injection changes.  
7\. Conditional conclusions for the target interfaces  
　F-1 (8.1; Column A conditional theorem)  Φ\_fly ∈ 𝒦\_PA^δ (Note F-1≺: ∈ 𝒦\_PA^≺), cited only under the single declared version \| I\_model^m \| (1) event-level FD; (2) input-level shift; (3) blockade; (4) fixed-reading threshold and a priori design threshold; (5) DAN flip under robust threshold crossing or a certificate for YIN\_D@t₂; (6) event-by-event declaration at t₂. FD-level predictions can be cited conditionally; claiming HistCond requires the FD → η bridge.  
　B-1 (8.2; Column A conditional theorem)  Lipschitz, SD\_i, C-3, D-2 \| preimage compatible set \| conditional conclusion in partial-derivative form; the HistCond reading depends on the upstream.  
Hints for downstream citation: the “upper bound on side effects” of 5.5 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* can be declared in the budget forms of 13-B, D-5, D-6, G-1, G-2 and M-1; verdicts of 1 for the gating class in measurement mode use G-1, G-2 and M-1, and when only firing is read the information-domain limits of G-3 and M-2 are cited; class definitions for extensions of the gating class can follow PA-1 and PA-2. Chapter 19 of the Field Concretization Group Form, *The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Compartmentalized Third-Factor Circuits*: EPSC-type readings follow I-1; pooling of several flies follows D-2 and D-4; history-type settings across flies follow C-1 and cross-record composition; the fly return loop follows the layered criterion of F-1 — the DAN input shift by (2), changes in DAN firing and in plasticity events event by event under (6) only when the crossing condition of (5) holds or YIN\_D@t₂ has been given 1 under M-1, and positive M-1 determinations for other events issue only an FD-level change of that event. Chapter 21 of the Field Concretization Group Form, *The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields*: the first layer and the sampling-unit questions are that paper's own responsibility; verdicts of 1 in measurement mode at the second layer that use incomplete interventions and population readings follow 13-B, 13-E and D-1–D-4.

10\. Declaration of Strength  
　Proved (with proofs): closure and nonemptiness of Definitions 1–7, G-0 and MG-0; Proposition 3.5-1; PA-1, PA-2; R1′–R3′; 13-A, D-1′; 13-B, 13-B3, 13-B1, 13-B1′, 13-B2, 13-C, degenerate compatibility; 13-D(1)–(4) (each with the information domain and subdomain noted); D-5 (available domain of the tested position); D-6 and D-6′ (characterization by the true compatible set; computability limited to affine other processes); D-7 (subclass tgt ∉ N\_i); 13-E(1)–(5), 13-E′; D-8 (hi\_j \> 0), D-9, D-10; 13-F, D-1, D-2 (equal clusters), D-3, D-4; G-1 and its corollary, G-2, G-3 (within 𝔇\_g); M-1 (four classes, all kinds in Table 5.7-1); M-2 (single output, single-flip type, 𝔇\_g^m, \[0, t\_e\]); C-1, C-2, C-3; M2-1, M2-2, M2-3; I-1; E-1, E-2.  
　Conditional conclusions (Column A conditional theorems at the model layer): F-1(1)–(6) and Note F-1≺; B-1.  
　Finite verification by programs: the readings listed in Appendix B; they cover their generating domains, and universal conclusions rest on the proofs in Sections 5 and 8.  
　Not issued:  
　　that any real host satisfies the declared channel or implementation fidelity;  
　　duties or Sedimentation-Field qualification of any material host (Column C is empty);  
　　η(e) and HistCond for the gated extension class (the FD → η bridge has not been established); HistCond for instances of the continuous class;  
　　negative verdicts (measurement mode does not give 0);  
　　necessity of the M-1 margin condition; multi-beat certificates under partial gating readings; general flip types of M-2, and anything after t\_e;  
　　any conclusion about the class of same-beat cyclic firing inputs;  
　　the open items listed in Section 11.  
　Evidence levels: mathematical proofs and finite verification are listed separately; Columns A, B and C are kept separate, and model conclusions, conditional inferences about real hosts and empirical certification do not substitute for one another.

12\. Interfaces  
　*The Adjudication Form of the Sedimentation-Field*: under imperfect channels, this paper gives criteria for verdicts of 1 and non-certifiability bounds for that paper's second-layer atoms; for Λ = 0 it degenerates to that paper's 4.6(1)(2) (degenerate compatibility in 5.2); the sampling-unit clause is generalized from its 4.3 to the second layer and to cluster structure (13-F, D-2); “the physical legitimacy of knockout and local setting in measurement” in its U9 is taken up here through channel declarations and identifiability boundaries, while physical legitimacy itself remains a declaration on the host side.  
　*The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts*: this paper gives the budget forms and criteria (13-B, D-5, D-6, G-1, G-2, M-1) for its 5.5 clause “reading back to a material host further requires implementation fidelity (an upper bound on side effects)”; the class definitions and closure of the extensions of the gating class (PA-1, PA-2, R1′–R3′) are available for its U8 to cite; same-beat cycles are not included; the FD → η bridge remains with its U8 (U13-16).  
　*The Indistinguishability Form of Field Concretization*: the twins of this paper are indistinguishability at the realization layer under bounded channels, with information sets I\_pair, I\_full, 𝔇\_g and 𝔇\_g^m; indistinguishability for exact observation sets is cited from that paper and not re-proved.  
　*The Mathematical Form of Duty Correspondence in Field Concretization* (Chapter 15 of the Field Concretization Group Form): composition of records with the same object, the same T and the same process inventory but different runs, regimes or measurements belongs to this paper (5.8); the rest belongs to that paper.  
　Chapter 19 of the Field Concretization Group Form, *The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Compartmentalized Third-Factor Circuits*: EPSC read-out (I-1), FD-level conditional predictions for the fly return loop (the layered criterion of F-1), pooling of several flies and composition across flies (D-2, D-4, C-1, M2).  
　Chapter 21 of the Field Concretization Group Form, *The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields*: evidence rules for verdicts of 1 in measurement mode at the second layer (13-B, 13-E, D-1–D-4); the first layer and the sampling-unit questions are that paper's own responsibility.  
　Registration of the 17 candidates: the material verdict for each candidate is ⊥, with no 0; experimental gaps are registered as pending verification and are not read as negative determinations.

## Appendix A  Program Source

The following 11 programs run in the same directory and use only the Python standard library, with no third-party dependencies; all numerical values are computed exactly with fractions.Fraction (Hoeffding radii are given as rational upper bounds). A.1–A.5 are only imported; A.6–A.11 are the run entry points. The programs give finite verification, exact witnesses and twin constructions; random generation covers only its generating domain, and universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.  
A.1  t13\_core.py  Core library for imperfect channels on F-class single beats: compatible sets, legitimate side-effect intervals and budgets, RLC, RLC\*, constant margins, legitimate twins, population rules, Hoeffding radii (3.3, 3.4, 5.1–5.5)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization: core library for F-class single-beat imperfect channels. Exact rational arithmetic, Python standard library only.
Contents: the legitimate side-effect interval and the legitimate radius rho (frozen state box [0, hi_j]); the exact H0 budget on the valid domain, b_legal = L·rho + zeta_b;
the legitimate twin construction twin_legal (the constructive proof of 13-D (2)); the constant-margin rule diff_rule (Lemma 13-B1′); the availability atom avail_ok (Form evidence and the suprathreshold reading kept separate);
populations: support existence pop_supp_exists, the corrected lower bound on the fraction pop_fraction (antecedent M1 > B0 and incompatibility test), the lower bound on the availability fraction avail_fraction_lower,
and the intersection of support and availability pop_hc_exists (13-E (5)).
Object: the F-class single-beat mechanisms of Chapter 3 (constant, linear and bilinear laws; functional support per D15 of the Mathematical Form; 4.2 books only effective components),
overlaid with the imperfect observation channel Y_mat declared in Definition 2 of the main text:
  A  compensation (KO~ reading bias |kappa| <= gamma)
  B  setting side effects (LOC~_i shifts each position in the declared neighbourhood N_i by |eta_j| <= beta; not read out)
  C  substitute setting (Ca setting-value deviation |delta' - delta| <= zeta; Cb bypass: the process component is shifted directly, |omega| <= zeta_b)
  D1 measurement error (reading error supported on [-tau, tau]; bounded reading, or statistical reading over n independent sampling units)
  D2 population reading (mean over N individuals, individual parameters within the declared intervals)
Criteria:
  act_rule     verdict-1 rule for Act (Proposition 13-A)
  rlc_rule     HistCond local-response certificate, Lipschitz budget (Proposition 13-B; maximal among criteria that use only this process's readings, Proposition 13-D)
  rlc_star     HistCond baseline-calibration certificate, on the declared subclass of “intercept-free linear laws” (Proposition 13-C)
  pop_exists   population existence-type certificate (Proposition 13-E)
  ch3_R        exact rule of 4.6 (2) of Chapter 3, R_m(i) = [hat c(s~) != hat c(s)] (for comparison)
All values are fractions.Fraction; no floating point is used.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from math import comb
ZERO = F(0)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- F-class laws and functional support
def value(law, s):
    """F laws of D12 of the Mathematical Form: ('const', b) | ('lin', k, reads[, b]) | ('bil', k, (j1, j2)[, b]);
    reads is a multiset (tuple); the optional fourth component b is the intercept (D12: c = b + k·Σ s(j), c = b + k·s(j1)·s(j2)), default 0."""
    kind = law[0]
    if kind == 'const':
        return F(law[1])
    b0 = F(law[3]) if len(law) > 3 else ZERO
    if kind == 'lin':
        return b0 + F(law[1]) * sum((F(s[j]) for j in law[2]), ZERO)
    if kind == 'bil':
        j1, j2 = law[2]
        return b0 + F(law[1]) * F(s[j1]) * F(s[j2])
    raise ValueError(law)

def hat(c, eps):
    """4.2 of the Mathematical Form: book only effective components."""
    return c if abs(c) > eps else ZERO

def supp_act(law, s, i):
    """D15 of the Mathematical Form: the read positions on which the numerical law has a nonzero partial derivative."""
    kind = law[0]
    if kind == 'const':
        return False
    k = F(law[1])
    if kind == 'lin':
        return k != 0 and i in law[2]
    j1, j2 = law[2]
    return k != 0 and ((j1 == i and F(s[j2]) != 0) or (j2 == i and F(s[j1]) != 0))

def reads(law):
    if law[0] == 'const':
        return ()
    if law[0] == 'lin':
        return tuple(law[2])
    return tuple(law[2])

def lipschitz_on(law, s, nbhd, beta, hi):
    """Supremum of the change in the component under the true law on the neighbourhood nbhd for shifts of at most beta (used to check whether the declared L is truthful)."""
    kind = law[0]
    if kind == 'const':
        return ZERO
    k = abs(F(law[1]))
    if kind == 'lin':
        return k * sum(1 for j in law[2] if j in nbhd) * beta
    j1, j2 = law[2]
    a = beta if j1 in nbhd else ZERO
    b = beta if j2 in nbhd else ZERO
    if j1 == j2:
        a = beta if j1 in nbhd else ZERO
        return k * (2 * F(s[j1]) * a + a * a)
    return k * (F(s[j1]) * b + F(s[j2]) * a + a * b)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- intervals and compatible sets
def consistent(y, r, eps):
    """I(y; r, eps): closure of the set of true components c compatible with reading y and radius r, {c : hat(c) in [y - r, y + r]}; returns a list of closed intervals."""
    lo, hi = y - r, y + r
    out = []
    if lo < -eps:
        out.append((lo, min(hi, -eps)))
    if hi > eps:
        out.append((max(lo, eps), hi))
    if lo <= 0 <= hi:
        out.append((-eps, eps))
    return [p for p in out if p[0] <= p[1]]

def gap(p, q):
    return max(ZERO, q[0] - p[1], p[0] - q[1])

def dist_sets(A, B):
    return min(gap(p, q) for p in A for q in B)

def dist_point(x, B):
    return min(gap((x, x), q) for q in B)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- criteria
def act_rule(D, gamma, r):
    """Proposition 13-A: KO~ reading D = hat c - kappa + e, |kappa| <= gamma, |e| <= r. Verdict 1 if and only if |D| > gamma + r."""
    return 1 if abs(D) > gamma + r else None

def ch3_act(D, eps):
    """4.6 (1) of Chapter 3: verdict 1 if |Delta_m| > eps (the rule under the exact protocol, for comparison)."""
    return 1 if abs(D) > eps else None

def ch3_R(y0, y1):
    """Local response of 4.6 (2) of Chapter 3, R_m(i) = [hat c(s~) != hat c(s)], applied with the readings taken as exact values (for comparison)."""
    return 1 if y0 != y1 else None

def rlc_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b, avail_ok=True):
    """Proposition 13-B: verdict 1 if dist(I(y0; r), I(y1; r)) > b, otherwise ⊥ (None). b = S + X + zeta_b is the H0 budget."""
    if not avail_ok:
        return None
    return 1 if dist_sets(consistent(y0, r, eps), consistent(y1, r, eps)) > b else None

def _g(c0, beta, L, smin, zeta_b):
    """Response upper bound under H0 (linear-law class): g(c0) = beta * min(L, |c0| / smin) + zeta_b; L = None means undeclared (infinite)."""
    if smin == 0:
        if L is None:
            return None  # unbounded
        return beta * L + zeta_b
    cal = abs(c0) / smin
    m = cal if L is None else min(L, cal)
    return beta * m + zeta_b

def rlc_star_margin(y0, y1, r, eps, beta, L, smin, zeta_b=ZERO):
    """Proposition 13-C (sound only on the declared “intercept-free” subclass; see 5.2 of the main text): min_{c0 in I0, c1 in I1} (|c1 - c0| - g(c0)). Verdict 1 if > 0. Piecewise linear; the minimum is attained at a candidate point."""
    I0 = consistent(y0, r, eps)
    I1 = consistent(y1, r, eps)
    if smin == 0 and L is None:
        return None
    cands = set()
    for (a, b) in I1:
        cands.update([a, b])
    for p in I1:
        for q in I1:
            if p[1] < q[0]:
                cands.add((p[1] + q[0]) / 2)
    cands.add(ZERO)
    if smin != 0 and L is not None:
        cands.update([L * smin, -L * smin])
    best = None
    for (a, b) in I0:
        pts = {a, b} | {x for x in cands if a <= x <= b}
        for x in pts:
            v = dist_point(x, I1) - _g(x, beta, L, smin, zeta_b)
            best = v if best is None or v < best else best
    return best

def rlc_star(y0, y1, r, eps, beta, L, smin, zeta_b=ZERO, avail_ok=True):
    if not avail_ok:
        return None
    m = rlc_star_margin(y0, y1, r, eps, beta, L, smin, zeta_b)
    return 1 if (m is not None and m > 0) else None

def legal_side_interval(sj, beta, hij):
    """Legitimate side-effect interval of position j within the frozen state box [0, hij]: [max(-beta, -sj), min(beta, hij - sj)]."""
    return (max(-beta, -sj), min(beta, hij - sj))

def rho_legal(s, Ni, beta, hi):
    """Legitimate side-effect radius rho_i(s) = max_{j in N_i} max(min(beta, hi_j - s_j), min(beta, s_j)); 0 when N_i is empty. hi is {position: upper bound}."""
    best = ZERO
    for j in Ni:
        lo_, up_ = legal_side_interval(F(s[j]), beta, F(hi[j]))
        best = max(best, up_, -lo_)
    return best

def b_conservative(L, beta, zeta_b):
    """Uniformly sound conservative budget (linear-law class): L·beta + zeta_b; holds for every state. Returns None (unbounded) when L is None (undeclared)."""
    return None if L is None else L * beta + zeta_b

def b_legal(L, rho, zeta_b):
    """Exact H0 budget on the valid domain (linear-law class, with intercept): L·rho + zeta_b, attainable and tight (13-D (2)).
    When rho = 0 it does not depend on L and returns zeta_b; when L is undeclared and rho > 0 it returns None (unbounded)."""
    if rho == 0:
        return zeta_b
    return None if L is None else L * rho + zeta_b

def rlc_legal(y0, y1, r, eps, L, rho, zeta_b, avail=True):
    """RLC with the valid-domain budget: verdict 1 if dist(I(y0), I(y1)) > b_legal; ⊥ when the budget is unbounded."""
    b = b_legal(L, rho, zeta_b)
    if b is None or not avail:
        return None
    return rlc_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b)

def twin_legal(c0, c1, s, Ni, beta, hi, L, zeta_b):
    """Construction of 13-D (2): given true members c0, c1 (|c1 - c0| <= b_legal), construct an H0 twin within the frozen state box.
    Returns (law, {j: eta_j}, omega) or None. The twin is m' = b' + k'·s(j*) with |k'| = L (or chosen as needed when L is undeclared); j* and the direction are taken where rho is attained."""
    D = c1 - c0
    omega = max(-zeta_b, min(zeta_b, D))
    Dp = D - omega
    if Dp == 0:
        return ('const', c0), {}, omega
    rho = rho_legal(s, Ni, beta, hi)
    if rho == 0:
        return None
    # choose j* and the direction
    jstar, side = None, None
    for j in Ni:
        lo_, up_ = legal_side_interval(F(s[j]), beta, F(hi[j]))
        if up_ == rho:
            jstar, side = j, 1
            break
        if -lo_ == rho:
            jstar, side = j, -1
            break
    if L is None:
        eta = side * rho
        kp = Dp / eta
    else:
        if abs(Dp) > L * rho:
            return None
        kp = L if (Dp > 0) == (side > 0) else -L
        if L == 0:
            return None
        eta = Dp / kp
    lo_, up_ = legal_side_interval(F(s[jstar]), beta, F(hi[jstar]))
    if not (lo_ <= eta <= up_):
        return None
    law = ('lin', kp, (jstar,), c0 - kp * F(s[jstar]))
    return law, {jstar: eta}, omega

def diff_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b):
    """Lemma 13-B1′: constant-margin rule |y1 - y0| > B0 + 2r, with B0 = eps + b (b > 0) or 0 (b = 0). Sound; dominated by RLC."""
    return 1 if abs(y1 - y0) > pop_null_bound(eps, b) + 2 * r else None

def avail_ok(i_in_T, s_read, r_s, eps, form_record):
    """Availability atom (D17): i ∈ T, suprathreshold (lower bound of the state reading s_read - r_s > eps), and a Form record earlier than t, kept as three separate items;
    the state reading checks only the suprathreshold condition and does not replace Form evidence. form_record is True / False / None (undetermined)."""
    if not i_in_T:
        return False
    if s_read - r_s <= eps:
        return None
    return form_record

def pop_null_bound(eps, b):
    """Proposition 13-E: under H0 (no individual has i as support), upper bound on the difference of a single individual's effective components: eps + b when b > 0, 0 when b = 0."""
    return (eps + b) if b > 0 else ZERO

def pop_supp_exists(ybar0, ybar1, r, eps, b):
    """Proposition 13-E (1): certificate of population “support existence” SuppEx (∃n: i ∈ Supp_act(m, s_n)). Certifies only functional support, not availability or HistCond."""
    return 1 if abs(ybar1 - ybar0) > pop_null_bound(eps, b) + 2 * r else None

pop_exists = pop_supp_exists   # alias (meaning: support existence)

def pop_fraction(ybar0, ybar1, r, eps, b, M1):
    """Proposition 13-E (2): lower bound on the support fraction. Returns ('bound', f), ('none', reason) or ('incompatible', explanation).
    Antecedent M1 > B0; a nontrivial lower bound f = (|Δ|_low - B0)/(M1 - B0) is given only when B0 < |Δ|_low <= M1; when |Δ|_low > max(M1, B0) the readings are incompatible with the declared model and no certificate is issued."""
    B0 = pop_null_bound(eps, b)
    d = abs(ybar1 - ybar0) - 2 * r
    if d > max(M1, B0):
        return ('incompatible', 'reading lower bound exceeds the largest mean difference the declared model can produce, max(M1, B0)')
    if M1 <= B0:
        return ('none', 'M1 <= B0: the difference bounds of supporting and non-supporting individuals cannot be separated; no nontrivial lower bound')
    if d <= B0:
        return ('none', '|Δ|_low <= B0')
    return ('bound', (d - B0) / (M1 - B0))

def pop_fraction_lower(ybar0, ybar1, r, eps, b, M1):
    """Numerical interface: returns the value of the lower bound (0 when there is no nontrivial lower bound or the readings are incompatible)."""
    kind, v = pop_fraction(ybar0, ybar1, r, eps, b, M1)
    return v if kind == 'bound' else ZERO

def avail_fraction_lower(mean_s, r_s, eps, hi):
    """Lower bound on the fraction of suprathreshold individuals: individual states lie in [0, hi]; the population mean reading is mean_s (radius r_s).
    mean <= a·hi + (1 - a)·eps ⇒ a >= (mean_low - eps)/(hi - eps). Returns the lower bound (possibly 0) or None (readings incompatible with the declaration)."""
    m = mean_s - r_s
    if mean_s + r_s < 0 or m > hi:
        return None
    if m <= eps:
        return ZERO
    return (m - eps) / (hi - eps)

def pop_hc_exists(f_low, a_low):
    """Proposition 13-E (5): if the support-fraction lower bound f_low and the availability-fraction lower bound a_low (available = i ∈ T, suprathreshold, Form record present) satisfy f_low + a_low > 1,
    then some individual is both supporting and available, i.e. P_HC^∃ = 1; otherwise ⊥."""
    if f_low is None or a_low is None:
        return None
    return 1 if f_low + a_low > 1 else None

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Hoeffding radius (rational upper bound)
def exp_lower(u, K=80):
    """Taylor partial sum of e^u (a lower bound when u >= 0)."""
    s, term = F(1), F(1)
    for k in range(1, K):
        term = term * u / k
        s += term
    return s

def ln_upper(x, den=1000):
    """Rational u with e^u >= x (hence u >= ln x); precision 1/den."""
    lo, hi = F(0), F(1)
    while exp_lower(hi) < x:
        hi *= 2
    while hi - lo > F(1, den):
        mid = (lo + hi) / 2
        if exp_lower(mid) >= x:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    return hi

def sqrt_upper(q, den=10 ** 6):
    """Rational t >= sqrt(q), with denominator den."""
    if q == 0:
        return F(0)
    lo, hi = F(0), max(F(1), q)
    while hi - lo > F(1, den):
        mid = (lo + hi) / 2
        if mid * mid >= q:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    t = F(-((-hi.numerator * den) // hi.denominator), den)
    assert t * t >= q
    return t

def hoeffding_radius(n, tau, a):
    """Mean of n i.i.d. readings supported on [mu - tau, mu + tau]: P(|mean - mu| >= t) <= 2 exp(-n t^2 / (2 tau^2)) <= a."""
    u = ln_upper(F(2) / a)
    return sqrt_upper(2 * tau * tau * u / n)

def mean_dist_two_point(n, tau):
    """Exact distribution of the mean error of n readings when the reading error e is -tau or +tau with probability 1/2 each: {error: probability}."""
    out = {}
    for b in range(n + 1):
        e = tau * F(2 * b - n, n)
        out[e] = out.get(e, ZERO) + F(comb(n, b), 2 ** n)
    return out
~~~

A.2  t13\_ext.py  Extension library: Fourier–Motzkin, nested radii, shared channels, bilinear budgets and twins, closure-conservative algorithms for I\_full and the successor in the box, same-beat gating certificates, model-mediated preimages (5.3, 5.5, 5.6, 5.8)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization: extension library (exact rational arithmetic, standard library only). Depends on t13_core.py.
Functions and the propositions of the main text they implement:
  General tools: fm_feasible (Fourier–Motzkin feasibility and witness)
  Statistics and composition: nested_radius (nested Hoeffding radius under within-cluster correlation, Proposition D-2), joint_channel_rule (joint compatible set for a shared measurement channel, Proposition D-3)
  Valid-domain boundary: b_bil_legal / twin_bil (exact supremum on the valid domain and twin for bilinear laws, Proposition D-5), ifull_feasible (closure-conservative algorithm for the reachability test of the I_full structural family, Proposition D-6; the exact algorithm is in t13_exact),
          sh_feasible (closure-conservative algorithm for the successor-in-box variant, Proposition D-7)
  Same-beat gating class: gate_input_cert / robust_crossing_cert (same-beat gating channel: input-reading certificate and robust threshold-crossing certificate, Propositions G-1–G-3)
  Comparative observation families: mediated_preimage (preimage compatible set for model-mediated readings, Proposition C-3)
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_core import hat, consistent, gap, dist_sets, legal_side_interval
ZERO = F(0)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Fourier–Motzkin
def fm_feasible(cons, variables):
    """cons: [(dict variable→coefficient, rhs)], meaning Σ a·x ≤ rhs (closed constraints). Returns (feasible?, witness dict or None)."""
    stages = []
    cur = [({k: F(x) for k, x in a.items() if x != 0}, F(r)) for a, r in cons]
    for v in variables:
        pos = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) > 0]
        neg = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) < 0]
        zer = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) == 0]
        stages.append((v, pos, neg))
        new = list(zer)
        for ap, rp in pos:
            for an, rn in neg:
                cp, cn = ap[v], -an[v]
                a = {}
                for k in set(ap) | set(an):
                    if k == v:
                        continue
                    val = ap.get(k, 0) / cp + an.get(k, 0) / cn
                    if val != 0:
                        a[k] = val
                new.append((a, rp / cp + rn / cn))
        cur = new
    for a, r in cur:
        if not a and r < 0:
            return False, None
        if a:
            raise ValueError('uneliminated variable')
    wit = {}
    for v, pos, neg in reversed(stages):
        ub, lb = None, None
        for a, r in pos:
            val = (r - sum(c * wit[k] for k, c in a.items() if k != v)) / a[v]
            ub = val if ub is None or val < ub else ub
        for a, r in neg:
            val = (r - sum(c * wit[k] for k, c in a.items() if k != v)) / a[v]
            lb = val if lb is None or val > lb else lb
        if lb is None and ub is None:
            wit[v] = ZERO
        elif lb is None:
            wit[v] = ub
        elif ub is None:
            wit[v] = lb
        else:
            wit[v] = (lb + ub) / 2
    return True, wit

def fm_bounds(cons, elim, target):
    """After eliminating each variable in elim, returns (lower bound, upper bound, constant constraints feasible?) for target."""
    cur = [({k: F(x) for k, x in a.items() if x != 0}, F(r)) for a, r in cons]
    for v in elim:
        pos = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) > 0]
        neg = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) < 0]
        new = [(a, r) for a, r in cur if a.get(v, 0) == 0]
        for ap, rp in pos:
            for an, rn in neg:
                cp, cn = ap[v], -an[v]
                a = {k: ap.get(k, 0) / cp + an.get(k, 0) / cn for k in set(ap) | set(an) if k != v}
                a = {k: x for k, x in a.items() if x != 0}
                new.append((a, rp / cp + rn / cn))
        cur = new
    lb = ub = None
    for a, r in cur:
        c = a.get(target, 0)
        if c == 0:
            if r < 0:
                return None, None, False
        elif c > 0:
            ub = r / c if ub is None or r / c < ub else ub
        else:
            lb = r / c if lb is None or r / c > lb else lb
    if lb is not None and ub is not None and lb > ub:
        return lb, ub, False
    return lb, ub, True

def pieces_with_slope(y, r, eps):
    """Pieces of the compatible set I(y; r, eps) with the slope of hat on each (effective pieces 1, zero piece 0)."""
    out = []
    lo, hi = y - r, y + r
    if lo < -eps:
        out.append(((lo, min(hi, -eps)), 1))
    if hi > eps:
        out.append(((max(lo, eps), hi), 1))
    if lo <= 0 <= hi:
        out.append(((-eps, eps), 0))
    return [(p, a) for p, a in out if p[0] <= p[1]]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- statistics and composition
def sqrt_up(q, den=10 ** 6):
    from t13_core import sqrt_upper
    return sqrt_upper(q, den)

def nested_radius(G, n, tau_b, tau_e, a):
    """Proposition D-2: G clusters (cluster deviations i.i.d., mean 0, support ±tau_b), n units in all (unit errors i.i.d., mean 0, support ±tau_e);
    overall mean = mean of the cluster deviations (weighted by cluster) + mean of the unit errors. Hoeffding with a/2 for each: t1 (G clusters), t2 (n units). Returns t1 + t2."""
    from t13_core import ln_upper
    u = ln_upper(F(4) / a)
    t1 = sqrt_up(2 * tau_b * tau_b * u / G)
    t2 = sqrt_up(2 * tau_e * tau_e * u / n)
    return t1 + t2

def joint_channel_rule(y0, y1, r, tau_ch, eps, b):
    """Proposition D-3: the baseline and post-setting readings share a channel offset o (|o| ≤ tau_ch, same sign and value in both readings); the remaining error radius is r.
    Verdict 1 if and only if min_{|o|≤tau_ch} dist(I(y0 - o; r), I(y1 - o; r)) > b. dist is piecewise linear in o; the minimum is attained at a candidate point."""
    cands = {tau_ch, -tau_ch}
    for y in (y0, y1):
        for sg in (r, -r):
            for v in (-eps, ZERO, eps):
                o = y + sg - v
                if -tau_ch <= o <= tau_ch:
                    cands.add(o)
    pts = sorted(cands)
    mids = [(pts[k] + pts[k + 1]) / 2 for k in range(len(pts) - 1)]
    vals = []
    for o in pts + mids:
        A, B = consistent(y0 - o, r, eps), consistent(y1 - o, r, eps)
        if A and B:                       # an empty compatible set means this o is incompatible with the readings; skip it
            vals.append(dist_sets(A, B))
    if not vals:
        return None, None                 # readings incompatible with the channel declaration: no certificate is issued
    best = min(vals)
    return (1 if best > b else None), best

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- valid domain of bilinear laws
def bil_corner_max(s1, s2, E1, E2):
    """max |(s1+e1)(s2+e2) - s1 s2| over corners of E1 × E2 (a bilinear function attains its extrema at corners)."""
    return max(abs((s1 + e1) * (s2 + e2) - s1 * s2) for e1 in E1 for e2 in E2)

def b_bil_legal(s, i, Ni, beta, hi, K, positions, dprime_max):
    """Proposition D-5: exact supremum of |c(s~) - c(s)| for an H0 bilinear law with intercept (i ∉ Supp_act), |k| ≤ K, on the frozen valid domain with known baseline.
    positions: all positions; dprime_max: largest admissible post-setting value s(i) + delta'. Returns (supremum, the read pair and corner attaining it)."""
    E = {}
    for p in positions:
        if p in Ni:
            lo, up = legal_side_interval(s[p], beta, hi[p])
            E[p] = (lo, up)
        else:
            E[p] = (ZERO, ZERO)
    best, arg = ZERO, None
    others = [p for p in positions if p != i]
    for a_ in range(len(others)):
        for b_ in range(a_, len(others)):
            j1, j2 = others[a_], others[b_]
            if j1 == j2:
                lo, up = E[j1]
                v = max(abs((s[j1] + e) ** 2 - s[j1] ** 2) for e in (lo, up))
            else:
                v = bil_corner_max(s[j1], s[j2], E[j1], E[j2])
            if K * v > best:
                best, arg = K * v, ('pair', j1, j2)
    # cross case: i with j2, s(j2) = 0 (i ∉ Supp), response k·(s_i + delta')·eta_2, eta_2 ∈ [0, up]
    for j in others:
        if s[j] == 0:
            v = dprime_max * max(abs(E[j][0]), abs(E[j][1]))
            if K * v > best:
                best, arg = K * v, ('cross', i, j)
    return best, arg

def twin_bil(c0, c1, s, i, Ni, beta, hi, K, positions, dprime_max):
    """Build a legitimate H0 bilinear twin from the maximizer of b_bil_legal: law b' + k s(j1) s(j2) (or cross k s(i) s(j)); returns (law, eta dict, post-setting value of s(i) or None)."""
    D = c1 - c0
    if D == 0:
        return ('const', c0), {}, None
    bmax, arg = b_bil_legal(s, i, Ni, beta, hi, K, positions, dprime_max)
    if abs(D) > bmax or arg is None:
        return None
    kind, j1, j2 = arg
    E = {p: (legal_side_interval(s[p], beta, hi[p]) if p in Ni else (ZERO, ZERO)) for p in positions}
    if kind == 'pair':
        best = None
        corners = [(e1, e2) for e1 in E[j1] for e2 in E[j2]] if j1 != j2 else [(e, e) for e in E[j1]]
        for e1, e2 in corners:
            g = ((s[j1] + e1) * (s[j2] + e2) - s[j1] * s[j2]) if j1 != j2 else ((s[j1] + e1) ** 2 - s[j1] ** 2)
            if g != 0 and (best is None or abs(g) > abs(best[0])):
                best = (g, e1, e2)
        if D == 0:
            return ('const', c0), {}, None
        g, e1, e2 = best
        k = D / g
        law = ('bil', k, (j1, j2), c0 - k * s[j1] * s[j2])
        eta = {j1: e1} if j1 == j2 else {j1: e1, j2: e2}
        return law, eta, None
    # cross
    e = E[j2][1] if abs(E[j2][1]) >= abs(E[j2][0]) else E[j2][0]
    if D == 0:
        return ('const', c0), {}, None
    k = D / (dprime_max * e)
    law = ('bil', k, (i, j2), c0)     # s(j2) = 0 ⇒ baseline component = intercept
    return law, {j2: e}, dprime_max

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- I_full structural family (closure-conservative)
def ifull_feasible(y0, y1, r, eps, L, s_y, Ey, R, yR1, rR, S_base, s_g, need_succ=True):
    """Proposition D-6: H0 reachability test for the I_full structural family.
    The H0 law of m reads only N_i = {y} (linear with intercept, |k| ≤ L) and acts on position g; another process R (known law b_R + k_R s(y), also acting on g) has post-setting reading yR1 (radius rR).
    S_base: the part of the one-step successor of g at baseline other than m (s_g + hat c_R(baseline), known); after the setting it is likewise s_g + hat c_R(s_y + eta).
    Variables (c0, c1, eta). Returns (twin exists?, witness). The case L = None (undeclared) is handled separately."""
    bR, kR = R
    for P0, a0 in pieces_with_slope(y0, r, eps):
        for Pc1, a1 in pieces_with_slope(y1, r, eps):
            for PR, aR in pieces_with_slope(yR1, rR, eps):
                for sgn in (1, -1):
                    cons = []
                    cons += [({'c0': F(1)}, P0[1]), ({'c0': F(-1)}, -P0[0])]
                    cons += [({'c1': F(1)}, Pc1[1]), ({'c1': F(-1)}, -Pc1[0])]
                    cons += [({'e': F(1)}, Ey[1]), ({'e': F(-1)}, -Ey[0])]
                    # post-setting reading of R: c_R = bR + kR (s_y + e) ∈ PR
                    cons += [({'e': kR}, PR[1] - bR - kR * s_y), ({'e': -kR}, -(PR[0] - bR - kR * s_y))]
                    # sign branch of eta
                    cons += [({'e': F(-sgn)}, ZERO)]
                    if L is not None:
                        # |c1 - c0| ≤ L·sgn·e
                        cons += [({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1), 'e': -L * sgn}, ZERO),
                                 ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1), 'e': -L * sgn}, ZERO)]
                    if need_succ:
                        # baseline successor: S_base + a0·c0 ≥ 0; post-setting successor: s_g + aR·c_R(e) + a1·c1 ≥ 0
                        cons += [({'c0': F(-a0)}, S_base)]
                        cons += [({'c1': F(-a1), 'e': -aR * kR}, s_g + aR * (bR + kR * s_y))]
                    if L is not None:
                        ok, w = fm_feasible(cons, ['c1', 'c0', 'e'])
                        if ok:
                            return True, w
                        continue
                    # L undeclared: the twin needs eta ≠ 0 (arbitrary coefficient) or c1 = c0 (constant law)
                    ok, w = fm_feasible(cons + [({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1)}, ZERO), ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1)}, ZERO)],
                                        ['c1', 'c0', 'e'])
                    if ok:
                        return True, w
                    lb, ub, okc = fm_bounds(cons, ['c1', 'c0'], 'e')
                    if okc:
                        top = ub if sgn > 0 else (-lb if lb is not None else None)
                        if top is None or top > 0:
                            e_val = (ub if sgn > 0 else lb)
                            if e_val is None:
                                e_val = F(sgn)
                            ok, w = fm_feasible(cons + [({'e': F(1)}, e_val), ({'e': F(-1)}, -e_val)], ['c1', 'c0', 'e'])
                            if ok:
                                return True, w
    return False, None

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- successor-in-box variant (I_pair, closure-conservative)
def sh_feasible(y0, y1, r, eps, b, dlo, dhi, H):
    """Proposition D-7: under I_pair the successor is also box-constrained (upper bound H at position tgt). The twin adds a constant background K at tgt: both successors S0 + hat c0 + K and S0 + d + hat c1 + K lie in [0, H],
    where d ∈ [dlo, dhi] is the state change the setting makes at tgt (d = 0 when tgt ∉ {i} ∪ N_i). Feasible if and only if there exist c0 ∈ I0, c1 ∈ I1 and d with
    |c1 - c0| ≤ b and |d + hat c1 - hat c0| ≤ H. Variables (c0, c1, d)."""
    for P0, a0 in pieces_with_slope(y0, r, eps):
        for Pc1, a1 in pieces_with_slope(y1, r, eps):
            cons = [({'c0': F(1)}, P0[1]), ({'c0': F(-1)}, -P0[0]), ({'c1': F(1)}, Pc1[1]), ({'c1': F(-1)}, -Pc1[0]),
                    ({'d': F(1)}, dhi), ({'d': F(-1)}, -dlo),
                    ({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1)}, b), ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1)}, b),
                    ({'d': F(1), 'c1': F(a1), 'c0': F(-a0)}, H), ({'d': F(-1), 'c1': F(-a1), 'c0': F(a0)}, H)]
            ok, w = fm_feasible(cons, ['c1', 'c0', 'd'])
            if ok:
                return True, w
    return False, None

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 𝒦_g same-beat gating channel
def gate_input_cert(v0, v1, rv, Xi):
    """Proposition G-1: reading of the gating input vt (radius rv). Under H0 (same beat: T not in gr_j), |Δvt| ≤ Xi. |v1 - v0| > Xi + 2rv gives the verdict “T ∈ gr_j and the setting changes vt”."""
    return 1 if abs(v1 - v0) > Xi + 2 * rv else None

def robust_crossing_cert(v0, v1, rv, Xi, theta):
    """Proposition G-2: robust threshold crossing. Baseline vt0 + rv < theta and post-setting vt1 - rv - Xi ≥ theta (or the reverse) ⇒ the setting of T alone (without side effects) changes the gating ⇒ FD_𝒟₁(e) = 1."""
    if v0 + rv < theta and v1 - rv - Xi >= theta:
        return 1
    if v0 - rv >= theta and v1 + rv + Xi < theta:
        return 1
    return None

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- model-mediated readings
def mediated_preimage(z, tau, zeta_phi, phi_pieces):
    """Proposition C-3: reading z = phi(theta) + e, |e| ≤ tau, with the declared model differing from the true map by at most zeta_phi. phi is piecewise linear and nondecreasing (list [(x0, x1, y0, y1)]).
    Returns the compatible set of theta, phi^{-1}([z - tau - zeta_phi, z + tau + zeta_phi]) (a list of closed intervals)."""
    lo, hi = z - tau - zeta_phi, z + tau + zeta_phi
    out = []
    for x0, x1, y0, y1 in phi_pieces:
        a, b = max(lo, min(y0, y1)), min(hi, max(y0, y1))
        if a > b:
            continue
        if y1 == y0:
            out.append((x0, x1))
        else:
            ta, tb = x0 + (a - y0) * (x1 - x0) / (y1 - y0), x0 + (b - y0) * (x1 - x0) / (y1 - y0)
            out.append((min(ta, tb), max(ta, tb)))
    return out
~~~

A.3  t13\_exact.py  True compatible sets, Fourier–Motzkin with strict inequalities, exact solution and back-substitution for the I\_full structural family and the successor in the box, bilinear budgets on the available domain of the tested position (5.3)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization: true compatible sets and exact solution (exact rational arithmetic, standard library only).
ifull_feasible and sh_feasible in t13_ext.py use the closure of the compatible set and serve only as “closure-conservative algorithms” (used only for conservative verdicts of 1; they do not certify exactness).
This file contains:
  true_pieces        true compatible set J(y; r, eps) = {c : hat(c) ∈ [y - r, y + r]}; active pieces have open endpoints at ±eps, the zero piece is closed
  fm_strict          Fourier–Motzkin feasibility test with strict inequalities, with a witness that can be back-substituted
  ifull_exact        exact H0 reachability test for the I_full structural family (other processes affine), returning a true witness
  verify_ifull       verifies a witness by back-substituting all observation and successor constraints with the true hat
  sh_exact / verify_sh  exact test and back-substitution for the successor-in-box variant (subclass tgt ∉ N_i)
  b_bil_avail        bilinear budget of D-5, given only when the tested position is available (s_i > eps ≥ 0)
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_core import hat, legal_side_interval
from t13_ext import b_bil_legal
ZERO = F(0)

def true_pieces(y, r, eps):
    """Pieces of the true compatible set: (lo, hi, lo_strict, hi_strict, slope). Active pieces have slope = 1 (hat(c) = c), the zero piece slope = 0."""
    out = []
    lo, hi = y - r, y + r
    if lo < -eps:
        up = min(hi, -eps)
        out.append((lo, up, False, hi >= -eps, 1))
    if hi > eps:
        dn = max(lo, eps)
        out.append((dn, hi, lo <= eps, False, 1))
    if lo <= 0 <= hi:
        out.append((-eps, eps, False, False, 0))
    return out

def in_true(c, y, r, eps):
    return y - r <= hat(c, eps) <= y + r

def fm_strict(cons, variables):
    """cons: [(dict variable→coefficient, rhs, strict)], meaning Σ a·x < rhs (strict) or ≤ rhs. Returns (feasible?, witness).
    Fourier–Motzkin elimination with strict inequalities is exact for projection; back-substitution takes midpoints of open intervals, so the witness satisfies every strict constraint."""
    cur = [({k: F(x) for k, x in a.items() if x != 0}, F(r), bool(st)) for a, r, st in cons]
    stages = []
    for v in variables:
        pos = [c for c in cur if c[0].get(v, 0) > 0]
        neg = [c for c in cur if c[0].get(v, 0) < 0]
        new = [c for c in cur if c[0].get(v, 0) == 0]
        stages.append((v, pos, neg))
        for ap, rp, sp in pos:
            for an, rn, sn in neg:
                cp, cn = ap[v], -an[v]
                a = {k: ap.get(k, 0) / cp + an.get(k, 0) / cn for k in set(ap) | set(an) if k != v}
                a = {k: x for k, x in a.items() if x != 0}
                new.append((a, rp / cp + rn / cn, sp or sn))
        cur = new
    for a, r, st in cur:
        if a:
            raise ValueError('uneliminated variable')
        if (st and not (r > 0)) or (not st and r < 0):
            return False, None
    wit = {}
    for v, pos, neg in reversed(stages):
        ups = [((r - sum(c * wit[k] for k, c in a.items() if k != v)) / a[v], st) for a, r, st in pos]
        dns = [((r - sum(c * wit[k] for k, c in a.items() if k != v)) / a[v], st) for a, r, st in neg]
        ub = min(x for x, _ in ups) if ups else None
        lb = max(x for x, _ in dns) if dns else None
        ubs = any(st for x, st in ups if x == ub)
        lbs = any(st for x, st in dns if x == lb)
        if lb is None and ub is None:
            x = ZERO
        elif lb is None:
            x = ub - 1 if ubs else ub
        elif ub is None:
            x = lb + 1 if lbs else lb
        elif lb < ub:
            x = (lb + ub) / 2
        else:
            if lbs or ubs:
                raise AssertionError('back-substitution failed: projection feasible but endpoint strict')
            x = lb
        wit[v] = x
    return True, wit

def _piece_cons(var, p):
    lo, hi, ls, hs, _a = p
    return [({var: F(1)}, hi, hs), ({var: F(-1)}, -lo, ls)]

def ifull_exact(y0, y1, r, eps, L, s_y, Ey, R, yR1, rR, S_base, s_g):
    """Structural family of Proposition D-6: the H0 law of m reads only N_i = {y} (linear with intercept, |k| ≤ L; L = None means undeclared);
    the other process R (affine law b_R + k_R s(y)) acts on g together with m; R has post-setting reading yR1 (radius rR); successors must be nonnegative.
    Variables (c0, c1, e). Uses true compatible sets and strict inequalities; returns (twin exists?, witness)."""
    bR, kR = R
    for Pc0 in true_pieces(y0, r, eps):
        for Pc1 in true_pieces(y1, r, eps):
            for Pcr in true_pieces(yR1, rR, eps):
                a0, a1, aR = Pc0[4], Pc1[4], Pcr[4]
                base = _piece_cons('c0', Pc0) + _piece_cons('c1', Pc1)
                base += [({'e': F(1)}, Ey[1], False), ({'e': F(-1)}, -Ey[0], False)]
                lo, hi, ls, hs, _ = Pcr       # c_R(e) = bR + kR (s_y + e) lies in this piece
                base += [({'e': kR}, hi - bR - kR * s_y, hs), ({'e': -kR}, -(lo - bR - kR * s_y), ls)]
                base += [({'c0': F(-a0)}, S_base, False)]
                base += [({'c1': F(-a1), 'e': -aR * kR}, s_g + aR * (bR + kR * s_y), False)]
                branches = []
                if L is not None:
                    for sg in (1, -1):
                        branches.append(base + [({'e': F(-sg)}, ZERO, False),
                                                ({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1), 'e': -L * sg}, ZERO, False),
                                                ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1), 'e': -L * sg}, ZERO, False)])
                else:
                    branches.append(base + [({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1)}, ZERO, False), ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1)}, ZERO, False)])
                    for sg in (1, -1):
                        branches.append(base + [({'e': F(-sg)}, ZERO, True)])     # sg·e > 0, arbitrary coefficient
                for cons in branches:
                    ok, w = fm_strict(cons, ['c1', 'c0', 'e'])
                    if ok:
                        return True, w
    return False, None

def verify_ifull(w, y0, y1, r, eps, L, s_y, Ey, R, yR1, rR, S_base, s_g):
    """Build the twin m' = b' + k' s(y) from the witness and back-substitute all observation and successor constraints with the true hat."""
    c0, c1, e = w['c0'], w['c1'], w['e']
    if e != 0:
        kp = (c1 - c0) / e
    else:
        if c1 != c0:
            return False
        kp = ZERO
    if L is not None and abs(kp) > L:
        return False
    if not (Ey[0] <= e <= Ey[1]):
        return False
    t0 = c0                     # m'(s_y) = b' + k' s_y = c0
    t1 = c0 + kp * e            # = c1
    cR1 = hat(R[0] + R[1] * (s_y + e), eps)
    return (in_true(t0, y0, r, eps) and in_true(t1, y1, r, eps) and yR1 - rR <= cR1 <= yR1 + rR
            and S_base + hat(t0, eps) >= 0 and s_g + cR1 + hat(t1, eps) >= 0)

def sh_exact(y0, y1, r, eps, b, dlo, dhi, H):
    """Proposition D-7 (subclass tgt ∉ N_i): there exist c0 ∈ J0, c1 ∈ J1 and d ∈ [dlo, dhi] with |c1 - c0| ≤ b and |d + hat c1 - hat c0| ≤ H."""
    for Pc0 in true_pieces(y0, r, eps):
        for Pc1 in true_pieces(y1, r, eps):
            a0, a1 = Pc0[4], Pc1[4]
            cons = _piece_cons('c0', Pc0) + _piece_cons('c1', Pc1)
            cons += [({'d': F(1)}, dhi, False), ({'d': F(-1)}, -dlo, False),
                     ({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1)}, b, False), ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1)}, b, False),
                     ({'d': F(1), 'c1': F(a1), 'c0': F(-a0)}, H, False), ({'d': F(-1), 'c1': F(-a1), 'c0': F(a0)}, H, False)]
            ok, w = fm_strict(cons, ['c1', 'c0', 'd'])
            if ok:
                return True, w
    return False, None

def verify_sh(w, y0, y1, r, eps, b, dlo, dhi, H):
    c0, c1, d = w['c0'], w['c1'], w['d']
    return (in_true(c0, y0, r, eps) and in_true(c1, y1, r, eps) and abs(c1 - c0) <= b and dlo <= d <= dhi
            and abs(d + hat(c1, eps) - hat(c0, eps)) <= H)

def b_bil_avail(s, i, Ni, beta, hi, K, positions, dprime_max, eps):
    """Proposition D-5: gives the bilinear budget only when the tested position is available (s_i > eps ≥ 0); otherwise returns None (outside the domain of the theorem).
    In this domain an H0 law with read pair (i, i) can only have k = 0 (D15: i ∈ Supp when s_i ≠ 0), so the cases are exhaustive."""
    if not (s[i] > eps >= 0):
        return None
    return b_bil_legal(s, i, Ni, beta, hi, K, positions, dprime_max)[0]
~~~

A.4  t13\_gate.py  Multi-beat gating library: runs and events of 𝒦\_g, the third factor, 𝒦\_PA^δ and 𝒦\_PA^≺, the R1′ check, enumeration of simultaneous equations, propagation upper bounds, component-difference bounds, M-1, the fixed-reading threshold, M-2 twins (3.6, 5.7, 8.1)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization: multi-beat gating channels. Exact rational arithmetic, standard library only.
Objects (main text 3.6, 5.7):
  mode = 'g'      𝒦_g of Definitions 1–2 of The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts v1.0 (gate_of[j] = j), and its third-factor variant (gate_of[j] ≠ j, 𝒦_PA^3);
  mode = 'delay'  𝒦_PA^δ: the firing of gating units enters the integration of unit j with weight U[k][j], delayed by one beat (the state gains an auxiliary position h = firing vector of the previous beat);
  mode = 'dag'    𝒦_PA^≺: same-beat firing input; the support graph of U must be a directed acyclic graph, and units are integrated and gated one by one in the declared topological order order.
  Same-beat cyclic firing input (simultaneous equations) lies outside the working domain of this paper: simultaneous_solutions serves only to show that its solutions can be non-unique (main text 3.6, Example 3.6-1).
Functions:
  validate          class-membership check (parameter domain, DAG order)
  step / run        one-beat and multi-beat runs (return, beat by beat, the pre-beat state, gating, gating input and all events (occurrence, component))
  r1_check          Lemma R1′ (closure of K_t: pre-beat value + sum of the components directed to the node = post-beat value)
  simultaneous_solutions  all solutions (by enumeration) of the same-beat system g_j = [base_j + Σ_k U[k][j] g_k ≥ θ_j]
  prop_bounds       propagation recursion of Proposition M-1 (weight-difference coefficient 1 − ρ·[plastic]; delay includes the h term)
  event_bound       upper bound B_e on the component difference of each event kind at beat t_e (all kinds, RESET included)
  m1_certificate    multi-beat robust certificate M-1 (the occurrence rule is used only for kinds whose occurrence is determined by gating, letters and switches; CLIP, and FIN when affected by side effects on h, use the component rule)
  beta_star         largest scaling factor admitted by the margin condition under a unit side-effect profile (intervention bound)
  m2_twin           Proposition M-2: H0 twin construction with a globally fixed pat_B
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product
ZERO = F(0)
KINDS_W = ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP', 'SYN')

def clip(a, lo, hi):
    return min(hi, max(lo, a))

def _dx(P, i):
    d = P['dx']
    return d[i] if isinstance(d, list) else d

def _dy(P, j):
    d = P['dy']
    return d[j] if isinstance(d, list) else d

def validate(P, nin, nout):
    """Class-membership check. Returns (True, '') or (False, reason)."""
    for j in range(nout):
        if not (0 <= P['lam'][j] <= 1):
            return False, 'lam'
        if not (0 <= _dy(P, j) <= 1):
            return False, 'dy'
    for i in range(nin):
        if not (0 <= _dx(P, i) <= 1):
            return False, 'dx'
    if not (0 <= P['rho'] <= 1) or P['Ap'] < 0 or P['Am'] < 0:
        return False, 'rho/A'
    mode = P.get('mode', 'g')
    U = P.get('U')
    if mode == 'g':
        if U is not None and any(U[k][j] != 0 for k in range(nout) for j in range(nout)):
            return False, 'U in K_g'
    elif mode == 'dag':
        pos = {j: n for n, j in enumerate(P['order'])}
        if sorted(P['order']) != list(range(nout)):
            return False, 'order'
        for k in range(nout):
            for j in range(nout):
                if U[k][j] != 0 and not pos[k] < pos[j]:
                    return False, 'not DAG under order'
    elif mode != 'delay':
        return False, 'mode'
    return True, ''

def _base(P, st, s, I):
    w, v, x, y, h = st
    nin, nout = len(x), len(v)
    return [P['lam'][j] * v[j] + sum((w[i][j] for i in range(nin) if s[i]), ZERO) + I[j] for j in range(nout)]

def step(P, st, s, I):
    """st = (w, v, x, y, h). Returns (new state, g, vt, ev); ev[event] = (occurrence, component), with the component recorded as 0 when the event does not occur."""
    w, v, x, y, h = st
    nin, nout = len(x), len(v)
    mode = P.get('mode', 'g')
    U = P.get('U') or [[ZERO] * nout for _ in range(nout)]
    base = _base(P, st, s, I)
    if mode == 'g':
        vt = base[:]
        g = [vt[j] >= P['theta'][j] for j in range(nout)]
    elif mode == 'delay':
        vt = [base[j] + sum((U[k][j] for k in range(nout) if h[k]), ZERO) for j in range(nout)]
        g = [vt[j] >= P['theta'][j] for j in range(nout)]
    else:  # dag: in the declared topological order
        vt = [None] * nout
        g = [None] * nout
        for j in P['order']:
            vt[j] = base[j] + sum((U[k][j] for k in range(nout) if U[k][j] != 0 and g[k]), ZERO)
            g[j] = vt[j] >= P['theta'][j]
    v2 = [P['vres'][j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(nout)]
    x2 = [_dx(P, i) * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(nin)]
    y2 = [_dy(P, j) * y[j] + (1 if g[j] else 0) for j in range(nout)]
    h2 = [1 if g[j] else 0 for j in range(nout)]
    w2 = [[None] * nout for _ in range(nin)]
    ev = {}
    pl, bp = P['plastic'], P['bap']
    for i in range(nin):
        for j in range(nout):
            G = g[P['gate_of'][j]]
            occp = bool(pl and bp and G)
            occm = bool(pl and bp and s[i])
            pp = P['Ap'] * x2[i] if occp else ZERO
            pm = -P['Am'] * y2[P['gate_of'][j]] if occm else ZERO
            occr = bool(pl and P['rho'] != 0)
            pr = -P['rho'] * (w[i][j] - P['b'][i][j]) if pl else ZERO
            S = w[i][j] + pp + pm + pr
            w2[i][j] = clip(S, P['lo'], P['hi'])
            cc = w2[i][j] - S
            ev[('PLUS', i, j)] = (occp, pp)
            ev[('MINUS', i, j)] = (occm, pm)
            ev[('RELAX', i, j)] = (occr, pr)
            ev[('CLIP', i, j)] = (cc != 0, cc)
            ev[('SYN', i, j)] = (bool(s[i]), w[i][j] if s[i] else ZERO)
    for i in range(nin):
        ev[('XIN', i)] = (bool(s[i]), F(1) if s[i] else ZERO)
        ev[('XDEC', i)] = (True, (_dx(P, i) - 1) * x[i])
    for j in range(nout):
        ev[('LEAK', j)] = (True, (P['lam'][j] - 1) * v[j])
        ev[('INJ', j)] = (I[j] != 0, I[j])
        ev[('RESET', j)] = (g[j], P['vres'][j] - vt[j] if g[j] else ZERO)
        ev[('YIN', j)] = (g[j], F(1) if g[j] else ZERO)
        ev[('YDEC', j)] = (True, (_dy(P, j) - 1) * y[j])
        for k in range(nout):
            if U[k][j] != 0 and mode != 'g':
                on = bool(h[k]) if mode == 'delay' else bool(g[k])
                ev[('FIN', k, j)] = (on, U[k][j] if on else ZERO)
        if mode == 'delay':
            ev[('HCOPY', j)] = (True, F(h2[j] - h[j]))
    return (w2, v2, x2, y2, h2), g, vt, ev

def copy_state(st):
    w, v, x, y, h = st
    return ([row[:] for row in w], v[:], x[:], y[:], h[:])

def run(P, st0, seq, u=None, setting=None, side=None):
    """seq: [(s, I)]. Before beat u starts, apply setting ({(i, j): value}) and side ({('w',i,j)|('v',j)|('x',i)|('y',j): increment; ('h',k): flip}) to the state.
    Returns per-beat records dict(pre=pre-beat state, g, vt, ev)."""
    st = copy_state(st0)
    out = []
    for t, (s, I) in enumerate(seq):
        if u is not None and t == u:
            w, v, x, y, h = copy_state(st)
            for (i, j), val in (setting or {}).items():
                w[i][j] = val
            for key, d in (side or {}).items():
                if key[0] == 'w':
                    w[key[1]][key[2]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'v':
                    v[key[1]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'x':
                    x[key[1]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'y':
                    y[key[1]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'h':
                    if d:
                        h[key[1]] = 1 - h[key[1]]
            st = (w, v, x, y, h)
        pre = copy_state(st)
        st, g, vt, ev = step(P, st, s, I)
        out.append({'pre': pre, 'g': g, 'vt': vt, 'ev': ev, 'post': copy_state(st)})
    return out

def r1_check(P, rec):
    """Lemma R1′: for a one-beat record, check node by node that pre-beat value + Σ components = post-beat value. Returns the number of mismatches."""
    w, v, x, y, h = rec['pre']
    w2, v2, x2, y2, h2 = rec['post']
    ev = rec['ev']
    nin, nout = len(x), len(v)
    bad = 0
    for i in range(nin):
        for j in range(nout):
            tot = sum(ev[(k, i, j)][1] for k in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP'))
            bad += (w[i][j] + tot != w2[i][j])
        bad += (x[i] + ev[('XDEC', i)][1] + ev[('XIN', i)][1] != x2[i])
    for j in range(nout):
        tot = ev[('LEAK', j)][1] + ev[('INJ', j)][1] + ev[('RESET', j)][1]
        tot += sum(ev[('SYN', i, j)][1] for i in range(nin))
        tot += sum(c for (key, (o, c)) in ev.items() if key[0] == 'FIN' and key[2] == j)
        bad += (v[j] + tot != v2[j])
        bad += (y[j] + ev[('YDEC', j)][1] + ev[('YIN', j)][1] != y2[j])
        if ('HCOPY', j) in ev:
            bad += (h[j] + ev[('HCOPY', j)][1] != h2[j])
    return bad

def simultaneous_solutions(P, st, s, I):
    """All solutions (2^n enumeration) of the same-beat simultaneous system g_j = [base_j + Σ_k U[k][j]·g_k ≥ θ_j]."""
    nout = len(st[1])
    U = P['U']
    base = _base(P, st, s, I)
    sols = []
    for g in product((0, 1), repeat=nout):
        if all((base[j] + sum((U[k][j] for k in range(nout) if g[k]), ZERO) >= P['theta'][j]) == bool(g[j]) for j in range(nout)):
            sols.append(g)
    return sols

def prop_bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0):
    """Propagation recursion of Proposition M-1. D0 = (Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh): node-wise upper bounds on the side effects at the start of beat u (Dh takes 0/1 and is used only for delay).
    Recurses beat by beat along the gating pattern g_obs read in the setting experiment, using only declared parameters:
      Dvt_j(t) = λ_j Dv_j + Σ_{i: s_i=1} Dw_ij + [delay] Σ_k |U_kj| Dh_k
      Dv_j(t+1) = 0 (g_j = 1) or Dvt_j(t); Dx_i(t+1) = d_x,i Dx_i; Dy_j(t+1) = d_y,j Dy_j; Dh(t+1) = 0
      Dw_ij(t+1) = (1 − ρ·[plastic]) Dw_ij + A₊ d_x,i Dx_i·[plastic ∧ bap ∧ g_{gate(j)}] + A₋ d_y,gate(j) Dy_gate(j)·[plastic ∧ bap ∧ s_i]
    Returns (Dvt[t], Dstate[t]), where Dstate[t] bounds the pre-beat state of beat t, t = u..te+1 (Dstate[te+1].Dw is the difference bound on the pre-clip sum S of beat te)."""
    Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh = [row[:] for row in D0[0]], D0[1][:], D0[2][:], D0[3][:], D0[4][:]
    nin, nout = len(Dx), len(Dv)
    mode = P.get('mode', 'g')
    U = P.get('U') or [[ZERO] * nout for _ in range(nout)]
    Dvt_all, Dst = {}, {}
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        Dst[t] = (Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh)
        s, I = seq[t]
        g = g_obs[t]
        Dvt = [P['lam'][j] * Dv[j] + sum((Dw[i][j] for i in range(nin) if s[i]), ZERO) for j in range(nout)]
        if mode == 'delay':
            Dvt = [Dvt[j] + sum((abs(U[k][j]) * Dh[k] for k in range(nout)), ZERO) for j in range(nout)]
        Dvt_all[t] = Dvt
        Dv2 = [ZERO if g[j] else Dvt[j] for j in range(nout)]
        Dx2 = [_dx(P, i) * Dx[i] for i in range(nin)]
        Dy2 = [_dy(P, j) * Dy[j] for j in range(nout)]
        coef = (1 - P['rho']) if P['plastic'] else F(1)
        Dw2 = [[None] * nout for _ in range(nin)]
        for i in range(nin):
            for j in range(nout):
                gj = P['gate_of'][j]
                a = coef * Dw[i][j]
                if P['plastic'] and P['bap'] and g[gj]:
                    a += P['Ap'] * Dx2[i]
                if P['plastic'] and P['bap'] and s[i]:
                    a += P['Am'] * Dy2[gj]
                Dw2[i][j] = a
        Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh = Dw2, Dv2, Dx2, Dy2, [0] * nout
    Dst[te + 1] = (Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh)
    return Dvt_all, Dst

def prop_bounds_no_switch(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0):
    """For comparison only: the wrong recursion without the plastic switch (weight-difference coefficient unconditionally 1 − ρ). Not used in any certificate."""
    Pn = dict(P)
    Pn['plastic'] = True
    if not P['plastic']:
        Pn['Ap'] = ZERO
        Pn['Am'] = ZERO
    return prop_bounds(Pn, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0)

def event_bound(P, e, te, Dvt_all, Dst):
    """Upper bound B_e on the component difference of event e at beat te (only between the run setting only T and the setting experiment), given that the gating patterns on [u, te] are the same."""
    Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh = Dst[te]
    kind = e[0]
    if kind in ('YIN', 'XIN', 'INJ'):
        return ZERO
    if kind == 'RESET':
        return Dvt_all[te][e[1]]
    if kind == 'LEAK':
        return (1 - P['lam'][e[1]]) * Dv[e[1]]
    if kind == 'SYN':
        return Dw[e[1]][e[2]]
    if kind == 'FIN':
        return abs(P['U'][e[1]][e[2]]) * Dh[e[1]] if P.get('mode') == 'delay' else ZERO
    if kind == 'HCOPY':
        return F(Dh[e[1]])
    if kind == 'XDEC':
        return (1 - _dx(P, e[1])) * Dx[e[1]]
    if kind == 'YDEC':
        return (1 - _dy(P, e[1])) * Dy[e[1]]
    if kind == 'PLUS':
        return P['Ap'] * _dx(P, e[1]) * Dx[e[1]]
    if kind == 'MINUS':
        gj = P['gate_of'][e[2]]
        return P['Am'] * _dy(P, gj) * Dy[gj]
    if kind == 'RELAX':
        return P['rho'] * Dw[e[1]][e[2]]
    if kind == 'CLIP':
        return Dst[te + 1][0][e[1]][e[2]]
    raise ValueError(kind)

def occurrence_fixed(P, e, te, Dst):
    """Whether the occurrence of the event is determined by gating, letters and switches (so that identical gating patterns on [u, te] ⇒ identical occurrence in the two runs)."""
    if e[0] == 'CLIP':
        return False
    if e[0] == 'FIN' and P.get('mode') == 'delay' and Dst[te][4][e[1]]:
        return False
    return True

def margins_ok(P, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dvt_all):
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        for j, zz in enumerate(z_obs[t]):
            if g_obs[t][j]:
                if not (zz - rv - Dvt_all[t][j] >= P['theta'][j]):
                    return False
            else:
                if not (zz + rv + Dvt_all[t][j] < P['theta'][j]):
                    return False
    return True

def m1_certificate(P, seq, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, D0, e, ev_obs, ev_act, r=ZERO, bounds=prop_bounds):
    """Proposition M-1. ev_obs, ev_act = (occurrence, component reading); occurrence is exact, component readings have radius r (component recorded as 0 when the event does not occur).
    Once all margins hold: for kinds whose occurrence is determined by gating, different occurrence ⇒ 1; both occur and |c_obs − c_act| > B_e + 2r ⇒ 1;
    CLIP, and FIN with side-effected h (occurrence not determined by gating): |c_obs − c_act| > B_e + 2r ⇒ 1 (component 0 when the event does not occur).
    Returns (verdict 1 or None, whether the margins hold)."""
    Dvt_all, Dst = bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0)
    if not margins_ok(P, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dvt_all):
        return None, False
    B = event_bound(P, e, te, Dvt_all, Dst)
    (oo, co), (oa, ca) = ev_obs, ev_act
    if occurrence_fixed(P, e, te, Dst):
        if oo != oa:
            return 1, True
        if oo and oa and abs(co - ca) > B + 2 * r:
            return 1, True
        return None, True
    if abs(co - ca) > B + 2 * r:
        return 1, True
    return None, True

def beta_star(P, seq, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dhat):
    """Under a unit side-effect profile Dhat (Dh must be 0), the supremum of κ for which the margin condition holds when D = κ·Dhat. Returns (κ*, strict?):
    the margins hold for κ < κ* (strict) or κ ≤ κ* (non-strict); κ* = None means they hold for every κ ≥ 0; κ* < 0 means the readings themselves fail the margins."""
    assert not any(Dhat[4])
    Dvt_all, _ = prop_bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, Dhat)
    best, strict = None, False
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        for j, zz in enumerate(z_obs[t]):
            d = Dvt_all[t][j]
            if g_obs[t][j]:
                room = zz - rv - P['theta'][j]
                if d == 0:
                    if room < 0:
                        return F(-1), False
                    continue
                k, st_ = room / d, False
            else:
                room = P['theta'][j] - zz - rv
                if d == 0:
                    if room <= 0:
                        return F(-1), False
                    continue
                k, st_ = room / d, True
            if best is None or k < best or (k == best and st_):
                best, strict = k, st_
    return best, strict

def changed(ev1, ev2):
    (o1, c1), (o2, c2) = ev1, ev2
    return o1 != o2 or (o1 and o2 and c1 != c2)

# ───────────────────────── Proposition M-2: twin with a globally fixed pat_B ─────────────────────────
def m2_twin(P, st0, labels, patA, I_A, u, te, Tin, beta_v, ga_base, ga_set):
    """Single-output 𝒦_g (mode 'g'). Frozen information: the label sequence labels, the firing readings ga_base and ga_set of the two runs (on [0, te]), and the declared identity of T.
    Tin: the set of entry units corresponding to T. Construction:
      pat_B(a) := pat_A(a) with the Tin components set to 0 (defined once per label and used throughout);
      the injection I_B(t) is chosen beat by beat: for t < u, so that B's firing equals A's prefix firing; for u ≤ t < te, so that neither run fires;
      for t = te, so that the gating inputs of the two runs fall on opposite sides of θ, in the same direction as A (side effect ±beta_v added to v).
    Returns dict(patB, I_B, seqB, side, used); used[t] = the (label, pattern) actually used at that beat, for checking beat by beat that the mapping is consistent."""
    theta = P['theta'][0]
    patB = {a: tuple(0 if i in Tin else bit for i, bit in enumerate(p)) for a, p in patA.items()}
    flip_up = (not ga_base[te]) and ga_set[te]
    side = {('v', 0): beta_v if flip_up else -beta_v}
    I_B = []
    st = copy_state(st0)
    used = []
    # prefix t < u: the two runs coincide; choose the injection beat by beat so that the gating equals A's prefix firing
    for t in range(u):
        s = list(patB[labels[t]])
        used.append((labels[t], tuple(s)))
        b = _base(P, st, s, [ZERO])[0]
        I = theta - b if ga_base[t] else theta - b - 1
        I_B.append([I])
        st, g, vt, ev = step(P, st, s, [I])
    # u..te: advance the two runs separately (same injection)
    stb = copy_state(st)
    w, v, x, y, h = copy_state(st)
    v[0] += side[('v', 0)]
    sts = (w, v, x, y, h)
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        s = list(patB[labels[t]])
        used.append((labels[t], tuple(s)))
        bb = _base(P, stb, s, [ZERO])[0]
        bs = _base(P, sts, s, [ZERO])[0]
        if t < te:
            I = theta - 1 - max(bb, bs)
        else:
            D = bs - bb
            if D == 0:
                return None
            I = (theta - D / 2 - bb) if D > 0 else (theta + (-D) / 2 - bb)
            # D > 0: baseline vt = θ − D/2 (no firing), setting vt = θ + D/2 (firing); the reverse for D < 0
        I_B.append([I])
        stb, _, _, _ = step(P, stb, s, [I])
        sts, _, _, _ = step(P, sts, s, [I])
    seqB = [(list(patB[labels[t]]), I_B[t]) for t in range(te + 1)]
    return {'patB': patB, 'I_B': I_B, 'seqB': seqB, 'side': side, 'used': used}
~~~

A.5  t13\_f1.py  Robust threshold-crossing conditions, event-by-event declaration, fixed-reading threshold and a priori design threshold of Proposition F-1 (8.1)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization: the threshold-crossing conditions, event-by-event declarations and intervention bounds of Proposition F-1. Exact rational arithmetic, standard library only.
Adds on top of t13_gate:
  f1_cross          robust DAN threshold-crossing condition of F-1 (5) (equality goes to the firing side, g = [vt ≥ θ])
~~~
