~~~python
  f1_event_change   declaration of F-1 (6): whether each target event at t₂ changes at t₂ when the DAN flips at t₂ (YIN_D, RESET_D, PLUS_iM, MINUS_iM)
  kappa_read        acceptance threshold for κ with the readings fixed (same as t13_gate.beta_star, returned branch by branch)
  kappa_design      a priori design threshold: readings unknown; gives a sufficient κ threshold for the guaranteed margins from model-predicted gating inputs (error ζ)
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_gate import prop_bounds, beta_star
ZERO = F(0)

def f1_cross(theta, z_act, rv, shift):
    """Baseline reading z_act (radius rv) and the model-given shift of the DAN gating input, shift = U_MD·Δg_M (exactly declared).
    Returns 'up' (the baseline does not fire, the run setting only T fires), 'down' (the reverse) or None (threshold crossing not guaranteed).
    up: z_act + rv < θ and z_act − rv + shift ≥ θ; down: z_act − rv ≥ θ and z_act + rv + shift < θ."""
    if z_act + rv < theta and z_act - rv + shift >= theta:
        return 'up'
    if z_act - rv >= theta and z_act + rv + shift < theta:
        return 'down'
    return None

def f1_event_change(P, kind, i, s_t2):
    """Whether the event at t₂ changes (model level) when the DAN (gating unit D) flips at t₂ and agrees with the baseline DAN at every τ before t₂.
    YIN_D, RESET_D: occurrence changes; PLUS_iM: occurrence changes ⟺ plastic ∧ bap; MINUS_iM: occurrence is the same in both runs (plastic ∧ bap ∧ s_i),
    component difference −A₋·Δg_D ⟺ changes if and only if plastic ∧ bap ∧ s_i(t₂) ∧ A₋ ≠ 0. No declaration is made for the other beats."""
    pb = bool(P['plastic'] and P['bap'])
    if kind in ('YIN', 'RESET'):
        return True
    if kind == 'PLUS':
        return pb
    if kind == 'MINUS':
        return pb and bool(s_t2[i]) and P['Am'] != 0
    raise ValueError(kind)

def kappa_read(P, seq, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dhat):
    """Acceptance threshold for κ with the readings fixed (per (t, j) branch):
      D̂_vt = 0: the constraint holds for every κ if m ≥ 0 on the firing side and m > 0 on the non-firing side; otherwise no κ is acceptable;
      D̂_vt > 0: κ ≤ m/D̂ on the firing side, κ < m/D̂ on the non-firing side; if m is negative (or m = 0 on the non-firing side) no κ ≥ 0 is acceptable;
      m = z − r_v − θ (firing) or θ − z − r_v (non-firing). If all D̂_vt = 0 and all constraints are satisfied, the threshold is +∞.
    Returns (status, κ*, strict): status 'all' (every κ ≥ 0), 'none' (no κ ≥ 0), 'bound' (κ ≤ κ* or κ < κ*)."""
    k, strict = beta_star(P, seq, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dhat)
    if k is None:
        return 'all', None, False
    if k < 0 or (k == 0 and strict):
        return 'none', k, strict
    return 'bound', k, strict

def kappa_design(P, seq, u, te, vt_pred, zeta, rv, g_pred, Dhat):
    """A priori design threshold (readings unknown). vt_pred[t][j]: the declared model's prediction of the gating input of the run setting only T (|error| ≤ zeta); g_pred is its gating.
    Sufficient condition: on the firing side vt_pred − ζ − 2r_v − 2κ D̂_vt ≥ θ, on the non-firing side vt_pred + ζ + 2r_v + 2κ D̂_vt < θ.
    (The vt of the setting experiment differs from that of the run setting only T by at most κD̂_vt, the reading deviates by a further r_v, and the margin test subtracts another r_v and κD̂_vt.) Returns the same as kappa_read."""
    Dvt_all, _ = prop_bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_pred, Dhat)
    best, strict = None, False
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        for j in range(len(vt_pred[t])):
            d = 2 * Dvt_all[t][j]
            if g_pred[t][j]:
                room, st_ = vt_pred[t][j] - zeta - 2 * rv - P['theta'][j], False
                if not vt_pred[t][j] >= P['theta'][j]:
                    return 'none', F(-1), False
            else:
                room, st_ = P['theta'][j] - vt_pred[t][j] - zeta - 2 * rv, True
                if not vt_pred[t][j] < P['theta'][j]:
                    return 'none', F(-1), False
            if d == 0:
                if room < 0 or (st_ and room == 0):
                    return 'none', F(-1), False
                continue
            k = room / d
            if best is None or k < best or (k == best and st_):
                best, strict = k, st_
    if best is None:
        return 'all', None, False
    if best < 0 or (best == 0 and strict):
        return 'none', best, strict
    return 'bound', best, strict
~~~

A.6  run\_minimal.py  Run entry: the minimal instance χ\_E (7.5)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization: the minimal instance chi_E (main text 7.5).
A model conclusion (Column A), not a certification of any material host. chi_E takes the abstract structure of E. coli chemotaxis; its parameters are constructed values.
Declaration summary:
  Frozen state box: every position has valid domain [0, hi], default hi = 2; side effects take only values in the legitimate side-effect interval, and the H0 budget uses the legitimate radius rho (b_legal = L·rho + zeta_b).
  T and the formation record: T = {x}; at beat t-1 an out-of-boundary entry pulse +1 writes x (record start point x = 0 <= eps, ENT trivial; REC covers t-1 to t),
  so Form(x) is recorded at t-1 and x is available at t — inside the model this comes from a legitimate history; read back onto a material host, it is a conditional declaration.
  Population: issue only “support existence” and (when M1 > B0) the support fraction; issue P_HC^∃ only on intersection with the availability fraction.
"""
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))   # so that python3 -I also works
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product
from math import comb
from t13_core import (value, hat, supp_act, consistent, dist_sets, act_rule, ch3_act, ch3_R, rlc_rule, rlc_star,
                      pop_supp_exists, pop_fraction, pop_null_bound, avail_fraction_lower, pop_hc_exists,
                      hoeffding_radius, mean_dist_two_point, lipschitz_on, rho_legal, b_legal, b_conservative,
                      rlc_legal, twin_legal, avail_ok, legal_side_interval)
EPS = F(1, 100)
LAWS = {'mB': ('lin', F(-1, 2), ('x',)), 'mR': ('lin', F(1, 4), ('y',)), 'm0': ('const', F(1, 4))}
NX = ('y',)
HI = {'x': F(2), 'y': F(2), 'z': F(2)}

def fmt(q):
    return str(q) if not isinstance(q, F) else (str(q.numerator) if q.denominator == 1 else f'{q.numerator}/{q.denominator}')

def perturbed(s, i, dprime, eta):
    s2 = dict(s)
    s2[i] = s[i] + dprime
    for j, e in eta.items():
        s2[j] = s[j] + e
    return s2

def in_box(s, hi):
    return all(F(0) <= s[p] <= hi[p] for p in s)

def grid(a, b, k=5):
    return [a + (b - a) * F(t, k - 1) for t in range(k)]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S0 legitimate history and availability
print('=' * 78)
print('S0  T, formation record and availability (produced inside the model by a legitimate history; a conditional declaration when read back onto a material host)')
print('=' * 78)
s_prev = {'x': F(0), 'y': F(1, 2), 'z': F(1, 2)}
pulse = F(1)                                  # beat t-1: an out-of-boundary entry pulse writes x; no mechanism runs in this beat
S = dict(s_prev)
S['x'] = s_prev['x'] + (pulse if pulse > EPS else F(0))      # Mathematical Form 4.2: effective pulses are booked
form_x = pulse > EPS and s_prev['x'] <= EPS                  # Chapter 3, 4.6 (6): an effective pulse takes the position across the threshold
print(f'  beat t-1 state {{x: 0, y: 1/2, z: 1/2}}, out-of-boundary pulse +1 at x (effective) ⇒ beat t state {{x: {fmt(S["x"])}, y: {fmt(S["y"])}, z: {fmt(S["z"])}}}, inside the box [0, 2]^3: {in_box(S, HI)}')
print(f'  T = {{x}}; Form(x) recorded at t-1: {form_x} (REC covers t-1 to t; record start point x = 0 ≤ eps, ENT trivial); Mark(x): the pulse is an out-of-boundary signal')
tau_s = F(1, 40)
av = avail_ok(True, S['x'], tau_s, EPS, form_x)
print(f'  availability atoms: i ∈ T; suprathreshold (lower bound of the state reading 1 ± {fmt(tau_s)} > eps); Form record (listed separately, not inferred from the state reading) ⇒ Avail(x) = {av}')
for m, law in LAWS.items():
    c = value(law, S)
    print(f'  {m}: c = {fmt(c)}, hat c = {fmt(hat(c, EPS))}, Act = {int(hat(c, EPS) != 0)}, x ∈ Supp_act = {supp_act(law, S, "x")}, '
          f'P_HC(m; x) = {supp_act(law, S, "x") and av}')
print(f'  net increment (4.2) = {fmt(sum(hat(value(l, S), EPS) for l in LAWS.values()))}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S1
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S1  incomplete intervention + reading error (bounded and statistical readings); the exact protocol of Chapter 3 cannot be carried out; valid-domain budget')
print('=' * 78)
gamma, beta, zeta, zeta_b, tau = F(1, 40), F(1, 10), F(1, 20), F(0), F(1, 40)
delta = F(-3, 4)
L_decl = F(1, 2)
n_units, alpha, pairs = 3, F(1, 20), 2
rho = rho_legal(S, NX, beta, HI)
b = b_legal(L_decl, rho, zeta_b)
print(f'  declared: gamma = {fmt(gamma)}, beta = {fmt(beta)}, N_x = {{y}}, zeta = {fmt(zeta)}, zeta_b = {fmt(zeta_b)}, tau = {fmt(tau)}, delta = {fmt(delta)}, L = {fmt(L_decl)}, box hi = 2')
print(f'  legitimate side-effect interval of y: {tuple(fmt(v) for v in legal_side_interval(S["y"], beta, HI["y"]))}; rho = {fmt(rho)}; b_legal = {fmt(b)}; conservative budget L·beta = {fmt(b_conservative(L_decl, beta, zeta_b))}')
a_each = alpha / (pairs * 2)
t_h = hoeffding_radius(n_units, tau, a_each)
r_b = gamma + tau
r_stat = gamma + min(tau, t_h)
print(f'  Hoeffding radius (n = {n_units}, a = {fmt(a_each)} per mean) t = {float(t_h):.6f}; r = gamma + min(tau, t) = {fmt(r_stat)}')
s1 = perturbed(S, 'x', delta, {'y': beta})
y0R, y1R = hat(value(LAWS['mR'], S), EPS), hat(value(LAWS['mR'], s1), EPS)
print(f'  Chapter 3, 4.6 (2) applied directly (eta_y = {fmt(beta)}): mR reading {fmt(y0R)} → {fmt(y1R)}, R_mR(x) = {ch3_R(y0R, y1R)}, misjudged (true value 0); the discipline of Chapter 3 gives ⊥ (as for Instance B of its 7.5)')

def sweep(m, rule):
    hits = tot = 0
    margins = []
    for dp, ey, k0, k1, e0, e1 in product(grid(delta - zeta, delta + zeta), grid(*legal_side_interval(S['y'], beta, HI['y'])),
                                          grid(-gamma, gamma), grid(-gamma, gamma), (-tau, F(0), tau), (-tau, F(0), tau)):
        s1 = perturbed(S, 'x', dp, {'y': ey})
        assert in_box(s1, HI)
        y0 = hat(value(LAWS[m], S), EPS) - k0 + e0
        y1 = hat(value(LAWS[m], s1), EPS) - k1 + e1
        tot += 1
        hits += (rule(y0, y1) == 1)
        margins.append(dist_sets(consistent(y0, r_b, EPS), consistent(y1, r_b, EPS)) - b)
    return hits, tot, min(margins), max(margins)

for m in ('mB', 'mR'):
    h, tot, mn, mx = sweep(m, lambda y0, y1: rlc_legal(y0, y1, r_b, EPS, L_decl, rho, zeta_b, av))
    print(f'  RLC (valid-domain budget, bounded reading r = {fmt(r_b)}) {m}: returns P_HC(m; x) = 1 in {h}/{tot}; dist - b ∈ [{fmt(mn)}, {fmt(mx)}]; '
          f'true P_HC = {int(supp_act(LAWS[m], S, "x") and av)}')
print('  (RLC certifies the support core; the S0 atoms give availability; their conjunction is P_HC.)')
dist_e = mean_dist_two_point(n_units, tau)
for m in ('mB', 'mR'):
    ps = []
    for dp, ey, k0, k1 in product(grid(delta - zeta, delta + zeta, 3), grid(*legal_side_interval(S['y'], beta, HI['y']), 3),
                                  grid(-gamma, gamma, 3), grid(-gamma, gamma, 3)):
        s1 = perturbed(S, 'x', dp, {'y': ey})
        mu0 = hat(value(LAWS[m], S), EPS) - k0
        mu1 = hat(value(LAWS[m], s1), EPS) - k1
        ps.append(sum(p0 * p1 for e0, p0 in dist_e.items() for e1, p1 in dist_e.items()
                      if rlc_legal(mu0 + e0, mu1 + e1, r_stat, EPS, L_decl, rho, zeta_b, av) == 1))
    print(f'  statistical reading {m}: P(verdict 1) ∈ [{fmt(min(ps))}, {fmt(max(ps))}] (under H0 it must be ≤ alpha/pairs = {fmt(alpha / pairs)})')
cB = hat(value(LAWS['mB'], S), EPS)
mnD = min(abs(cB - k + e) for k in (-gamma, gamma) for e in (-tau, tau))
print(f'  Act (Proposition 13-A): minimum |D| of mB = {fmt(mnD)} > gamma + tau = {fmt(gamma + tau)}: {mnD > gamma + tau}; '
      f'with compensation 1/10 > eps, the Chapter 3 rule on a process with hat c = 0 gives {ch3_act(F(1, 10), EPS)} (misjudgment), 13-A gives {act_rule(F(1, 10), F(1, 10), F(0))}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S1b boundary state
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S1b side-effect neighbour near the upper bound (hi_y = 3/4, s(y) = 1/2): the legitimate radius is truncated, and the conservative and valid-domain budgets separate (exact readings)')
print('=' * 78)
HIb = dict(HI); HIb['y'] = F(3, 4)
c0 = value(LAWS['mB'], S); c1 = value(LAWS['mB'], perturbed(S, 'x', delta, {}))
for bs in (F(1, 10), F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1), F(2)):
    rb = rho_legal(S, NX, bs, HIb)
    print(f'  beta = {fmt(bs):>5}: legitimate side-effect interval {tuple(fmt(v) for v in legal_side_interval(S["y"], bs, HIb["y"]))}, rho = {fmt(rb)}; '
          f'conservative RLC (b = L·beta = {fmt(L_decl * bs)}) = {rlc_rule(c0, c1, F(0), EPS, L_decl * bs)}; '
          f'valid-domain RLC (b = {fmt(b_legal(L_decl, rb, F(0)))}) = {rlc_legal(c0, c1, F(0), EPS, L_decl, rb, F(0))}; '
          f'twin_legal = {"none" if twin_legal(c0, c1, S, NX, bs, HIb, L_decl, F(0)) is None else "present"}')
print('  Interpretation: for beta ≥ 3/4 the conservative rule gives ⊥, but on the valid domain the maximal H0 response is L·rho ≤ 1/4 < |Δ| = 3/8, so the valid-domain rule soundly gives 1 (the case of a neighbour near the upper bound).')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S2
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S2  L undeclared: when a legitimate nonzero side effect is realizable (rho > 0), a verdict of 1 cannot be guaranteed; when rho = 0, no L is needed')
print('=' * 78)
D = c1 - c0
for bs in (F(1, 10), F(1, 100), F(1, 1000)):
    rb = rho_legal(S, NX, bs, HI)
    tw = twin_legal(c0, c1, S, NX, bs, HI, None, F(0))
    law, etas, om = tw
    sB = perturbed(S, 'x', delta, etas)
    ok = (in_box(sB, HI) and hat(value(law, S), EPS) == hat(c0, EPS) and hat(value(law, sB), EPS) == hat(c1, EPS)
          and not supp_act(law, S, 'x'))
    print(f"  beta = {fmt(bs):>7}: rho = {fmt(rb)}; legitimate twin m' = {fmt(law[3])} + {fmt(law[1])}·s(y), eta_y = {fmt(etas['y'])}, inside the box with readings identical value by value: {ok}")
S0y = dict(S); S0y['y'] = F(0); HI0 = dict(HI); HI0['y'] = F(0)
rb = rho_legal(S0y, NX, F(1, 10), HI0)
print(f'  variant: the valid domain of y degenerates to {{0}} (hi_y = 0): rho = {fmt(rb)}, b_legal = {fmt(b_legal(None, rb, F(0)))}, '
      f'with L undeclared rlc_legal = {rlc_legal(c0, c1, F(0), EPS, None, rb, F(0))} (the side effect can only be 0; LOC is in effect exclusive)')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S3
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S3  beta sweep with exact readings (box hi = 2): the boundary of a verdict of 1 coincides with the boundary of legitimate twins')
print('=' * 78)
print(f'  mB: c0 = {fmt(c0)}, c1 = {fmt(c1)}, |Δ| = {fmt(abs(D))}')
print(f'  (A) with intercept, L = 1/2: theoretical boundary L·rho = |Δ|, rho = min(beta, 3/2) ⇒ beta = 3/4')
for bs in (F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4) - F(1, 1000), F(3, 4), F(1)):
    rb = rho_legal(S, NX, bs, HI)
    v = rlc_legal(c0, c1, F(0), EPS, L_decl, rb, F(0))
    tw = twin_legal(c0, c1, S, NX, bs, HI, L_decl, F(0))
    if tw is None:
        txt = 'no legitimate twin exists (by the soundness proof of 13-B, a valid H0 cannot produce this pair of readings)'
    else:
        law, etas, om = tw
        sB = perturbed(S, 'x', delta, etas)
        txt = (f"legitimate twin m' = {fmt(law[3])} + {fmt(law[1])}·s(y), eta_y = {fmt(etas['y'])}, inside the box: {in_box(sB, HI)}, "
               f"identical readings: {hat(value(law, sB), EPS) == hat(c1, EPS) and hat(value(law, S), EPS) == hat(c0, EPS)}")
    print(f'    beta = {fmt(bs):>9}: rlc_legal = {v}; {txt}')
print('  (B) no intercept, L undeclared, RLC*: tightness is claimed only within the two-sided-headroom subdomain (s(j_min) ≥ beta and hi - s(j_min) ≥ beta)')
for bs in (F(1, 4), F(3, 8) - F(1, 1000), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)):
    v = rlc_star(c0, c1, F(0), EPS, bs, None, S['y'])
    sub = S['y'] >= bs and HI['y'] - S['y'] >= bs
    kp = c0 / S['y']; eta = D / kp
    sB = perturbed(S, 'x', delta, {'y': eta})
    ok = abs(eta) <= bs and in_box(sB, HI) and hat(value(('lin', kp, ('y',)), sB), EPS) == hat(c1, EPS)
    print(f"    beta = {fmt(bs):>9}: RLC* = {v}; inside the subdomain: {sub}; " + (f"intercept-free legitimate twin m' = {fmt(kp)}·s(y), eta_y = {fmt(eta)}" if (v is None and ok) else
          ('—' if v == 1 else 'twin not legitimate or outside the subdomain')))
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S4 population
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S4  population readings: support existence, support fraction (M1 > B0), intersection with the availability fraction')
print('=' * 78)
N = 4
popA = [F(-1, 2)] * N
popB = [F(-1), F(0), F(-1), F(0)]
popC = [F(-1, 4), F(-3, 4), F(-1, 2), F(-1, 2)]
same = all(sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), perturbed(S, 'x', dp, {})), EPS) for k in popA)
           == sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), perturbed(S, 'x', dp, {})), EPS) for k in popB)
           for dp in grid(F(-4, 5), F(-7, 10), 7) + [F(0)])
print(f'  populations A (all k = -1/2) and B (-1, 0, -1, 0) have identical mean readings at every setting value: {same}; individual-level support 4/4 vs 2/4 ⇒ “every individual” cannot be certified (Theta contains 0)')
b_pop = b; r_pop = gamma + tau
M1 = EPS + F(1) * (abs(delta) + zeta) + b_pop
print(f'  B0 = {fmt(pop_null_bound(EPS, b_pop))}, M1 = eps + K(|delta| + zeta) + b = {fmt(M1)} (K = 1), M1 > B0: {M1 > pop_null_bound(EPS, b_pop)}')
for name, pop in (('A', popA), ('B', popB), ('C', popC)):
    sA = perturbed(S, 'x', delta + zeta, {'y': beta})
    y0 = sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), S), EPS) for k in pop) / N + gamma - tau
    y1 = sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), sA), EPS) for k in pop) / N - gamma + tau
    se = pop_supp_exists(y0, y1, r_pop, EPS, b_pop)
    kind, f = pop_fraction(y0, y1, r_pop, EPS, b_pop, M1)
    print(f'  population {name}: mean reading {fmt(y0)} → {fmt(y1)}; support existence = {se}; support fraction: {kind} {fmt(f) if kind == "bound" else f}'
          f' (true support fraction {sum(supp_act(("lin", k, ("x",)), S, "x") for k in pop)}/{N})')
f_low = pop_fraction(sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), S), EPS) for k in popB) / N + gamma - tau,
                     sum(hat(value(('lin', k, ('x',)), perturbed(S, 'x', delta + zeta, {})), EPS) for k in popB) / N - gamma + tau,
                     r_pop, EPS, b_pop, M1)[1]
print('  intersection with availability (Proposition 13-E (5): f_low + a_low > 1 ⇒ P_HC^∃ = 1):')
print(f'    (i) inside the model every individual is formed by the legitimate history of S0 (a = 1, Column A): P_HC^∃ = {pop_hc_exists(f_low, F(1))}')
print(f'    (ii) only a population-mean state reading 1 ± {fmt(tau_s)}, box hi_x = 2, FORM_PROTO declared: a_low = {fmt(avail_fraction_lower(F(1), tau_s, EPS, F(2)))}, '
      f'P_HC^∃ = {pop_hc_exists(f_low, avail_fraction_lower(F(1), tau_s, EPS, F(2)))} (insufficient evidence, ⊥)')
print(f'    (iii) as above but hi_x = 1 (individual states at the upper bound): a_low = {fmt(avail_fraction_lower(F(1), F(0), EPS, F(1)))}, P_HC^∃ = {pop_hc_exists(f_low, avail_fraction_lower(F(1), F(0), EPS, F(1)))}')
print(f'    (iv) no formation record (record start point already suprathreshold, ENT undeclared): a_low undetermined, P_HC^∃ = {pop_hc_exists(f_low, None)}; support existence still = 1')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S5
print()
print('=' * 78)
print('S5  sampling units: cells from the same culture share a batch bias (Act, H0 process)')
print('=' * 78)
tau_b, tau_e, cells = F(1, 20), F(1, 20), 400
t_ill = hoeffding_radius(cells, tau_b + tau_e, alpha / 2)
q = F(0)
for bs in (-1, 1):
    for k in range(cells + 1):
        e = bs * tau_b + tau_e * F(2 * k - cells, cells)
        if act_rule(e, F(0), t_ill) != 1:
            q += F(1, 2) * F(comb(cells, k), 2 ** cells)
print(f'  wrongly treating the n = {cells} cells as independent units: t = {float(t_ill):.6f}; under H0 P(verdict 1) = 1 - {float(q):.3e}')
print(f'  counting units by culture (n = 1, support bound r = {fmt(tau_b + tau_e)}): P(verdict 1) = 0')
print()
print('Run finished.')
~~~

A.7  run\_checks.py  Run entry: finite verification of the F-class single-beat criteria (5.1–5.5, 5.8)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization: finite verification of the F-class single-beat criteria (randomly generated; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text).
C5a, C5a′ and C5b are controls: their twin constructions check only nonnegativity, not the state upper bound, and serve to show that the valid-domain version (C9) is necessary; C9–C12 are verifications on the frozen valid domain.
C0 membership check of the compatible set I(y; r, eps)
C1 soundness of Proposition 13-B (RLC, Lipschitz budget, linear- and bilinear-law classes): verdict 1 ⇒ i ∈ Supp_act
C2 soundness of Proposition 13-C (RLC*, baseline calibration, linear-law class)
C3 degenerate compatibility: with gamma = beta = zeta = zeta_b = tau = 0, RLC, RLC* ≡ Chapter 3 R_m(i); Proposition 13-A ≡ Chapter 3, 4.6 (1)
C4 Lemma 13-B1: the naive difference rule |y1 - y0| > b + 2r is unsound (count of H0 instances giving 1, and one example)
C5 Proposition 13-D (non-certifiability bound): every effective-region case in which RLC* gives ⊥ has an H0 twin with readings identical value by value (exact readings, L undeclared)
C6 Proposition 13-E (1): soundness of the population existence certificate; B0 is attained
C7 M2: the transfer lemma for linear-law support atoms, and three kinds of counterexample
C8 exact coverage of the Hoeffding radius under two-point errors
"""
import random
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))   # so that python3 -I also works
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_core import (legal_side_interval, rho_legal, b_legal, b_conservative, rlc_legal, twin_legal, diff_rule,
                      avail_ok, pop_supp_exists, pop_fraction, avail_fraction_lower, pop_hc_exists, dist_sets,
                      value, hat, supp_act, consistent, gap, rlc_rule, rlc_star, rlc_star_margin, act_rule, ch3_act,
                      ch3_R, pop_exists, pop_null_bound, hoeffding_radius, mean_dist_two_point, lipschitz_on)
rng = random.Random(20261102)
POS = ['p0', 'p1', 'p2', 'p3', 'p4']

def rq(lo, hi, den=8):
    """Random rational in [lo, hi] with denominator den."""
    a = int(lo * den)
    b = int(hi * den)
    return F(rng.randint(a, b), den)

def rand_state(hi):
    s = {}
    for p in POS:
        s[p] = F(0) if rng.random() < 0.2 else rq(F(0), hi)
    return s

def rand_law(K, rmax, allow_bil=True, avoid=None):
    """Random law with |k| <= K; if avoid is given, generate a law that does not read avoid (one of the H0 constructions)."""
    kinds = ['const', 'lin', 'lin', 'bil'] if allow_bil else ['const', 'lin', 'lin']
    kind = rng.choice(kinds)
    k = rq(-K, K)
    pool = [p for p in POS if p != avoid] if avoid else POS
    if kind == 'const':
        return ('const', k)
    b0 = rq(F(-1), F(1)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)   # intercept of Mathematical Form D12
    if kind == 'lin':
        n = rng.randint(1, rmax)
        return ('lin', k, tuple(rng.choice(pool) for _ in range(n)), b0)
    return ('bil', k, (rng.choice(POS if not avoid else POS), rng.choice(pool)), b0)

def fmt(q):
    return str(q) if q.denominator != 1 else str(q.numerator)

out = []

def say(x=''):
    print(x)
    out.append(x)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C0
bad = 0
cnt = 0
for _ in range(400):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    y = rq(F(-2), F(2))
    r = rq(F(0), F(1))
    I = consistent(y, r, eps)
    for t in range(-200, 201):
        c = F(t, 50)
        cnt += 1
        mem = (y - r <= hat(c, eps) <= y + r)
        inU = any(a <= c <= b for a, b in I)
        if mem and not inU:
            bad += 1
        if inU and not mem and c not in (eps, -eps):
            bad += 1
say(f'C0 compatible set: {cnt} grid points checked, {bad} violations (closure endpoints ±eps excepted)')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C1
viol = 0
n_h0 = 0
n_alt = 0
cert_alt = 0
for _ in range(30000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    K, rmax, hi = F(1), 3, F(2)
    s = rand_state(hi)
    cand = [p for p in POS if s[p] > eps]
    if not cand:
        continue
    i = rng.choice(cand)
    Ni = tuple(p for p in POS if p != i and rng.random() < 0.5)
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    zeta = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    zeta_b = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    gamma = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    tau = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    law = rand_law(K, rmax)
    if law[0] == 'bil':
        # state upper bound for bilinear laws: within hi
        pass
    # declared H0 budget (independent of the true law; depends only on the declared class and bounds)
    if Ni:
        b = max(K * rmax * beta, K * (2 * hi * beta + beta * beta), K * hi * beta) + zeta_b
    else:
        b = zeta_b
    delta = rq(-s[i], hi - s[i])
    if delta == 0:
        continue
    lo_d, hi_d = max(-s[i], delta - zeta), min(hi - s[i], delta + zeta)
    dprime = rq(lo_d, hi_d, 64) if lo_d <= hi_d else delta
    if not (-s[i] <= dprime <= hi - s[i]):
        continue
    s1 = dict(s)
    s1[i] = s[i] + dprime
    ok = True
    for j in Ni:
        e = rq(-beta, beta, 64)
        if s[j] + e < 0 or s[j] + e > hi:
            e = F(0)
        s1[j] = s[j] + e
    omega = rq(-zeta_b, zeta_b, 64) if zeta_b else F(0)
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1) + omega
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    v = rlc_rule(y0, y1, gamma + tau, eps, b)
    truth = supp_act(law, s, i)
    if truth:
        n_alt += 1
        cert_alt += (v == 1)
    else:
        n_h0 += 1
        if v == 1:
            viol += 1
say(f'C1 RLC soundness: {n_h0} H0 cases, {viol} violations giving 1; {n_alt} support cases, {cert_alt} of them giving 1')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C2
viol = 0
n_h0 = 0
n_alt = 0
cert_alt = 0
for _ in range(30000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    hi = F(2)
    s = rand_state(hi)
    cand = [p for p in POS if s[p] > eps]
    if not cand:
        continue
    i = rng.choice(cand)
    Ni = tuple(p for p in POS if p != i and rng.random() < 0.5)
    if not Ni:
        continue
    smin = min(s[j] for j in Ni)
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    zeta = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    zeta_b = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    gamma = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    tau = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    L = None if rng.random() < 0.5 else rq(F(0), F(3))
    k = rq(F(-1), F(1))
    n = rng.randint(1, 4)
    law = ('lin', k, tuple(rng.choice(POS) for _ in range(n)))
    if L is not None and lipschitz_on(law, s, Ni, F(1), hi) > L:
        continue  # the declaration must be truthful
    if smin == 0 and L is None:
        continue
    delta = rq(-s[i], hi - s[i])
    if delta == 0:
        continue
    dprime = delta
    s1 = dict(s)
    s1[i] = s[i] + dprime
    for j in Ni:
        s1[j] = s[j] + rq(-min(beta, s[j]), beta, 64)
    omega = rq(-zeta_b, zeta_b, 64) if zeta_b else F(0)
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1) + omega
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    v = rlc_star(y0, y1, gamma + tau, eps, beta, L, smin, zeta_b)
    truth = supp_act(law, s, i)
    if truth:
        n_alt += 1
        cert_alt += (v == 1)
    else:
        n_h0 += 1
        viol += (v == 1)
say(f'C2 RLC* soundness (declared intercept-free linear-law subclass): {n_h0} H0 cases, {viol} violations giving 1; {n_alt} support cases, {cert_alt} of them giving 1')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C2'

for _ in range(20000):

    s = {'i': rq(F(1, 4), F(2)), 'y': rq(F(1, 8), F(2))}
    beta = rq(F(1, 100), F(1, 2), 100)
    k = rq(F(-2), F(2), 16)
    b0 = rq(F(-2), F(2), 16)
    law = ('lin', k, ('y',), b0)              # H0: does not read i, has an intercept
    delta = -s['i'] / 2
    s1 = {'i': s['i'] + delta, 'y': s['y'] + beta}
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1)
    n_h0 += 1
    if rlc_star(hat(c0, eps), hat(c1, eps), F(0), eps, beta, None, s['y']) == 1:
        viol += 1
        if ex2 is None:
            ex2 = (law, s, beta, c0, c1)
say(f"C2' RLC* applied to F laws with intercept (D12): among {n_h0} H0 instances, {viol} misjudged as 1 (hence 13-C must declare no intercept)")
if ex2:

    say(f"   example: m = {fmt(law[3])} + {fmt(law[1])}·s(y), s(y) = {fmt(s_['y'])}, beta = {fmt(beta)}, component {fmt(c0)} → {fmt(c1)}")
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C3

    y0, y1 = hat(value(law, s), eps), hat(value(law, s1), eps)

    b_ = ch3_R(y0, y1)
    c = rlc_star(y0, y1, F(0), eps, F(0), None, F(1))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C4
naive_bad = 0

for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(1, 100), F(1, 4), 100)
    beta = rq(F(1, 100), F(1, 2), 100)
    L = F(1, 2)
    b = L * beta
    # H0: m reads y, not i; the baseline component lies just below the threshold
    sy = rq(F(1, 2), F(2))
    k = (eps / sy) * rng.choice([F(1), F(99, 100), F(1, 2)])
    law = ('lin', k, ('y',))
    s = {'i': F(1), 'y': sy}
    s1 = {'i': F(1, 2), 'y': sy + beta}
    if lipschitz_on(law, s, ('y',), F(1), F(3)) > L:
        continue
    y0, y1 = hat(value(law, s), eps), hat(value(law, s1), eps)
    tot += 1
    naive = 1 if abs(y1 - y0) > b else None
    if naive == 1:
        naive_bad += 1
        if example is None:
            example = (eps, beta, k, sy, y0, y1, rlc_rule(y0, y1, F(0), eps, b))
say(f'C4 naive difference rule (margin b + 2r; only this rule breaks, the constant margin eps + b + 2r is sound, see C12): among {tot} H0 instances, {naive_bad} give 1 (misjudged); RLC gives 1 on 0 of these instances (by the proof of C1)')
if example:
    eps, beta, k, sy, y0, y1, v = example
    say(f'   example: eps = {fmt(eps)}, beta = {fmt(beta)}, m = {fmt(k)}·s(y), s(y) = {fmt(sy)}, readings {fmt(y0)} → {fmt(y1)}, '
        f'|Δ| = {fmt(abs(y1 - y0))} > b = {fmt(F(1, 2) * beta)}; RLC = {v}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C5a
def twin_lin_intercept(c0, c1, sj, L):
    """H0 twin m' = b' + k'·s(j), |k'| = L, eta = (c1 - c0)/k', with the sign of k' chosen so that eta ≥ 0 (which guarantees s(j) + eta ≥ 0)."""
    D = c1 - c0
    if D == 0:
        return ('lin', L, ('j',), c0 - L * sj), F(0)
    kp = L if D > 0 else -L
    eta = D / kp
    return ('lin', kp, ('j',), c0 - kp * sj), eta

n_bot = n_twin = n_one = n_boundary = 0
n_hi_viol = 0
for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    L = rq(F(1, 8), F(2))
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1))
    gamma = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    tau = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    r = gamma + tau
    b = L * beta
    si, sj = rq(F(1, 4), F(2)), rq(F(0), F(2))
    law = ('lin', rq(F(-1), F(1)), ('i',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    delta = rq(-si, F(2) - si)
    c0 = value(law, {'i': si})
    c1 = value(law, {'i': si + delta})
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    if rlc_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b) == 1:
        n_one += 1
        continue
    n_bot += 1
    # on the compatible sets, take a pair of true members with |c1' - c0'| ≤ b (exact construction: nearest points, moved inward by h if needed) and build the twin
    I0, I1 = consistent(y0, r, eps), consistent(y1, r, eps)
    mem = lambda c, y: y - r <= hat(c, eps) <= y + r
    found = False
    boundary = False
    for P in I0:
        for Q in I1:
            g = gap(P, Q)
            if g > b:
                continue
            if P[1] < Q[0]:
                a0, a1, d0, d1 = P[1], Q[0], -1, 1
            elif Q[1] < P[0]:
                a0, a1, d0, d1 = P[0], Q[1], 1, -1
            else:
                lo_, hi_ = max(P[0], Q[0]), min(P[1], Q[1])
                a0 = a1 = (lo_ + hi_) / 2
                d0 = d1 = 0
            h = (b - g) / 4
            h0 = min(h, (P[1] - P[0]) / 2)
            h1 = min(h, (Q[1] - Q[0]) / 2)
            hs0 = [F(0)] if h0 == 0 else [F(0), h0, -h0]
            hs1 = [F(0)] if h1 == 0 else [F(0), h1, -h1]
            cands = [(a0 + u, a1 + v) for u in hs0 for v in hs1
                     if P[0] <= a0 + u <= P[1] and Q[0] <= a1 + v <= Q[1]]
            for p0, p1 in cands:
                if mem(p0, y0) and mem(p1, y1) and abs(p1 - p0) <= b:
                    tw, eta = twin_lin_intercept(p0, p1, sj, L)
                    tc0 = value(tw, {'j': sj})
                    tc1 = value(tw, {'j': sj + eta})
                    if (abs(eta) <= beta and sj + eta >= 0 and mem(tc0, y0) and mem(tc1, y1)
                            and not supp_act(tw, {'i': si, 'j': sj}, 'i')
                            and lipschitz_on(tw, {'j': sj}, ('j',), F(1), F(9)) <= L):
                        found = True
                        if sj + eta > 2:
                            n_hi_viol += 1
            if not found and g == b:
                boundary = True
    n_boundary += (not found) and boundary
    n_twin += found
say(f'C5a [control: state upper bound not kept; for the valid-domain version see C9] Proposition 13-D (RLC is maximal among criteria that use only this pair of process readings; linear law with intercept, L declared, compensation and error included): RLC gives ⊥ in {n_bot} cases, '
    f'H0 twins with compatible readings constructed exactly in {n_twin}; {n_bot - n_twin} not constructed, of which {n_boundary} are boundary cases with dist = b where the infimum is attained only at the open endpoints ±eps; {n_one} return 1')
say(f'    control: the twins above check only s(j) + eta >= 0, not the state upper bound; with hi = 2, {n_hi_viol} twin constructions exceed the upper bound (for the valid-domain version see C9)')
# L undeclared: for any beta > 0 a twin always exists (with intercept)
n_all = n_ok = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    beta = rq(F(1, 1000), F(1, 10), 1000)
    si, sj = rq(F(1, 4), F(2)), rq(F(0), F(2))
    law = ('lin', rq(F(-4), F(4)), ('i',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    delta = rq(-si, F(2) - si)
    c0, c1 = value(law, {'i': si}), value(law, {'i': si + delta})
    n_all += 1
    D = c1 - c0
    kp = (abs(D) / beta) if D != 0 else F(1)
    kp = kp if D >= 0 else -kp
    eta = D / kp if D != 0 else F(0)
    tw = ('lin', kp, ('j',), c0 - kp * sj)
    n_ok += (abs(eta) <= beta and sj + eta >= 0 and hat(value(tw, {'j': sj}), eps) == hat(c0, eps)
             and hat(value(tw, {'j': sj + eta}), eps) == hat(c1, eps))
say(f'C5a′ [control: state upper bound not checked; for the conclusion on the valid domain see main text 13-D (1) and run_minimal S2] L undeclared (linear law with intercept): over {n_all} arbitrary instances with beta ∈ [1/1000, 1/10], H0 twins with readings identical value by value exist in {n_ok}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C5b
n5b_hi = 0
n5b_out_sub = 0
n_bot = 0
n_twin = 0
n_one = 0
for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    hi = F(2)
    s = rand_state(hi)
    cand = [p for p in POS if s[p] > eps]
    if not cand:
        continue
    i = rng.choice(cand)
    Ni = tuple(p for p in POS if p != i and rng.random() < 0.6)
    if not Ni:
        continue
    smin = min(s[j] for j in Ni)
    if smin == 0:
        continue
    jmin = min(Ni, key=lambda j: s[j])
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1))
    k = rq(F(-1), F(1))
    law = ('lin', k, tuple(rng.choice(POS) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3))))
    delta = rq(-s[i], hi - s[i])
    s1 = dict(s)
    s1[i] = s[i] + delta
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1)
    if abs(c0) <= eps or abs(c1) <= eps:
        continue  # check only the effective region
    v = rlc_star(c0, c1, F(0), eps, beta, None, smin)
    if v == 1:
        n_one += 1
        continue
    n_bot += 1
    kp = c0 / s[jmin]
    eta = (c1 - c0) / kp
    twin = ('lin', kp, (jmin,))
    sB = dict(s)
    sB[i] = s[i] + delta
    sB[jmin] = s[jmin] + eta
    if (abs(eta) <= beta and s[jmin] + eta >= 0 and hat(value(twin, s), eps) == hat(c0, eps)
            and hat(value(twin, sB), eps) == hat(c1, eps) and not supp_act(twin, s, i)):
        n_twin += 1
        if s[jmin] + eta > hi:
            n5b_hi += 1
        if not (s[jmin] >= beta and hi - s[jmin] >= beta):
            n5b_out_sub += 1
say(f'C5b [control] paired twin of Proposition 13-C (intercept-free subclass, exact readings, effective region, L undeclared): RLC* gives ⊥ in {n_bot} cases, H0 twins with readings identical value by value constructed in {n_twin}; {n_one} return 1')
say(f'    control: of these, {n5b_hi} twins exceed the state upper bound hi = 2, and {n5b_out_sub} lie outside the two-sided-headroom subdomain (s(j_min) >= beta and hi - s(j_min) >= beta) — the tightness of 13-C holds only within that subdomain')

    y0 = sum(ys0) / N
    y1 = sum(ys1) / N
    tot += 1
    if pop_exists(y0, y1, F(0), eps, b) == 1:
        viol += 1
~~~

~~~python
    f'conservative-budget RLC = {_rlc(c0, c1, F(0), eps, F(1))}, valid-domain-budget rlc_legal = {rlc_legal(c0, c1, F(0), eps, F(1), rho, F(0))}; '
    f'the twin that ignores the valid domain, eta = {fmt(old_tw_eta)}, makes y = {fmt(s9["y"] + old_tw_eta)} > hi = 1 (not legitimate, rejected); twin_legal = {twin_legal(c0, c1, s9, ("y",), F(1), hi9, F(1), F(0))}; '
    f'enumerating valid H0 (reading y, multiplicity ≤ 6, |k|·n ≤ 1): maximal |Δ| = {fmt(mx)}')
# neighbour at the upper bound: hi = 1, s(y) = 1 = hi, beta = 1
s9 = {'x': F(1, 2), 'y': F(1)}
law = ('lin', F(2), ('x',))
c0, c1 = value(law, s9), value(law, {'x': F(3, 4), 'y': F(1)})
mxc1 = None
for n in range(1, 7):
    k = c0 / (n * s9['y'])                       # no intercept, reads only y (the positions are just x and y); the baseline fixes k·n
    lo_, up_ = legal_side_interval(s9['y'], F(1), F(1))
    for t in range(0, 11):
        eta = lo_ + (up_ - lo_) * F(t, 10)
        v = k * n * (s9['y'] + eta)
        mxc1 = v if mxc1 is None or v > mxc1 else mxc1
say(f'C9b neighbour at the upper bound: c0 = {fmt(c0)}, c1 = {fmt(c1)}; RLC* (L undeclared) = {rlc_star(c0, c1, F(0), eps, F(1), None, F(1))}; the twin that ignores the valid domain, eta = 1/2, makes y = 3/2 > hi = 1 (not legitimate, rejected); '
    f'the upper headroom of y is hi - s(y) = 0 < beta = 1, so this lies outside the tightness subdomain of 13-C (outside the shrunk domain); the maximal post-setting component over enumerated intercept-free valid H0 = {fmt(mxc1)} < c1 ⇒ verdict 1 is sound on the valid domain (direct argument in main text 5.3)')
# random: soundness and maximality on the valid domain, including empty N, L = 0, beta = 0 and boundary states
viol = 0; n_h0 = 0; n_bot = 0; n_twin = 0; n_bd = 0; n_one = 0; n_unb = 0
cases = {'N empty': 0, 'L=0': 0, 'beta=0': 0, 'boundary state': 0}
cons_ok = 0; n_sh = 0; n_sh_ok = 0
for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    P = ['i', 'j1', 'j2', 'o']
    hi = {p: rq(F(1, 4), F(2)) for p in P}
    st = {}
    for p in P:
        u = rng.random()
        st[p] = F(0) if u < 0.2 else (hi[p] if u < 0.4 else rq(F(0), hi[p], 16))
    st['i'] = max(st['i'], eps + F(1, 16)) if eps + F(1, 16) <= hi['i'] else hi['i']
    if st['i'] <= eps:
        continue
    Ni = tuple(p for p in ('j1', 'j2') if rng.random() < 0.6)
    beta = F(0) if rng.random() < 0.1 else rq(F(0), F(1))
    L = F(0) if rng.random() < 0.1 else rq(F(0), F(2))
    zeta_b = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)) if rng.random() < 0.3 else F(0)
    gamma = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    tau = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else F(0)
    r = gamma + tau
    if not Ni: cases['N empty'] += 1
    if L == 0: cases['L=0'] += 1
    if beta == 0: cases['beta=0'] += 1
    if any(st[j] in (0, hi[j]) for j in Ni): cases['boundary state'] += 1
    rho = rho_legal(st, Ni, beta, hi)
    bl = b_legal(L, rho, zeta_b)
    bc = b_conservative(L, beta, zeta_b)
    cons_ok += (bl <= bc)
    # true law: H0 or support; the declared L must be truthful
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        reads_ = tuple(rng.choice(['j1', 'j2', 'o']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3)))
    else:
        reads_ = tuple(['i'] + [rng.choice(['j1', 'j2', 'o']) for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 2))])
    nN = sum(1 for q in reads_ if q in Ni)
    kmax = (L / nN) if nN else F(2)
    k = rq(-kmax, kmax, 64) if kmax > 0 else F(0)
    law = ('lin', k, reads_, rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    lo_d, up_d = -st['i'], hi['i'] - st['i']
    dprime = rq(lo_d, up_d, 64)
    s1 = dict(st); s1['i'] = st['i'] + dprime
    for j in Ni:
        lo_, up_ = legal_side_interval(st[j], beta, hi[j])
        s1[j] = st[j] + (lo_ + (up_ - lo_) * F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8))
    omega = rq(-zeta_b, zeta_b, 64) if zeta_b else F(0)
    c0, c1 = value(law, st), value(law, s1) + omega
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    v = rlc_legal(y0, y1, r, eps, L, rho, zeta_b)
    if not supp_act(law, st, 'i'):
        n_h0 += 1
        viol += (v == 1)
    if v == 1:
        n_one += 1
        continue
    n_bot += 1
    I0, I1 = consistent(y0, r, eps), consistent(y1, r, eps)
    mem = lambda c, y: y - r <= hat(c, eps) <= y + r
    found = False; boundary = False
    for Pp in I0:
        for Q in I1:
            g = gap(Pp, Q)
            if g > bl:
                continue
            if Pp[1] < Q[0]:
                a0, a1 = Pp[1], Q[0]
            elif Q[1] < Pp[0]:
                a0, a1 = Pp[0], Q[1]
            else:
                a0 = a1 = (max(Pp[0], Q[0]) + min(Pp[1], Q[1])) / 2
            h = (bl - g) / 4
            h0 = min(h, (Pp[1] - Pp[0]) / 2); h1 = min(h, (Q[1] - Q[0]) / 2)
            for u in ([F(0)] if h0 == 0 else [F(0), h0, -h0]):
                for w in ([F(0)] if h1 == 0 else [F(0), h1, -h1]):
                    p0, p1 = a0 + u, a1 + w
                    if not (Pp[0] <= p0 <= Pp[1] and Q[0] <= p1 <= Q[1]):
                        continue
                    if not (mem(p0, y0) and mem(p1, y1) and abs(p1 - p0) <= bl):
                        continue
                    tw = twin_legal(p0, p1, st, Ni, beta, hi, L, zeta_b)
                    if tw is None:
                        continue
                    law_t, etas, om = tw
                    sB = dict(st); sB['i'] = st['i'] + dprime
                    okb = True
                    for j, e in etas.items():
                        lo_, up_ = legal_side_interval(st[j], beta, hi[j])
                        okb &= (lo_ <= e <= up_)
                        sB[j] = st[j] + e
                    okb &= all(F(0) <= sB[q] <= hi[q] for q in sB)
                    tc0 = value(law_t, st); tc1 = value(law_t, sB) + om
                    okL = law_t[0] == 'const' or abs(law_t[1]) * sum(1 for q in law_t[2] if q in Ni) <= L
                    if okb and okL and mem(tc0, y0) and mem(tc1, y1) and not supp_act(law_t, st, 'i'):
                        found = True
                        # variant SH: when successors are also box-constrained, can a constant process at the tgt position (a separate position tg, state hi/2) keep both successors inside the box
                        n_sh += 1
                        n_sh_ok += (abs(tc1 - tc0) <= F(1))
            if not found and g == bl:
                boundary = True
    n_twin += found
    n_bd += (not found) and boundary
say(f'C9c random verification on the valid domain (40% of states take the boundary value 0 or hi; N empty {cases["N empty"]}, L = 0 {cases["L=0"]}, beta = 0 {cases["beta=0"]}, boundary state at a neighbour {cases["boundary state"]} cases):')
say(f'    b_legal ≤ L·beta + zeta_b holds throughout {cons_ok}/{cons_ok}; soundness: {n_h0} H0 cases, {viol} rlc_legal violations giving 1')
~~~

say(f'    maximality: rlc\_legal gives ⊥ in {n\_bot} cases; legitimate (setting, side effect, L, inside the box) H0 twins with compatible readings constructed in {n\_twin}; {n\_bot - n\_twin} not constructed, '

~~~text
    f'of which {n_bd} are boundary cases with dist = b_legal where the infimum is attained only at open endpoints; {n_one} return 1')
say(f'    variant SH (successors also box-constrained; tgt position with upper bound 1 and state 1/2): of {n_sh} twins, {n_sh_ok} admit a constant process that brings the successors into the box (by default, legitimate perturbations per Chapter 3, 3.5 require only nonnegative successors, which can always be met)')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C10 no formation record
eps = F(1, 100)
law = ('lin', F(1, 2), ('x',))
y0, y1 = value(law, {'x': F(1)}), value(law, {'x': F(3, 2)})
se = pop_supp_exists(y0, y1, F(0), eps, F(0))
av = avail_ok(True, F(1), F(0), eps, False)
a_low = F(0)   # no formation record: the lower bound on the availability fraction is 0
~~~

say(f'C10a suprathreshold without a formation record: readings {fmt(y0)} → {fmt(y1)}; support existence SuppEx = {se}; individual availability atom = {av} (suprathreshold but no Form record); '

~~~text
    f'P_HC^∃ = pop_hc_exists(f, 0) = {pop_hc_exists(F(1), a_low)} (⊥) — the population rule issues only “support existence”')
viol = 0; tot = 0; cert = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)); N = rng.randint(1, 8); K = F(1); beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 4)); L = F(1, 2)
    b = L * beta; delta = F(1, 2)
    M1 = eps + K * delta + b
    ys0 = []; ys1 = []; states = []; sup = []; formed = []
    for _n in range(N):
        si = rq(F(0), F(2))
        start_low = rng.random() < 0.7          # FORM_PROTO: record start point ≤ eps, so suprathreshold implies a threshold-crossing formation record
        frm = start_low and si > eps
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            lw = ('lin', rq(-K, K), ('i',), rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2)))
        else:
            lw = ('lin', rq(-L, L), ('y',), rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2)))
        sy = rq(F(0), F(2))
        s_ = {'i': si, 'y': sy}
        s1_ = {'i': si + delta, 'y': sy + rq(-min(beta, sy), beta, 64)}
        ys0.append(hat(value(lw, s_), eps)); ys1.append(hat(value(lw, s1_), eps))
        states.append(si); sup.append(supp_act(lw, s_, 'i')); formed.append(frm)
    if not all(formed[n] == (states[n] > eps) for n in range(N)):
        # use the lower bound on the availability fraction only on populations that declare FORM_PROTO
        a_low = None
    else:
        a_low = avail_fraction_lower(sum(states) / N, F(0), eps, F(2))
    kind, f = pop_fraction(sum(ys0) / N, sum(ys1) / N, F(0), eps, b, M1)
    f_low = f if kind == 'bound' else F(0)
    v = pop_hc_exists(f_low, a_low)
    tot += 1
    if v == 1:
        cert += 1
        if not any(sup[n] and formed[n] for n in range(N)):
            viol += 1
say(f'C10b intersection of support and availability (Proposition 13-E (5)): {tot} populations, P_HC^∃ gives 1 in {cert}, of which violations with no individual both supporting and available: {viol}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C11 antecedent of the fraction lower bound
say(f'C11a M1 < B0: eps = 1, b = 1 (B0 = 2), M1 = 1, readings 2 → 5/2: pop_fraction = {pop_fraction(F(2), F(5, 2), F(0), F(1), F(1), F(1))}; '
    f'the formula without the antecedent gives (1/2 - 2)/(1 - 2) = 3/2 > true fraction 1/2, unusable')
say(f'C11b M1 = B0 = 2: pop_fraction = {pop_fraction(F(2), F(5, 2), F(0), F(1), F(1), F(2))}')
say(f'C11c incompatible readings (B0 = 3/20, M1 = 1/2, mean difference 1): pop_fraction = {pop_fraction(F(0), F(1), F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 20), F(1, 2))}')
viol = 0; inc = 0; nb = 0; tot = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)); N = rng.randint(1, 8); beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 4)); L = F(1, 2); b = L * beta
    K = F(1); delta = F(1, 2); M1 = eps + K * delta + b
    ys0 = []; ys1 = []; sup = []
    for _n in range(N):
        si = rq(eps + F(1, 8), F(2)); sy = rq(F(0), F(2))
        lw = ('lin', rq(-K, K), ('i',)) if rng.random() < 0.5 else ('lin', rq(-L, L), ('y',))
        ys0.append(hat(value(lw, {'i': si, 'y': sy}), eps))
        ys1.append(hat(value(lw, {'i': si + delta, 'y': sy + rq(-min(beta, sy), beta, 64)}), eps))
        sup.append(supp_act(lw, {'i': si, 'y': sy}, 'i'))
    kind, f = pop_fraction(sum(ys0) / N, sum(ys1) / N, F(0), eps, b, M1)
    tot += 1
    if kind == 'incompatible':
        inc += 1
    if kind == 'bound':
        nb += 1
        viol += (F(sum(sup), N) < f)
say(f'C11d random (in-model readings): {tot} populations, nontrivial lower bound given in {nb}, violations with the bound above the true support fraction {viol}; false reports of incompatibility {inc}')
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C12 constant margin
viol = 0; dom_fail = 0; dom_b0 = 0; tot = 0; gain = 0
for _ in range(20000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4)); beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 2)); L = rq(F(0), F(1)); b = L * beta
    r = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    sy = rq(F(0), F(2)); si = rq(eps + F(1, 16), F(2))
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        lw = ('lin', rq(-L, L), ('y',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))      # H0
    else:
        lw = ('lin', rq(F(-2), F(2)), ('i',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    c0 = value(lw, {'i': si, 'y': sy})
    c1 = value(lw, {'i': si / 2, 'y': sy + rq(-min(beta, sy), beta, 64)})
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64); y1 = hat(c1, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    d = diff_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b); R = rlc_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b)
    tot += 1
    if not supp_act(lw, {'i': si, 'y': sy}, 'i') and d == 1:
        viol += 1
    if d == 1 and R != 1:
        if b > 0:
            dom_fail += 1
        else:
            dom_b0 += 1
    if R == 1 and d != 1:
        gain += 1
eps, b, r = F(1, 10), F(1, 20), F(1, 40)
y0 = F(1); y1 = y0 + b + 2 * r + eps / 2 + 2 * r
~~~

say(f'C12 constant margin eps + b + 2r: of {tot} cases, {viol} H0 violations giving 1; with b \> 0, diff\_rule gives 1 while RLC does not in {dom\_fail} cases (RLC dominates it); with b = 0, in {dom\_b0} cases (all boundary cases in which the compatible sets meet only at the open endpoints ±eps, see main text 5.2); RLC gives 1 while the constant margin does not in {gain} cases; '  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;f'example: eps = 1/10, b = 1/20, r = 1/40, readings 1 → {fmt(y1)}: diff\_rule = {diff\_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b)}, RLC = {rlc\_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b)}')  
say('Verification finished.')  
A.8  run\_channel.py  Run entry: finite verification of statistical composition, valid-domain boundaries, populations, same-beat gating, comparative observation families and read-outs (5.1, 5.3–5.6, 5.8, 5.9)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization: finite verifications D1–D7 of statistical composition, valid-domain boundaries, populations, same-beat gating, comparative observation families and read-outs
(random seed 20261104; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text). D1e includes the exact xi = 0 branch; D2a uses b_bil_avail (available domain of the tested position);
D2b, D2b′ and D2c use exact solvers with true compatible sets and strict inequalities (t13_exact), and every witness is substituted back with the true hat; D5a lists “deletion from the window entry” and “deletion from the start” separately;
D5c includes a positive control that is certifiable despite saturation; D4c is restricted to the information domain in which only g is read.
D1 Statistics and composition: union bound for composite atoms (shared readings), nested radius for within-cluster correlation, joint compatible set for a shared channel, adaptive selection, replica state discrepancy (REP-xi)
D2 Valid-domain boundaries: soundness and maximality of the valid-domain supremum for bilinear laws; reachability adjudication for the I_full structural family (soundness, completeness on the grid, gain over I_pair, corroboration channel); successor-in-box variant
D3 Population read-back: universal read-back for bilinear populations (mean criterion) and support fraction; tightness of the fraction lower bound after rounding; spot-checked availability fraction (exact coverage for sampling without replacement)
D4 𝒦_g same-beat gating channel: gating-input reading certificate, robust threshold-crossing certificate, uncertifiable twin when only firing is read, correspondence with the exact criterion of Chapter 11 when Λ_g = 0
D5 Comparative observation families and transfer: lineage-type removal, law rewriting, model-mediated readings; M2 transfer conditions for Act and for bilinear support
D6 Target interface: EPSC readings and process components (non-identifiability twin, identification under SAM); local-response certificate and sign-definiteness antecedent for Lipschitz laws (MWC type)
"""
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import random
from fractions import Fraction as F
from math import comb
from t13_core import (value, hat, supp_act, consistent, gap, dist_sets, rlc_rule, legal_side_interval, rho_legal,
                      b_legal, rlc_legal, hoeffding_radius, mean_dist_two_point, lipschitz_on, act_rule)
from t13_ext import (fm_feasible, nested_radius, joint_channel_rule, b_bil_legal, twin_bil, ifull_feasible,
                      sh_feasible, gate_input_cert, robust_crossing_cert, mediated_preimage, pieces_with_slope)
from t13_exact import (true_pieces, in_true, fm_strict, ifull_exact, verify_ifull, sh_exact, verify_sh, b_bil_avail)
rng = random.Random(20261104)

def rq(lo, hi, den=8):
    return F(rng.randint(int(lo * den), int(hi * den)), den)

def fmt(q):
    return str(q.numerator) if q.denominator == 1 else f'{q.numerator}/{q.denominator}'

def say(x=''):
    print(x)

def member_pair(y0, y1, r, eps, b):
    """Find a pair of true members in I(y0)×I(y1) with |c1 - c0| ≤ b (nearest points and inward shifts); return None if there is none."""
    mem = lambda c, y: y - r <= hat(c, eps) <= y + r
    for P in consistent(y0, r, eps):
        for Q in consistent(y1, r, eps):
            g = gap(P, Q)
            if g > b:
                continue
            if P[1] < Q[0]:
                a0, a1 = P[1], Q[0]
            elif Q[1] < P[0]:
                a0, a1 = P[0], Q[1]
            else:
                a0 = a1 = (max(P[0], Q[0]) + min(P[1], Q[1])) / 2
            h = (b - g) / 4
            h0, h1 = min(h, (P[1] - P[0]) / 2), min(h, (Q[1] - Q[0]) / 2)
            for u in ([F(0)] if h0 == 0 else [F(0), h0, -h0]):
                for w in ([F(0)] if h1 == 0 else [F(0), h1, -h1]):
                    p0, p1 = a0 + u, a1 + w
                    if P[0] <= p0 <= P[1] and Q[0] <= p1 <= Q[1] and mem(p0, y0) and mem(p1, y1) and abs(p1 - p0) <= b:
                        return p0, p1
    return None

# ================================================================ D1
say('=' * 78)
say('D1  Statistics and composition: multi-beat atom combination, correlated readings, adaptive selection')
say('=' * 78)
# D1a union bound: the Act and support-core tests share the baseline reading (dependent); each gets alpha/2
alpha = F(1, 20)
n = 12
tau = F(1, 10)
dist_e = mean_dist_two_point(n, tau)
t_half = hoeffding_radius(n, tau, alpha / 4)        # alpha/4 per mean (the support-core test uses two means); the Act test uses the baseline mean
# null-hypothesis instance: Act = 1 (component 1/2), support core = 0 (reads y, side effect 0), b = 0
p_joint_false = F(0)
p_hc_false = F(0)
for e0, p0 in dist_e.items():
    for e1, p1 in dist_e.items():
        y0, y1 = F(1, 2) + e0, F(1, 2) + e1
        hc = rlc_rule(y0, y1, t_half, F(1, 100), F(0))
        act = act_rule(y0, F(0), t_half)
        if hc == 1 and act == 1:
            p_joint_false += p0 * p1
        if hc == 1:
            p_hc_false += p0 * p1
say(f'D1a composite HDyn certificate (Act and support core share the baseline mean, n = {n}, two-point error ±1/10): P(composite misjudged as 1) = {fmt(p_joint_false)} ≤ P(support core misjudged as 1) = {fmt(p_hc_false)} ≤ alpha/2 = {fmt(alpha / 2)}')
# D1b nested radius and its misuse
tau_b, tau_e = F(1, 20), F(1, 20)
for G, m in ((3, 100), (5, 20), (10, 10)):
    nn = G * m
    t_nest = nested_radius(G, nn, tau_b, tau_e, alpha)
    t_naive = hoeffding_radius(nn, tau_b + tau_e, alpha)
    # exact distributions: cluster mean (1/G)Σ ±tau_b, unit mean (1/n)Σ ±tau_e
    pb = {tau_b * F(2 * k - G, G): F(comb(G, k), 2 ** G) for k in range(G + 1)}
    pe = {tau_e * F(2 * k - nn, nn): F(comb(nn, k), 2 ** nn) for k in range(nn + 1)}
    P_nest = sum(x * y for bb, x in pb.items() for ee, y in pe.items() if abs(bb + ee) >= t_nest)
    P_naive = sum(x * y for bb, x in pb.items() for ee, y in pe.items() if abs(bb + ee) >= t_naive)
    say(f'D1b G = {G} clusters × {m} units: nested radius {float(t_nest):.5f}, P(|error| ≥ t) = {float(P_nest):.3e} ≤ alpha: {P_nest <= alpha}; '
        f'{nn} units wrongly treated as independent: radius {float(t_naive):.5f}, P = {float(P_naive):.4f} (> alpha: {P_naive > alpha})')
# D1c joint compatible set for a shared channel
viol = gain = tot = 0
ex = None
for _ in range(4000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    L, beta = F(1, 2), rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    b = L * beta
    r = rq(F(0), F(1, 16))
    tch = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    sy = rq(F(0), F(2))
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        law = ('lin', rq(-L, L), ('y',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
        h0 = True
    else:
        law = ('lin', rq(F(-1), F(1)), ('i',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
        h0 = False
    c0 = value(law, {'i': F(1), 'y': sy})
    c1 = value(law, {'i': F(1, 4), 'y': sy + rq(-min(beta, sy), beta, 64)})
    o = rq(-tch, tch, 64)
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + o + rq(-r, r, 64)
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) + o + rq(-r, r, 64)
    v, _ = joint_channel_rule(y0, y1, r, tch, eps, b)
    vs = rlc_rule(y0, y1, r + tch, eps, b)
    tot += 1
    if h0 and v == 1:
        viol += 1
    if v == 1 and vs != 1:
        gain += 1
        ex = ex or (y0, y1, r, tch, eps, b)
say(f'D1c shared channel (the same offset enters both readings): {tot} cases, H0 verdict-1 violations {viol}; gain where the joint compatible set gives 1 while separate radii r + tau_ch give ⊥: {gain} cases'
    + (f'; one case: y = {fmt(ex[0])} → {fmt(ex[1])}, r = {fmt(ex[2])}, tau_ch = {fmt(ex[3])}, b = {fmt(ex[5])}' if ex else ''))
# D1d adaptive selection: a single test is calibrated with exact quantiles; the maximum of K candidates is taken without correction
K, n, tau = 5, 20, F(1, 10)
dist_e = mean_dist_two_point(n, tau)

def exact_thr(a):
    vals = sorted({abs(e) for e in dist_e}, reverse=True)
    thr = None
    for v in vals:
        if sum(p for e, p in dist_e.items() if abs(e) >= v) <= a:
            thr = v
        else:
            break
    return thr

t1 = exact_thr(alpha)
p1 = sum(p for e, p in dist_e.items() if abs(e) >= t1)
pK = 1 - (1 - p1) ** K
tK = exact_thr(alpha / K)
pKc = 1 - (1 - sum(p for e, p in dist_e.items() if abs(e) >= tK)) ** K
~~~

say(f'D1d adaptive selection ({K} independent null-hypothesis candidates, each n = {n}): the exact single-test threshold gives P = {float(p1):.4f} ≤ alpha; taking the maximum without correction gives P(misjudged as 1) = {float(pK):.4f} \> alpha: {pK \> alpha}; '

~~~text
    f'after Bonferroni (alpha/{K}) P = {float(pKc):.4f} ≤ alpha: {pKc <= alpha}. The certificate of the bounded reading has zero misjudgment under any selection rule (Proposition D-4(iii))')
# D1e replica state discrepancy REP-xi: read Act on a replica, read back the reference state (xi = 0 uses the exact branch 13-A)
viol = tot = n_xi0 = eq_xi0 = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    xi = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    gamma, r = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)), rq(F(0), F(1, 16))
    k = rq(F(-1), F(1))
    reads_ = tuple(rng.choice(['p', 'q']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3)))
    law = ('lin', k, reads_, rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    Lam = abs(k) * len(reads_)
    sref = {'p': rq(F(0), F(2)), 'q': rq(F(0), F(2))}
    su = {p: max(F(0), sref[p] + rq(-xi, xi, 64)) for p in sref}
    D = hat(value(law, su), eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    rule = (1 if abs(D) > gamma + r else None) if xi == 0 else (1 if abs(D) > eps + gamma + r + Lam * xi else None)
    tot += 1
    if rule == 1 and not abs(value(law, sref)) > eps:
        viol += 1
    if xi == 0:
        n_xi0 += 1
        eq_xi0 += (rule == act_rule(D, gamma, r))
say(f'D1e REP-xi (xi > 0 uses the conservative sufficient condition |D| > eps + gamma + r + Λ·xi; xi = 0 uses 13-A): {tot} cases, violations with reference-state Act = 0 but verdict 1: {viol}; the xi = 0 branch agrees with 13-A value by value {eq_xi0}/{n_xi0}')

# D2a bilinear
viol = n_h0 = n_bot = n_tw = n_one = n_nomp = n_notw = 0
P = ['i', 'j1', 'j2', 'o']
for _ in range(6000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    hi = {p: rq(F(1, 2), F(2)) for p in P}
    s = {}
    for p in P:
        u = rng.random()
        s[p] = F(0) if u < 0.25 else (hi[p] if u < 0.4 else rq(F(0), hi[p], 16))
    if s['i'] <= eps:
        s['i'] = min(hi['i'], eps + F(1, 8))
        if s['i'] <= eps:
            continue
    Ni = tuple(p for p in ('j1', 'j2') if rng.random() < 0.7)
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1))
    Kc = rq(F(0), F(2))
    gamma, tau_ = (rq(F(0), F(1, 16)), rq(F(0), F(1, 16))) if rng.random() < 0.5 else (F(0), F(0))
    r = gamma + tau_
    delta = rq(-s['i'], hi['i'] - s['i'], 16)
    zeta = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    dlo, dhi = max(-s['i'], delta - zeta), min(hi['i'] - s['i'], delta + zeta)
    dprime_max = s['i'] + dhi
    bb = b_bil_avail(s, 'i', Ni, beta, hi, Kc, P, dprime_max, eps)
    assert bb is not None            # the generator guarantees s_i > eps (the theorem domain of D-5)
    # true law: bilinear, random read pair
    j1, j2 = rng.choice(P), rng.choice(P)
    law = ('bil', rq(-Kc, Kc, 64), (j1, j2), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    dp = rq(dlo, dhi, 64) if dlo <= dhi else delta
    s1 = dict(s); s1['i'] = s['i'] + dp
    for j in Ni:
        lo, up = legal_side_interval(s[j], beta, hi[j])
        s1[j] = s[j] + rng.choice([lo, up, lo + (up - lo) * F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)])
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1)
    y0, y1 = hat(c0, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64), hat(c1, eps) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    v = rlc_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, bb)
    if not supp_act(law, s, 'i'):
        n_h0 += 1
        viol += (v == 1)
    if v == 1:
        n_one += 1
        continue
    n_bot += 1
    mp = member_pair(y0, y1, r, eps, bb)
    if mp is None:
        n_nomp += 1
        continue
    tw = twin_bil(mp[0], mp[1], s, 'i', Ni, beta, hi, Kc, P, dprime_max)
    if tw is None:
        n_notw += 1
        continue
    lawt, etas, si_set = tw
    sB = dict(s)
    sB['i'] = si_set if si_set is not None else s['i'] + (dlo if dlo <= dhi else delta)
    ok = True
    for j, e in etas.items():
        lo, up = legal_side_interval(s[j], beta, hi[j])
        ok &= (lo <= e <= up) and (j in Ni or e == 0)
        sB[j] = s[j] + e
    ok &= all(F(0) <= sB[p] <= hi[p] for p in P)
    ok &= (lawt[0] == 'const' or abs(lawt[1]) <= Kc)
    ok &= not supp_act(lawt, s, 'i')
    ok &= (y0 - r <= hat(value(lawt, s), eps) <= y0 + r) and (y1 - r <= hat(value(lawt, sB), eps) <= y1 + r)
    n_tw += ok
say(f'D2a bilinear (with intercept, |k| ≤ K, valid domain, tested position available, s_i > eps): H0 {n_h0} cases, violations of RLC verdict 1 with budget b_bil_avail {n_h0 and viol}; '
    f'RLC verdict ⊥ {n_bot} cases, legitimate bilinear twin constructed in {n_tw} cases (no true member pair found {n_nomp}, construction failed {n_notw}); verdict 1 {n_one} cases')
# D2b I_full structural family (true compatible sets, strict inequalities, back-substitution of every witness)
viol = tot = cert = gain = wit_ok = wit_tot = grid_miss = grid_tot = clos_cons = 0
for _ in range(1500):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    hi_y = rq(F(1, 2), F(2))
    s_y = rq(F(0), hi_y, 16)
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    Ey = legal_side_interval(s_y, beta, hi_y)
    L = F(0) if rng.random() < 0.1 else rq(F(0), F(2))
    r, rR = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)), (F(0) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(F(0), F(1, 8)))
    s_g = rq(F(0), F(1))
    R = (rq(F(-1), F(1)), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    cR0 = hat(R[0] + R[1] * s_y, eps)
    S_base = s_g + cR0
    h0 = rng.random() < 0.5
    if h0:
        law = ('lin', rq(-L, L, 64) if L > 0 else F(0), ('y',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
    else:
        law = ('lin', rq(F(-1), F(1)), ('i', 'y') if rng.random() < 0.3 else ('i',), rq(F(-1), F(1)))
        if 'y' in law[2] and abs(law[1]) > L:
            law = ('lin', law[1], ('i',), law[3])
    s = {'i': F(1), 'y': s_y}
    eta = rng.choice([Ey[0], Ey[1], F(0), Ey[0] + (Ey[1] - Ey[0]) * F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)])
    s1 = {'i': F(1, 2), 'y': s_y + eta}
    c0, c1 = value(law, s), value(law, s1)
    cR1 = hat(R[0] + R[1] * (s_y + eta), eps)
    if S_base + hat(c0, eps) < 0 or s_g + cR1 + hat(c1, eps) < 0:
        continue
    # raise the share of readings at the threshold so that open endpoints are covered
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + (rng.choice([-r, r]) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(-r, r, 64))
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) + (rng.choice([-r, r]) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(-r, r, 64))
    yR1 = cR1 + rq(-rR, rR, 64)
    args = (y0, y1, r, eps, L, s_y, Ey, R, yR1, rR, S_base, s_g)
    feas, w = ifull_exact(*args)
    feas_cl, _wc = ifull_feasible(*args)
    tot += 1
    if feas_cl and not feas:
        clos_cons += 1
    if h0 and not feas:
        viol += 1
    if not feas:
        cert += 1
        if rlc_legal(y0, y1, r, eps, L, rho_legal({'y': s_y}, ('y',), beta, {'y': hi_y}), F(0)) != 1:
            gain += 1
        grid_tot += 1
        found = False
        for te in range(9):
            e = Ey[0] + (Ey[1] - Ey[0]) * F(te, 8)
            cRe = hat(R[0] + R[1] * (s_y + e), eps)
            if not (yR1 - rR <= cRe <= yR1 + rR):
                continue
            for Pc0 in true_pieces(y0, r, eps):
                for Pc1 in true_pieces(y1, r, eps):
                    for t0 in range(1, 8):
                        for t1 in range(1, 8):
                            a = Pc0[0] + (Pc0[1] - Pc0[0]) * F(t0, 8)
                            bq = Pc1[0] + (Pc1[1] - Pc1[0]) * F(t1, 8)
                            okL = (abs(bq - a) <= L * abs(e)) if L is not None else (e != 0 or a == bq)
                            if okL and in_true(a, y0, r, eps) and in_true(bq, y1, r, eps) \
                                    and S_base + hat(a, eps) >= 0 and s_g + cRe + hat(bq, eps) >= 0:
                                found = True
        grid_miss += found
    else:
        wit_tot += 1
        wit_ok += verify_ifull(w, *args)
say(f'D2b I_full structural family (exact solver: true compatible sets, strict inequalities): {tot} cases; H0 adjudicated as having no twin (misjudged as 1) {viol}; verdict 1 {cert} cases, '
    f'of which gain where the I_pair valid-domain budget gives ⊥: {gain} cases; grid completeness check of the verdict-1 cases, misses {grid_miss}/{grid_tot}; '
    f'in the verdict-⊥ cases, back-substituting the witness with the true hat satisfies all observation and successor constraints {wit_ok}/{wit_tot}; the closure-conservative algorithm reports a twin while the exact adjudication finds none: {clos_cons} cases')
~~~

~~~python
# D2c successor in box (I_pair, subclass tgt ∉ N_i; exact solver)
def plain_exact(y0, y1, r, eps, b):
    for Pc0 in true_pieces(y0, r, eps):
        for Pc1 in true_pieces(y1, r, eps):
            cons = [({'c0': F(1)}, Pc0[1], Pc0[3]), ({'c0': F(-1)}, -Pc0[0], Pc0[2]), ({'c1': F(1)}, Pc1[1], Pc1[3]), ({'c1': F(-1)}, -Pc1[0], Pc1[2]),
                    ({'c1': F(1), 'c0': F(-1)}, b, False), ({'c1': F(-1), 'c0': F(1)}, b, False)]
            if fm_strict(cons, ['c1', 'c0'])[0]:
                return True
    return False

viol = tot = binds = eqv_bad = eqv_tot = wit_ok = wit_tot = 0
for _ in range(4000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    b = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    r = rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    H = rq(F(1, 8), F(2))
    dlo = rq(F(-1, 2), F(0)); dhi = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        dlo = dhi = F(0)
    c0 = rq(F(-1), F(1)); c1 = c0 + rq(-b, b, 64)
    y0 = hat(c0, eps) + (rng.choice([-r, r]) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(-r, r, 64))
    y1 = hat(c1, eps) + (rng.choice([-r, r]) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(-r, r, 64))
    d = rq(dlo, dhi, 64) if dlo < dhi else dlo
    A_ok = abs(d + hat(c1, eps) - hat(c0, eps)) <= H
    feas, w = sh_exact(y0, y1, r, eps, b, dlo, dhi, H)
    tot += 1
    if feas:
        wit_tot += 1
        wit_ok += verify_sh(w, y0, y1, r, eps, b, dlo, dhi, H)
    if A_ok and not feas:
        viol += 1
    plain = plain_exact(y0, y1, r, eps, b)
    if plain and not feas:
        binds += 1
    if max(abs(dlo), abs(dhi)) + b + eps <= H:
        eqv_tot += 1
        eqv_bad += (plain != feas)
say(f'D2c successor in box (I_pair plus constant background, tgt ∉ N_i; exact solver): {tot} cases; H0 instances adjudicated as having no twin {viol}; witness back-substitution holds {wit_ok}/{wit_tot}; '
    f'SH makes the twin nonexistent while it exists without the constraint: {binds} cases; under the sufficient condition |d| + b + eps ≤ H, disagreements with the unconstrained exact adjudication {eqv_bad}/{eqv_tot}')
~~~

say(f'D3a universal read-back for bilinear populations (structurally homogeneous; with 0 ∉ Theta, Supp\_n ⇔ s\_n(j) \> 0; criterion m̄ - r \> (1 - 1/N)·hi): {tot} cases, verdict 1 {cert}, violations {viol}; '

~~~text
    f'tight case: N = 5, hi = 1, one individual at 0 and the rest at 1, mean {fmt(sum(pop) / N)} = (1 - 1/N)·hi, verdict ⊥, and some individual indeed lacks support')
viol = tot = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    N = rng.randint(1, 8)
    hi = rq(F(1, 2), F(2))
    sj = [F(0) if rng.random() < 0.3 else rq(F(0), hi, 16) for _n in range(N)]
    r = rq(F(0), F(1, 16))
    mbar = sum(sj) / N + rq(-r, r, 64)
    flow = max(F(0), (mbar - r) / hi)
    tot += 1
    viol += (F(sum(1 for x in sj if x > 0), N) < flow)
say(f'D3b support-fraction lower bound (m̄ - r)/hi from the state mean (structurally homogeneous): {tot} cases, violations {viol}')
bad = tot = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    N = rng.randint(1, 10)
    B0 = rq(F(0), F(1, 2)); M1 = B0 + rq(F(1, 16), F(2), 16)
    d = B0 + (M1 - B0) * F(rng.randint(1, 100), 100)
    fstar = (d - B0) / (M1 - B0)
    k = -((-fstar * N).numerator // (-fstar * N).denominator) if False else -(-(fstar * N).numerator // (fstar * N).denominator)
    k = max(1, min(N, k))
    Mp = (N * d - (N - k) * B0) / k          # common difference of the supporting individuals
    ok = (B0 <= Mp <= M1 or Mp <= M1) and ((k * Mp + (N - k) * B0) / N == d)
    if k > 1:
        ok &= ((k - 1) * M1 + (N - k + 1) * B0) / N < d
    tot += 1
    bad += not ok
say(f'D3c tightness of the rounded fraction lower bound ⌈N·f*⌉/N (in the constructible family whose differences range over [0, M1] and B0): {tot} cases construct a population with exactly that number of supporting individuals and mean difference exactly d; '
    f'with one supporting individual fewer, d is not reached; failures {bad}')
# D3d spot-checked availability fraction: sampling without replacement, Hoeffding (1963, Theorem 4)
worst = F(0)
for (Npop, A, nsamp) in ((40, 30, 10), (40, 20, 12), (100, 70, 20)):
    a_s = F(1, 20)
    t = hoeffding_radius(nsamp, F(1, 2), a_s)    # indicator ∈ {0,1}: support width 1, take tau = 1/2
    p = F(0)
    for x in range(max(0, nsamp - (Npop - A)), min(A, nsamp) + 1):
        pr = F(comb(A, x) * comb(Npop - A, nsamp - x), comb(Npop, nsamp))
        if F(x, nsamp) - t > F(A, Npop):
            p += pr
    worst = max(worst, p)
    say(f'D3d spot-checked availability fraction: population {Npop}, available {A}, sampled {nsamp}; exact probability that the lower bound â - t (t = {float(t):.4f}) exceeds the true fraction {float(p):.3e} ≤ 1/20: {p <= a_s}')
# ================================================================ D4
say()
say('=' * 78)
say('D4  𝒦_g same-beat gating channel')
say('=' * 78)
viol1 = viol2 = tot = cert2 = 0
twin_ok = twin_tot = one_sided = 0
corr_bad = corr_tot = 0
for _ in range(6000):
    nin = 4
    act = [rng.random() < 0.6 for _k in range(nin)]
    hi_w = F(2)
    w = [rq(F(0), hi_w, 16) for _k in range(nin)]
    lam, v, I, theta = rq(F(0), F(1)), rq(F(-1), F(2)), rq(F(-1), F(1)), rq(F(1, 2), F(3))
    T = [k for k in range(nin) if rng.random() < 0.4]
    Nn = [k for k in range(nin) if k not in T and rng.random() < 0.5]
    side_v = rng.random() < 0.5
    beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 2))
    rv = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)) if rng.random() < 0.7 else F(0)
    Xi = sum((max(abs(x) for x in legal_side_interval(w[k], beta, hi_w)) for k in Nn if act[k]), F(0)) + (lam * beta if side_v else 0)
    vt = lambda ww, vv: lam * vv + sum((ww[k] for k in range(nin) if act[k]), F(0)) + I
    # set T (within [0, hi]); side effects on N (legitimate) and on v
    w1 = list(w)
    for k in T:
        w1[k] = rq(F(0), hi_w, 16)
    w1s = list(w1)
    for k in Nn:
        lo, up = legal_side_interval(w[k], beta, hi_w)
        w1s[k] = w[k] + rng.choice([lo, up, F(0)])
    v1 = v + (rng.choice([-beta, beta, F(0)]) if side_v else F(0))
    vt0, vt1 = vt(w, v), vt(w1s, v1)
    z0, z1 = vt0 + rq(-rv, rv, 64), vt1 + rq(-rv, rv, 64)
    h0 = not any(act[k] for k in T)
    tot += 1
    c1 = gate_input_cert(z0, z1, rv, Xi)
    if h0 and c1 == 1:
        viol1 += 1
    c2 = robust_crossing_cert(z0, z1, rv, Xi, theta)
    if c2 == 1:
        cert2 += 1
        # whether the setting of T alone (no side effects) changes the gate
        g0 = vt(w, v) >= theta
        gT = vt(w1, v) >= theta
        viol2 += (g0 == gT)
    # twin when only firing is read: A flips and Xi > 0 (realizable in some direction)
    g0, g1 = vt0 >= theta, vt1 >= theta
    if g0 != g1 and Xi > 0:
        # twin B: T is not driven (vt0 compensated by injection), baseline vt placed near the threshold, flipped by a legitimate side effect
        need_up = (not g0) and g1
        # pick a read-set node that can move in the required direction
        cand = []
        for k in Nn:
            if act[k]:
                lo, up = legal_side_interval(w[k], beta, hi_w)
                if (up > 0 if need_up else lo < 0):
                    cand.append(up if need_up else lo)
        if side_v and lam * beta > 0:
            cand.append(lam * beta if need_up else -lam * beta)
        if not cand:
            one_sided += 1          # no nonzero legitimate side effect in the required direction (one-sided restriction); the twin does not apply
        if cand:
            twin_tot += 1
            step = max(cand, key=abs)
            vt0B = theta - step / 2 if need_up else theta - step / 2   # need_up: vt0B < theta ≤ vt0B + step
            vt1B = vt0B + step
            gB0, gB1 = vt0B >= theta, vt1B >= theta
            twin_ok += (gB0 == g0 and gB1 == g1)
    # correspondence with the exact criterion when Λ_g = 0
    if Xi == 0 and rv == 0:
        corr_tot += 1
        exact_dep = sum((w1[k] - w[k] for k in T if act[k]), F(0)) != 0
        corr_bad += ((c1 == 1) != exact_dep)
say(f'D4a gating-input reading certificate (Proposition G-1): {tot} cases, H0 (T not in gr_j) verdict-1 violations {viol1}')

say(f'D4d with Λ_g = 0 (Xi = 0, rv = 0), disagreements of G-1 with the exact adjudication “the joint setting changes vt (sum of the changes at the T positions in gr_j ≠ 0)” {corr_bad}/{corr_tot}')
# ================================================================ D5

bad_w = bad_h = tot = pre_only = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    Hn, Wn = rng.randint(0, 3), rng.randint(1, 4)       # Hn beats of pre-window history, Wn beats within the window
    laws = {f'p{q}': ('lin', rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2)), tuple(rng.choice(['a', 'b']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 2))), rq(F(0), F(1, 2)))
            for q in range(3)}
    tg = {nm: rng.choice(['a', 'b']) for nm in laws}
    runs_m = {t: (rng.random() < 0.7) for t in range(Hn + Wn)}   # whether m runs at this beat (alphabet gating)
    def run(skip_from):
        st = {'a': F(1), 'b': F(1)}
        eff_hist, eff_win = False, False
        for t in range(Hn + Wn):
            inc = {'a': F(0), 'b': F(0)}
            for nm, lw in laws.items():
                if nm == 'p0' and not runs_m[t]:
                    continue
                c = hat(value(lw, st), eps)
                if nm == 'p0':
                    if c != 0:
                        eff_hist = True
                        if t >= Hn:
                            eff_win = True
                    if skip_from is not None and t >= skip_from:
                        continue
                inc[tg[nm]] += c
            st = {p: st[p] + inc[p] for p in st}
        return st, eff_hist, eff_win
    sA, eh, ew = run(None)
    sW, _, _ = run(Hn)          # deletion from the window entry (common prefix)
    sO, _, _ = run(0)           # deletion from the start (lineage type)
    tot += 1
    if not ew and sA != sW:
        bad_w += 1
    if not eh and sA != sO:
        bad_h += 1
    if (not ew) and sA != sO:
        pre_only += 1
say(f'D5a deletion comparison (Proposition C-1): {tot} random F runs. Deletion from the window entry: violations “m never effective within the window yet difference ≠ 0” {bad_w}; '
~~~

~~~python
# D5c model-mediated readings
phi = [(F(0), F(1), F(0), F(2)), (F(1), F(2), F(2), F(3)), (F(2), F(4), F(3), F(3))]   # monotone, saturating on [2, 4]

def phi_of(x):
    for x0, x1, y0, y1 in phi:
        if x0 <= x <= x1:
            return y0 + (y1 - y0) * (x - x0) / (x1 - x0)
viol = tot = 0

    th0 = rq(F(0), F(4), 16)
    th1 = th0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else rq(F(0), F(4), 16)
    z0 = phi_of(th0) + rq(-zp, zp, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    z1 = phi_of(th1) + rq(-zp, zp, 64) + rq(-tau, tau, 64)
    pre0 = mediated_preimage(z0, tau, zp, phi)
    pre1 = mediated_preimage(z1, tau, zp, phi)
    inside = any(a <= th0 <= b_ for a, b_ in pre0) and any(a <= th1 <= b_ for a, b_ in pre1)
    certify = (pre0 and pre1 and dist_sets(pre0, pre1) > 0)
    tot += 1
    viol += (not inside) or (certify and th0 == th1)
say(f'D5c model-mediated readings (monotone piecewise-linear phi, saturating on [2, 4]): {tot} cases, violations where the true value lies outside the preimage compatible set or “change adjudicated while the true value is unchanged” {viol}')
~~~

~~~text
# D5d M2 transfer
viol = tot = 0
for _ in range(5000):
    eps = rq(F(0), F(1, 4))
    tb, tk, ts = rq(F(0), F(1, 8)), rq(F(0), F(1, 8)), rq(F(0), F(1, 8))
    hi = F(2)
    reads_ = tuple(rng.choice(['p', 'q']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 2)))
    k, b0 = rq(F(-1), F(1)), rq(F(-1), F(1))
    ks, bs = k + rq(-tk, tk, 64), b0 + rq(-tb, tb, 64)
    s = {'p': rq(F(0), hi), 'q': rq(F(0), hi)}
    sst = {p: min(hi, max(F(0), s[p] + rq(-ts, ts, 64))) for p in s}
    gamma, r = rq(F(0), F(1, 16)), rq(F(0), F(1, 16))
    c = value(('lin', k, reads_, b0), s)
    D = hat(c, eps) - rq(-gamma, gamma, 64) + rq(-r, r, 64)
    c_low = abs(D) - gamma - r
    Dtr = tb + tk * len(reads_) * hi + abs(k) * len(reads_) * ts
    rule = 1 if (hat(c, eps) != 0 or abs(D) > gamma + r) and c_low - Dtr > eps and abs(D) > gamma + r else None
    tot += 1
    if rule == 1 and not abs(value(('lin', ks, reads_, bs), sst)) > eps:
        viol += 1
say(f'D5d Act transfer condition of M2 (law tolerances tb, tk; state tolerance ts; transfer margin Δ_tr = tb + tk·n·hi + |k|·n·ts): {tot} cases, violations where Act = 0 at the target yet transfer is adjudicated {viol}')
~~~

~~~text
    v = rlc_rule(y0, y1, r, eps, b)
    tot += 1
    viol += (h0 and v == 1)
~~~
