A.9  run\_multitick.py  Run entry: finite verification of extension-class closure, M-1, M-2 and F-1 (3.6, 5.7, 8.1)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization: finite verifications N0–N6 of the multi-beat gating channel (random seed 20261106; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text).
N0 Four boundary witnesses: the recursion lacking the plastic switch, same-beat mutual input, twin with repeated use of the same label, RESET component type
N1 Soundness of Proposition M-1: verdict 1 ⇒ the run with only T set (no side effects) does change e; four classes (𝒦_g, third factor, 𝒦_PA^δ, 𝒦_PA^≺) ×
   four plastic/bap combinations × ρ ∈ {0, 1, other}, all event kinds; every false change caused by side effects is rejected; Lemma R1′ checked beat by beat
N2 The propagation bound itself: when the margin holds, the difference between the two runs at every beat and every node is ≤ D(t), and every event-component difference is ≤ B_e; targeted table of switch coefficients (plastic/bap × ρ = 0, 1)
N3 Zero-channel correspondence: with Λ = 0, M-1 verdict 1 ⟺ the realized σ′ changes e, covering all kinds of Definition 5 (including the RESET component type); not equivalent to Theorem D1
N4 Closure of the extension classes: in 𝒦_PA^≺ the simultaneous equations of every beat have exactly one solution, which agrees with the sequential computation and does not depend on the choice of topological order; same-beat cycles produce no solution or multiple solutions
N5 Proposition F-1 (main version 𝒦_PA^δ and version 𝒦_PA^≺): FD witness, quantitative prediction of the return component, T changes neither the DAN nor its gated events when the return is blocked, intervention bound κ*
N6 Proposition M-2: globally fixed pat_B, repeated use of the same label, single flips in both directions; per-model check of pat_B consistency and of the complete visible record on [0, t_e]
"""
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import random
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_gate import (run, step, validate, r1_check, simultaneous_solutions, prop_bounds, prop_bounds_no_switch,
                      event_bound, m1_certificate, beta_star, changed, m2_twin, copy_state)
rng = random.Random(20261106)
ZERO = F(0)

def rq(lo, hi, den=8):
    return F(rng.randint(int(lo * den), int(hi * den)), den)

def say(x=''):
    print(x)

def zeros_D(nin, nout):
    return ([[ZERO] * nout for _ in range(nin)], [ZERO] * nout, [ZERO] * nin, [ZERO] * nout, [0] * nout)

# ───────────────────────── N0 boundary witnesses ─────────────────────────
say('N0 boundary witnesses')
# control for the recursion lacking the switch: two inputs, one output, T = {w00}, non-T position w10 = 1/2, λ = 0, θ = 3/5, ρ = 1, plastic = False, A± = 0
P = {'lam': [F(0)], 'theta': [F(3, 5)], 'vres': [F(0)], 'dx': F(1), 'dy': F(1), 'Ap': F(0), 'Am': F(0), 'rho': F(1),
     'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': False, 'bap': True, 'b': [[F(0)], [F(0)]], 'gate_of': [0], 'mode': 'g'}
st = ([[F(1)], [F(1, 2)]], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [0])
seq = [([0, 0], [F(0)]), ([0, 1], [F(0)])]
act = run(P, st, seq)
obs = run(P, st, seq, 0, {(0, 0): F(3, 2)}, {('w', 1, 0): F(1, 5)})
pure = run(P, st, seq, 0, {(0, 0): F(3, 2)}, {})
z = {t: obs[t]['vt'] for t in range(2)}
g = {t: obs[t]['g'] for t in range(2)}
D0 = ([[F(0)], [F(1, 5)]], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [0])
e = ('YIN', 0)
new = m1_certificate(P, seq, 0, 1, z, ZERO, g, D0, e, obs[1]['ev'][e], act[1]['ev'][e])
old = m1_certificate(P, seq, 0, 1, z, ZERO, g, D0, e, obs[1]['ev'][e], act[1]['ev'][e], bounds=prop_bounds_no_switch)
~~~

say(f'  control for the recursion lacking the switch: setting experiment vt(1) = {obs\[1\]\["vt"\]\[0\]}, firing {obs\[1\]\["g"\]\[0\]}; with only T set, firing {pure\[1\]\["g"\]\[0\]} (the change is due entirely to side effects); '

~~~text
    f'verdict of the recursion with the switch {new[0]} (reject), verdict of the recursion lacking the switch {old[0]} (misjudged as 1)')
assert new[0] is None and old[0] == 1 and obs[1]['g'][0] and not pure[1]['g'][0]
Dvt, Dst = prop_bounds(P, seq, 0, 1, g, D0)
say(f'    recursion with the switch, D_w10 beat 0 → beat 1: {Dst[0][0][1][0]} → {Dst[1][0][1][0]} (plastic off, coefficient 1); beat-1 margin 7/10 − {Dvt[1][0]} < 3/5')
# same-beat mutual input
Pm = {'lam': [F(0), F(0)], 'theta': [F(1, 2), F(1, 2)], 'vres': [F(0)] * 2, 'dx': F(0), 'dy': F(0), 'Ap': F(0), 'Am': F(0),
      'rho': F(0), 'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': False, 'bap': False, 'b': [[F(0)] * 2], 'gate_of': [0, 1],
      'U': [[F(0), F(1)], [F(1), F(0)]]}
st2 = ([[F(0), F(0)]], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [0, 0])
sols = simultaneous_solutions(Pm, st2, [0], [F(0), F(0)])
ok_dag = validate({**Pm, 'mode': 'dag', 'order': [0, 1]}, 1, 2)
Pmd = {**Pm, 'mode': 'delay'}
r_a = run(Pmd, st2, [([0], [F(0), F(0)])] * 3)
st2b = ([[F(0), F(0)]], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [1, 1])
r_b = run(Pmd, st2b, [([0], [F(0), F(0)])] * 3)
~~~

say(f'  same-beat mutual input: solutions of the simultaneous equations vt1 = g2, vt2 = g1, θ = 1/2: {sols} (not unique; outside the working domain of this paper); 𝒦\_PA^≺ class check {ok\_dag}; '

~~~text

# ───────────────────────── random models ─────────────────────────
ALLC = [(False, False), (False, True), (True, False), (True, True)]

def rand_model(nin, nout, cls, combo):
    rho_pick = rng.random()
    rho = F(0) if rho_pick < 0.3 else (F(1) if rho_pick < 0.6 else rq(F(1, 8), F(7, 8)))
    def unit():
        p = rng.random()
        return F(0) if p < 0.15 else (F(1) if p < 0.3 else rq(F(1, 8), F(7, 8)))
    P = {'lam': [unit() for _ in range(nout)], 'theta': [rq(F(1, 2), F(2)) for _ in range(nout)],
         'vres': [rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 4)) for _ in range(nout)], 'dx': [unit() for _ in range(nin)], 'dy': [unit() for _ in range(nout)],
         'Ap': rq(F(0), F(1, 2)), 'Am': rq(F(0), F(1, 2)), 'rho': rho, 'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2),
         'plastic': combo[0], 'bap': combo[1], 'b': [[rq(F(0), F(1, 2))] * nout for _ in range(nin)]}
    P['gate_of'] = list(range(nout))
    P['mode'] = 'g'
    if cls == 'third':
        P['gate_of'] = [rng.randrange(nout) for _ in range(nout)]
        if all(P['gate_of'][j] == j for j in range(nout)):
            P['gate_of'][0] = nout - 1
    if cls in ('delay', 'dag'):
        P['gate_of'] = [rng.randrange(nout) if rng.random() < 0.5 else j for j in range(nout)]
        U = [[ZERO] * nout for _ in range(nout)]
        order = list(range(nout)); rng.shuffle(order)
        pos = {j: n for n, j in enumerate(order)}
        for k in range(nout):
            for j in range(nout):
                if k != j and rng.random() < 0.6:
                    if cls == 'dag' and not pos[k] < pos[j]:
                        continue
                    U[k][j] = rq(F(-1), F(1))
        P['U'] = U
        P['mode'] = cls
        if cls == 'dag':
            P['order'] = order
    assert validate(P, nin, nout)[0], validate(P, nin, nout)
    return P

def all_events(nin, nout, P):
    ev = [('YIN', j) for j in range(nout)] + [('RESET', j) for j in range(nout)] + [('LEAK', j) for j in range(nout)]
    ev += [('YDEC', j) for j in range(nout)] + [('XDEC', i) for i in range(nin)] + [('XIN', i) for i in range(nin)]
    ev += [('INJ', j) for j in range(nout)]
    ev += [(k, i, j) for k in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP', 'SYN') for i in range(nin) for j in range(nout)]
    if P['mode'] in ('delay', 'dag'):
        ev += [('FIN', k, j) for k in range(nout) for j in range(nout) if P['U'][k][j] != 0]
    if P['mode'] == 'delay':
        ev += [('HCOPY', j) for j in range(nout)]
    return ev

def rand_instance(cls, combo, T_len=6, nin=3, nout=None):
    nout = nout or (3 if cls in ('delay', 'dag') else 2)
    P = rand_model(nin, nout, cls, combo)
    seq = [([1 if rng.random() < 0.6 else 0 for _ in range(nin)], [rq(F(-1, 2), F(1)) for _ in range(nout)]) for _ in range(T_len)]
    w0 = [[rq(F(0), F(2), 16) for _ in range(nout)] for _ in range(nin)]
    st0 = (w0, [rq(F(0), F(1)) for _ in range(nout)], [rq(F(0), F(1)) for _ in range(nin)], [rq(F(0), F(1)) for _ in range(nout)],
           [rng.randint(0, 1) for _ in range(nout)])
    return P, seq, st0, nin, nout

def rand_side(P, st_u, nin, nout, Tpos, beta):
    wu, vu, xu, yu, hu = st_u
    Dw = [[ZERO] * nout for _ in range(nin)]
    Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh = [ZERO] * nout, [ZERO] * nin, [ZERO] * nout, [0] * nout
    side = {}
    for i in range(nin):
        for j in range(nout):
            if (i, j) not in Tpos and rng.random() < 0.4:
                lo_, up_ = max(-beta, P['lo'] - wu[i][j]), min(beta, P['hi'] - wu[i][j])
                Dw[i][j] = max(-lo_, up_)
                side[('w', i, j)] = rng.choice([lo_, up_, ZERO])
    for j in range(nout):
        if rng.random() < 0.4:
            Dv[j] = beta
            side[('v', j)] = rng.choice([-beta, beta, ZERO])
        if rng.random() < 0.3:
            Dy[j] = beta
            side[('y', j)] = rng.choice([max(-beta, -yu[j]), beta, ZERO])
        if P['mode'] == 'delay' and rng.random() < 0.1:
            Dh[j] = 1
            side[('h', j)] = rng.choice([0, 1])
    for i in range(nin):
        if rng.random() < 0.3:
            Dx[i] = beta
            side[('x', i)] = rng.choice([max(-beta, -xu[i]), beta, ZERO])
    return (Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh), side

CLASSES = ['g', 'third', 'delay', 'dag']
stats = {}
r1_bad = r1_ticks = 0
bound_checks = bound_bad = 0
ev_bound_checks = ev_bound_bad = 0
corr_tot = corr_bad = 0
corr_kinds = {}
N_PER = 400
for cls in CLASSES:
    for combo in ALLC:
        key = (cls, combo)
        S = stats.setdefault(key, {'n': 0, 'cert': 0, 'viol': 0, 'so': 0, 'so_rej': 0, 'rho0': 0, 'rho1': 0, 'margin': 0})
        for _ in range(N_PER):
            P, seq, st0, nin, nout = rand_instance(cls, combo)
            T_len = len(seq)
            S['rho0'] += P['rho'] == 0
            S['rho1'] += P['rho'] == 1
            u = rng.randint(0, T_len - 2)
            te = rng.randint(u, T_len - 1)
            Tpos = [(i, j) for i in range(nin) for j in range(nout) if rng.random() < 0.3] or [(0, 0)]
            setting = {(i, j): rq(F(1, 4), F(2), 16) for (i, j) in Tpos}
            beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 4)) if rng.random() < 0.8 else ZERO
            rv = rq(F(0), F(1, 16), 64) if rng.random() < 0.7 else ZERO
            r = rq(F(0), F(1, 32), 64) if rng.random() < 0.5 else ZERO
            act = run(P, st0, seq)
            st_u = act[u]['pre']
            D0, side = rand_side(P, st_u, nin, nout, Tpos, beta)
            obs = run(P, st0, seq, u, setting, side)
            tonly = run(P, st0, seq, u, setting, {})
            for rec in act + obs:
                r1_bad += r1_check(P, rec); r1_ticks += 1
            z_obs = {t: [obs[t]['vt'][j] + rq(-rv, rv, 256) for j in range(nout)] for t in range(T_len)}
            g_obs = {t: obs[t]['g'] for t in range(T_len)}
            evs = all_events(nin, nout, P)
            e = rng.choice(evs)
            def rd(rec):
                o, c = rec['ev'][e]
                return (o, c + (rq(-r, r, 256) if r else ZERO))
            ev_o, ev_a = rd(obs[te]), rd(act[te])
            v_, okm = m1_certificate(P, seq, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, D0, e, ev_o, ev_a, r)
            S['n'] += 1
            S['margin'] += okm
            true_fd = changed(tonly[te]['ev'][e], act[te]['ev'][e])
            obs_change = changed(obs[te]['ev'][e], act[te]['ev'][e])
            if v_ == 1:
                S['cert'] += 1
                S['viol'] += not true_fd
            if obs_change and not true_fd:
                S['so'] += 1
                S['so_rej'] += (v_ != 1)
            # N2: the propagation bound itself when the margin holds
            if okm:
                Dvt_all, Dst = prop_bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0)
                for t in range(u, te + 1):
                    a, b = obs[t], tonly[t]
                    bound_checks += 1
                    bad = a['g'] != b['g']
                    Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh = Dst[t]
                    wa, va, xa, ya, ha = a['pre']; wb, vb, xb, yb, hb = b['pre']
                    bad |= any(abs(wa[i][j] - wb[i][j]) > Dw[i][j] for i in range(nin) for j in range(nout))
                    bad |= any(abs(va[j] - vb[j]) > Dv[j] for j in range(nout))
                    bad |= any(abs(xa[i] - xb[i]) > Dx[i] for i in range(nin))
                    bad |= any(abs(ya[j] - yb[j]) > Dy[j] for j in range(nout))
                    bad |= any(ha[j] != hb[j] and not Dh[j] for j in range(nout))
                    bad |= any(abs(a['vt'][j] - b['vt'][j]) > Dvt_all[t][j] for j in range(nout))
                    bound_bad += bad
                    for ee in evs:
                        ev_bound_checks += 1
                        oa_, ca_ = a['ev'][ee]; ob_, cb_ = b['ev'][ee]
                        Bv = event_bound(P, ee, t, Dvt_all, Dst)
                        if ee[0] == 'CLIP' or (ee[0] == 'FIN' and P['mode'] == 'delay' and Dst[t][4][ee[1]]):
                            ev_bound_bad += abs(ca_ - cb_) > Bv
                        else:
                            ev_bound_bad += (oa_ != ob_) or abs(ca_ - cb_) > Bv
            # N3: zero-channel correspondence (every kind, one by one)
            z0 = {t: list(tonly[t]['vt']) for t in range(T_len)}
            g0 = {t: tonly[t]['g'] for t in range(T_len)}
            for ee in evs:
                v0, ok0 = m1_certificate(P, seq, u, te, z0, ZERO, g0, zeros_D(nin, nout), ee, tonly[te]['ev'][ee], act[te]['ev'][ee])
                exact = changed(tonly[te]['ev'][ee], act[te]['ev'][ee])
                corr_tot += 1
                corr_bad += ((v0 == 1) != exact) or not ok0
                kk = corr_kinds.setdefault(ee[0], [0, 0])
                kk[0] += 1; kk[1] += exact
say('N1 soundness of Proposition M-1 (recursion with switches; 400 random instances per cell, 3 inputs; 2 gating units for 𝒦_g and third factor, 3 gating units for 𝒦_PA^δ and 𝒦_PA^≺; 6 beats; all event kinds sampled at random)')
say('  class   (plastic, bap)  inst  ρ=0  ρ=1  margin   v=1  viol   side-effect false changes/rejected')
tot_viol = tot_cert = tot_so = tot_rej = 0
for cls in CLASSES:
    for combo in ALLC:
        S = stats[(cls, combo)]
        say(f'  {cls:6s}  ({int(combo[0])}, {int(combo[1])})          {S["n"]:4d}  {S["rho0"]:3d}  {S["rho1"]:3d}  {S["margin"]:6d}   {S["cert"]:4d}  {S["viol"]:3d}   {S["so"]:3d}/{S["so_rej"]:3d}')
        tot_viol += S['viol']; tot_cert += S['cert']; tot_so += S['so']; tot_rej += S['so_rej']
say(f'  total: verdict 1 {tot_cert}, violations {tot_viol}; false changes caused by side effects {tot_so}, rejected {tot_rej}')
say(f'  Lemma R1′ (pre-beat value + component sum = post-beat value, including FIN and HCOPY): {r1_ticks} beats, mismatches {r1_bad}')
say()
say(f'N2 propagation bound: {bound_checks} beats where the margin holds, {bound_bad} with differing gates or with some node difference / gate-input difference exceeding D; {ev_bound_checks} event-component difference comparisons, {ev_bound_bad} exceeding B_e or differing in occurrence (where occurrence is determined by the gate)')
# N2 targeted table: unit w side effect, no input; compare the actual |Δw| with the recursion
say('  targeted table of switch coefficients (single input, single output, w side effect 1/5, input inactive, no injection, 3 beats; columns: plastic, bap, ρ, actual |Δw|(1..3), recursion D_w(1..3))')
for pl, bp in ALLC:
    for rho in (F(0), F(1)):
        Pq = {'lam': [F(0)], 'theta': [F(5)], 'vres': [F(0)], 'dx': F(1, 2), 'dy': F(1, 2), 'Ap': F(1, 4), 'Am': F(1, 4), 'rho': rho,
              'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': pl, 'bap': bp, 'b': [[F(1, 2)]], 'gate_of': [0], 'mode': 'g'}
        stq = ([[F(1)]], [F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0)], [0])
        sq = [([0], [F(0)])] * 4
        a = run(Pq, stq, sq, 0, {}, {('w', 0, 0): F(1, 5)})
        b = run(Pq, stq, sq)
        act_d = [abs(a[t]['pre'][0][0][0] - b[t]['pre'][0][0][0]) for t in (1, 2, 3)]
        Dvt_q, Dst_q = prop_bounds(Pq, sq, 0, 3, {t: a[t]['g'] for t in range(4)}, ([[F(1, 5)]], [ZERO], [ZERO], [ZERO], [0]))
        rec_d = [Dst_q[t][0][0][0] for t in (1, 2, 3)]
        say(f'    {int(pl)} {int(bp)} ρ={rho}  actual {[str(q) for q in act_d]}  recursion {[str(q) for q in rec_d]}')
        assert act_d == rec_d
say()
say(f'N3 zero channel (no side effects, r_v = r = 0): disagreements of M-1 verdict 1 with “the realized σ′ changes e” {corr_bad}/{corr_tot}; by kind (comparisons / of which e changes):')
say('  ' + ', '.join(f'{k} {v[0]}/{v[1]}' for k, v in sorted(corr_kinds.items())))
say()
# ───────────────────────── N4 closure of the extension classes ─────────────────────────
dag_ticks = dag_bad = dag_order_bad = 0
cyc_ticks = cyc_zero = cyc_multi = 0
for _ in range(600):
    P, seq, st0, nin, nout = rand_instance('dag', rng.choice(ALLC), nout=4)
    recs = run(P, st0, seq)
    # another topological order
    pos = {j: n for n, j in enumerate(P['order'])}
    import itertools
    alt = None
    for perm in itertools.permutations(range(nout)):
        pp = {j: n for n, j in enumerate(perm)}
        if all(not (P['U'][k][j] != 0) or pp[k] < pp[j] for k in range(nout) for j in range(nout)) and list(perm) != P['order']:
            alt = list(perm); break
    recs2 = run({**P, 'order': alt}, st0, seq) if alt else recs
    for t, rec in enumerate(recs):
        dag_ticks += 1
        sols = simultaneous_solutions(P, rec['pre'], *seq[t])
        dag_bad += sols != [tuple(int(a) for a in rec['g'])]
        dag_order_bad += recs2[t]['g'] != rec['g']
    # same-beat cycle: modify U to contain a 2-cycle
    Pc = dict(P); U = [row[:] for row in P['U']]
    U[0][1] = rq(F(1, 4), F(1)); U[1][0] = rq(F(1, 4), F(1)) * (1 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1)
    Pc['U'] = U
    for t, rec in enumerate(recs):
        cyc_ticks += 1
        n = len(simultaneous_solutions(Pc, rec['pre'], *seq[t]))
        cyc_zero += n == 0
        cyc_multi += n >= 2
say(f'N4 𝒦_PA^≺ (4 gating units, random DAG): {dag_ticks} beats, simultaneous-equation solutions ≠ the unique sequential solution {dag_bad}, gates differ after changing the topological order {dag_order_bad}; '
    f'modified to contain a same-beat 2-cycle: of {cyc_ticks} beats, no solution {cyc_zero}, multiple solutions {cyc_multi} (cycles are outside this paper)')
say()
# ───────────────────────── N5 Proposition F-1 ─────────────────────────
def fly_model(mode, Umd):
    """γ1pedc-type conditioning model: 4 KCs at the entries (0 and 1 are paired-odor KCs), gating unit 0 = MBON, 1 = DAN;
    gate_of(MBON) = DAN (third factor), gate_of(DAN) = DAN; MBON → DAN firing input U[0][1] = Umd; DAN gives no firing input to MBON."""
    P = {'lam': [F(1, 2), F(1, 2)], 'theta': [F(1), F(5, 4)], 'vres': [F(0), F(0)], 'dx': F(1, 2), 'dy': F(1, 2),
         'Ap': F(1, 8), 'Am': F(1, 8), 'rho': F(1, 8), 'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': True, 'bap': True,
         'b': [[F(1, 2), F(1, 4)] for _ in range(4)], 'gate_of': [1, 1], 'mode': mode,
         'U': [[F(0), Umd], [F(0), F(0)]]}
    if mode == 'dag':
        P['order'] = [0, 1]
    assert validate(P, 4, 2)[0]
    return P

f1 = {m: {'n': 0, 'flip': 0, 'pred_ok': 0, 'cert_dan': 0, 'cert_ev': 0, 'cert_min': 0, 'viol': 0} for m in ('delay', 'dag')}
block_cmp = block_bad = 0
example = None
for mode in ('delay', 'dag'):
    lag = 1 if mode == 'delay' else 0
    for _ in range(2500):
        Umd = rq(F(1, 2), F(3, 2)) * (1 if rng.random() < 0.7 else -1)
        P = fly_model(mode, Umd)
        T_len = 8
        seq = []
        for t in range(T_len):
            s = [1 if rng.random() < 0.5 else 0 for _ in range(4)]
            seq.append((s, [rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2)), rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2))]))
        st0 = ([[rq(F(1, 4), F(3, 2), 16), rq(F(0), F(1), 16)] for _ in range(4)], [rq(F(0), F(1, 2)), rq(F(0), F(1, 2))],
               [rq(F(0), F(1)) for _ in range(4)], [rq(F(0), F(1)), rq(F(0), F(1))], [0, 0])
        u = rng.randint(0, 3)
        setting = {(0, 0): rq(F(0), F(2), 16), (1, 0): rq(F(0), F(2), 16)}
        beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 16), 64)
        rv = rq(F(0), F(1, 32), 64)
        act = run(P, st0, seq)
        st_u = act[u]['pre']
        # side effects: non-T KC→MBON positions, DAN-column positions, MBON membrane
        Dw = [[ZERO, ZERO] for _ in range(4)]
        side = {}
        for (i, j) in [(2, 0), (3, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1)]:
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                lo_, up_ = max(-beta, P['lo'] - st_u[0][i][j]), min(beta, P['hi'] - st_u[0][i][j])
                Dw[i][j] = max(-lo_, up_); side[('w', i, j)] = rng.choice([lo_, up_])
        Dv = [beta if rng.random() < 0.5 else ZERO, ZERO]
        if Dv[0]:
            side[('v', 0)] = rng.choice([-beta, beta])
        D0 = (Dw, Dv, [ZERO] * 4, [ZERO] * 2, [0, 0])
        obs = run(P, st0, seq, u, setting, side)
        tonly = run(P, st0, seq, u, setting, {})
        z_obs = {t: [obs[t]['vt'][j] + rq(-rv, rv, 256) for j in range(2)] for t in range(T_len)}
        z_act = {t: [act[t]['vt'][j] + rq(-rv, rv, 256) for j in range(2)] for t in range(T_len)}
        g_obs = {t: obs[t]['g'] for t in range(T_len)}
        R = f1[mode]
        R['n'] += 1
        # first MBON flip beat t1 (from readings); requires t1 + lag ≤ T_len − 1
        t1 = next((t for t in range(u, T_len) if obs[t]['g'][0] != act[t]['g'][0]), None)
        if t1 is not None and t1 + lag <= T_len - 1:
            Dvt_all, Dst = prop_bounds(P, seq, u, t1 + lag, g_obs, D0)
            from t13_gate import margins_ok
            if margins_ok(P, u, t1 + lag, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dvt_all):
                R['flip'] += 1
                dz = z_obs[t1 + lag][1] - z_act[t1 + lag][1]
                pred = Umd * (int(obs[t1]['g'][0]) - int(act[t1]['g'][0]))
                R['pred_ok'] += abs(dz - pred) <= Dvt_all[t1 + lag][1] + 2 * rv
                for e in [('YIN', 1), ('PLUS', 0, 0), ('PLUS', 1, 0), ('MINUS', 0, 0), ('MINUS', 1, 0)]:
                    tt = t1 + lag
                    v_, ok = m1_certificate(P, seq, u, tt, z_obs, rv, g_obs, D0, e, obs[tt]['ev'][e], act[tt]['ev'][e])
                    if v_ == 1:
                        if e[0] == 'YIN':
                            R['cert_dan'] += 1
                        elif e[0] == 'PLUS':
                            R['cert_ev'] += 1
                        else:
                            R['cert_min'] += 1
                        R['viol'] += not changed(tonly[tt]['ev'][e], act[tt]['ev'][e])
                        if example is None and mode == 'delay' and e[0] == 'YIN' and beta > 0 and (Dv[0] or any(Dw[i][j] for i in range(4) for j in range(2))):
                            Dhat = ([[F(1) if Dw[i][j] else ZERO for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)], [F(1) if Dv[0] else ZERO, ZERO],
                                    [ZERO] * 4, [ZERO] * 2, [0, 0])
                            ks = beta_star(P, seq, u, tt, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dhat)
                            example = (Umd, u, t1, tt, beta, rv, ks, [int(a['g'][0]) for a in obs], [int(a['g'][0]) for a in act],
                                       [int(a['g'][1]) for a in obs], [int(a['g'][1]) for a in act])
        # blocked return: U = 0; with only T set (several random settings), compare DAN firing and the DAN-gated KC→MBON events
        if rng.random() < 0.4:
            Pb = fly_model(mode, F(0))
            ab = run(Pb, st0, seq)
            for _k in range(3):
                stg = {(0, 0): rq(F(0), F(2), 16), (1, 0): rq(F(0), F(2), 16)}
                tb = run(Pb, st0, seq, u, stg, {})
                for t in range(T_len):
                    for e in [('YIN', 1), ('RESET', 1)] + [(k, i, 0) for k in ('PLUS', 'MINUS') for i in range(4)] + \
                             [(k, i, 1) for k in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP', 'SYN') for i in range(4)]:
                        block_cmp += 1
                        block_bad += changed(tb[t]['ev'][e], ab[t]['ev'][e])
for mode in ('delay', 'dag'):
    R = f1[mode]
    name = '𝒦_PA^δ (main version, return delayed by one beat)' if mode == 'delay' else '𝒦_PA^≺ (same beat, order MBON ≺ DAN)'
    say(f'N5 Proposition F-1, {name}: {R["n"]} random instances; MBON flips for the first time and the margin holds on [u, t1 + {1 if mode == "delay" else 0}] in {R["flip"]} cases, '
        f'the return-component prediction |Δz_DAN − U·Δg_MBON| ≤ D_vt,DAN + 2r_v holds in {R["pred_ok"]} cases; M-1 adjudicates a change in DAN firing in {R["cert_dan"]} cases, '
        f'a change in the DAN-gated paired KC→MBON potentiation events (occurrence) in {R["cert_ev"]} cases, a change in the depression events MINUS (component −A₋y′_DAN) in {R["cert_min"]} cases; violations {R["viol"]}')
say(f'   blocked return (U_MBON→DAN = 0): with only T set, DAN firing, DAN reset and DAN-gated / DAN-column events compared with the baseline {block_cmp} times, changed {block_bad} times')
if example:
    Umd, u, t1, tt, beta, rv, ks, gmo, gma, gdo, gda = example
    say(f'   example (𝒦_PA^δ): U = {Umd}, setting beat u = {u}, MBON firing setting experiment {gmo} / baseline {gma}, DAN firing {gdo} / {gda}; '
        f'first flip t1 = {t1}, DAN changes at {tt} (M-1 verdict 1); declared side effect β = {beta}, r_v = {rv}; intervention bound under the unit profile κ* = {ks[0]} ({"strict" if ks[1] else "endpoint included"})')
say()
# ───────────────────────── N6 Proposition M-2 ─────────────────────────
def rand_k1(nin):
    P = rand_model(nin, 1, 'g', (True, True) if rng.random() < 0.5 else rng.choice(ALLC))
    P['vres'] = [F(0)]
    return P

tw_tot = tw_ok = tw_cons = tw_rec = tw_T = tw_fd0 = tw_conflict = tw_down = 0
for _ in range(6000):
    nin = 3
    P = rand_k1(nin)
    if P['lam'][0] == 0:
        continue
    alphabet = ['A', 'B']
    patA = {'none': (0, 0, 0)}
    for a in alphabet:
        patA[a] = tuple(1 if rng.random() < 0.6 else 0 for _ in range(nin))
    T_len = 6
    labels = [rng.choice(alphabet + ['none']) if rng.random() < 0.15 else rng.choice(alphabet) for _ in range(T_len)]
    IA = [[rq(F(-1, 2), F(1))] for _ in range(T_len)]
    seqA = [(list(patA[a]), I) for a, I in zip(labels, IA)]
    st0 = ([[rq(F(0), F(2), 16)] for _ in range(nin)], [rq(F(0), F(1))], [rq(F(0), F(1)) for _ in range(nin)], [rq(F(0), F(1))], [0])
    u = rng.randint(0, T_len - 2)
    te = rng.randint(u, T_len - 1)
    Tin = {0}
    setting = {(0, 0): rq(F(1, 4), F(2), 16)}
    baseA = run(P, st0, seqA)
    setA = run(P, st0, seqA, u, setting, {})
    ga = [baseA[t]['g'][0] for t in range(T_len)]
    go = [setA[t]['g'][0] for t in range(T_len)]
    if not (all(not ga[t] and not go[t] for t in range(u, te)) and ga[te] != go[te]):
        continue
    tw = m2_twin(P, st0, labels, patA, IA, u, te, Tin, F(1, 4), ga, go)
    if tw is None:
        continue
    tw_tot += 1
    tw_down += ga[te] and not go[te]
    # conflict case: the same label occurs both before u and within [u, te], and the T component of pat_A for that label is 1 (a construction that rewrites the pattern vector beat by beat would give two pats)
    pre_l = set(labels[:u]); post_l = set(labels[u:te + 1])
    tw_conflict += any(patA[a][0] == 1 for a in pre_l & post_l)
    seqB = tw['seqB']
    bB = run(P, st0, seqB)
    sB = run(P, st0, seqB, u, setting, tw['side'])
    tB = run(P, st0, seqB, u, setting, {})
    # pat_B consistency: the pattern used at each beat equals the global pat_B(label), pat_B(none) = 0, and each label corresponds to only one pattern
    seen = {}
    cons = all(seen.setdefault(a, p) == p and p == tw['patB'][a] for a, p in tw['used']) and tw['patB']['none'] == (0, 0, 0)
    cons &= all(tuple(seqB[t][0]) == tw['patB'][labels[t]] for t in range(te + 1))
    tw_cons += cons
    # complete visible record: same label sequence; firing of the two runs identical beat by beat on [0, te]
    rec = [bB[t]['g'][0] for t in range(te + 1)] == ga[:te + 1] and [sB[t]['g'][0] for t in range(te + 1)] == go[:te + 1]
    tw_rec += rec
    # T is never read; in B, setting only T (and three further random settings) does not change YIN@te
    tnr = all(seqB[t][0][0] == 0 for t in range(te + 1))
    tw_T += tnr
    fd0 = not changed(tB[te]['ev'][('YIN', 0)], bB[te]['ev'][('YIN', 0)])
    for _k in range(3):
        alt = run(P, st0, seqB, rng.randint(0, te), {(0, 0): rq(F(0), F(2), 16)}, {})
        fd0 &= all(alt[t]['g'] == bB[t]['g'] for t in range(te + 1))
    tw_fd0 += fd0
    tw_ok += cons and rec and tnr and fd0
say(f'N6 Proposition M-2 (global pat_B twin; alphabet {{A, B, none}}, labels reused): {tw_tot} single-flip instances (of which {tw_down} downward flips, '
    f'{tw_conflict} conflict cases where the same label occurs both before u and in [u, t_e] and the T component of pat_A is 1); pat_B globally consistent {tw_cons}, '
    f'complete visible record on [0, t_e] identical {tw_rec}, T never read throughout {tw_T}, the setting of T in B changes neither YIN@t_e nor firing {tw_fd0}; all four hold {tw_ok}')
say()
say('Run finished.')
~~~

A.10  run\_f1.py  Run entry: threshold-crossing conditions, event-by-event declaration and intervention bounds of F-1 (8.1)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization: finite verification F0–F3 of Proposition F-1 (random seed 20261107; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text).
F0 negative control: the MBON has flipped and the DAN input has moved by U_MD·Δg_M, but the DAN does not cross the threshold; the robust threshold-crossing condition fails, the target events are unchanged, and M-1 gives ⊥
F1 positive example of robust DAN threshold crossing, with per-event switch contrasts (bap off, KC not activated, A₋ = 0)
F2 random verification of F-1 (5) (6) (𝒦_PA^δ and 𝒦_PA^≺; the four plastic/bap combinations, A₋ including 0): the crossing condition holds ⇒ the DAN flips at t₂ and each event changes / stays unchanged as declared
F3 branches of the κ threshold (zero denominator, no positive denominator, negative headroom, zero headroom on the non-firing side) and sufficiency of the a priori design threshold
"""
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import random
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_gate import run, prop_bounds, margins_ok, changed, m1_certificate, validate
from t13_f1 import f1_cross, f1_event_change, kappa_read, kappa_design
rng = random.Random(20261107)
ZERO = F(0)

def rq(lo, hi, den=8):
    return F(rng.randint(int(lo * den), int(hi * den)), den)

def say(x=''):
    print(x)

def tiny(thD, bap=True, Am=F(1, 8), kc=1):
    P = {'lam': [F(0), F(0)], 'theta': [F(1, 2), thD], 'vres': [F(0), F(0)], 'dx': F(0), 'dy': F(0), 'Ap': F(1, 8), 'Am': Am,
         'rho': F(0), 'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': True, 'bap': bap, 'b': [[F(0), F(0)]], 'gate_of': [1, 1], 'mode': 'delay',
         'U': [[F(0), F(1)], [F(0), F(0)]]}
    st = ([[F(1, 4), F(0)]], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [0, 0])
    seq = [([1], [F(0), F(0)]), ([kc], [F(0), F(0)])]
    return P, st, seq

def tiny_case(thD, **kw):
    P, st, seq = tiny(thD, **kw)
    act = run(P, st, seq); obs = run(P, st, seq, 0, {(0, 0): F(3, 4)}, {})
    zero = ([[F(0), F(0)]], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [0, 0])
    z = {t: r['vt'] for t, r in enumerate(obs)}; g = {t: r['g'] for t, r in enumerate(obs)}
    dv, _ = prop_bounds(P, seq, 0, 1, g, zero)
    ok = margins_ok(P, 0, 1, z, ZERO, g, dv)
    shift = F(1) * (int(obs[0]['g'][0]) - int(act[0]['g'][0]))
    cross = f1_cross(P['theta'][1], act[1]['vt'][1], ZERO, shift)
    res = {}
    for e in [('YIN', 1), ('RESET', 1), ('PLUS', 0, 0), ('MINUS', 0, 0)]:
        ch = changed(obs[1]['ev'][e], act[1]['ev'][e])
        v_ = m1_certificate(P, seq, 0, 1, z, ZERO, g, zero, e, obs[1]['ev'][e], act[1]['ev'][e])[0]
        pred = f1_event_change(P, e[0], 0, seq[1][0]) if cross else False
        res[e[0]] = (ch, v_, pred)
    return ok, obs[0]['g'][0] != act[0]['g'][0], obs[1]['vt'][1] - act[1]['vt'][1], cross, res

# F0
ok, flip, dvt, cross, res = tiny_case(F(10))
say('F0 negative control (θ_D = 10, the DAN fires in neither run): margins hold %s, MBON flips at beat 0 %s, DAN gating input moves by %s (= U_MD·Δg_M), robust crossing condition %s;' % (ok, flip, dvt, cross))
say('   beat-1 events (actual change / M-1 verdict / change declared by F-1 (6)): ' + ', '.join(f'{k} {v[0]}/{v[1]}/{v[2]}' for k, v in res.items()))
assert ok and flip and dvt == 1 and cross is None and not any(v[0] or v[1] or v[2] for v in res.values())
say()
# F1
say('F1 robust-crossing positive example and per-event contrasts (θ_D = 1/2; otherwise as in F0):')
for name, kw in [('all on', {}), ('bap off', {'bap': False}), ('beat 1 KC not activated', {'kc': 0}), ('A₋ = 0', {'Am': F(0)})]:
    ok, flip, dvt, cross, res = tiny_case(F(1, 2), **kw)
    say(f'   {name}: margins {ok}, crossing {cross}; ' + ', '.join(f'{k} {v[0]}/{v[1]}/{v[2]}' for k, v in res.items()))
    assert ok and cross == 'up'
    for k, (ch, v_, pred) in res.items():
        assert ch == pred and (v_ == 1) == ch
say()
# F2
def fly(mode, Umd, combo, Am):
    P = {'lam': [F(1, 2), F(1, 2)], 'theta': [F(1), F(5, 4)], 'vres': [F(0), F(0)], 'dx': F(1, 2), 'dy': F(1, 2),
         'Ap': F(1, 8), 'Am': Am, 'rho': F(1, 8), 'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': combo[0], 'bap': combo[1],
         'b': [[F(1, 2), F(1, 4)] for _ in range(4)], 'gate_of': [1, 1], 'mode': mode, 'U': [[F(0), Umd], [F(0), F(0)]]}
    if mode == 'dag':
        P['order'] = [0, 1]
    assert validate(P, 4, 2)[0]
    return P

ALLC = [(False, False), (False, True), (True, False), (True, True)]
stat = {m: {'n': 0, 'cond': 0, 'flip_ok': 0, 'ev': 0, 'ev_ok': 0, 'nocross': 0, 'nocross_flip': 0, 'cert': 0, 'cert_bad': 0, 'margin_t2': 0}
        for m in ('delay', 'dag')}
for mode in ('delay', 'dag'):
    lag = 1 if mode == 'delay' else 0
    S = stat[mode]
    for _ in range(4000):
        Umd = rq(F(1, 4), F(3, 2)) * (1 if rng.random() < 0.7 else -1)
        Am = F(0) if rng.random() < 0.2 else rq(F(1, 8), F(1, 2))
        P = fly(mode, Umd, rng.choice(ALLC), Am)
        T_len = 8
        seq = [([1 if rng.random() < 0.5 else 0 for _ in range(4)], [rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2)), rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2))]) for _ in range(T_len)]
        st0 = ([[rq(F(1, 4), F(3, 2), 16), rq(F(0), F(1), 16)] for _ in range(4)], [rq(F(0), F(1, 2)), rq(F(0), F(1, 2))],
               [rq(F(0), F(1)) for _ in range(4)], [rq(F(0), F(1)), rq(F(0), F(1))], [0, 0])
        u = rng.randint(0, 3)
        setting = {(0, 0): rq(F(0), F(2), 16), (1, 0): rq(F(0), F(2), 16)}
        beta = rq(F(0), F(1, 16), 64); rv = rq(F(0), F(1, 32), 64)
        act = run(P, st0, seq); st_u = act[u]['pre']
        Dw = [[ZERO, ZERO] for _ in range(4)]; side = {}
        for (i, j) in [(2, 0), (3, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1)]:
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                lo_, up_ = max(-beta, P['lo'] - st_u[0][i][j]), min(beta, P['hi'] - st_u[0][i][j])
                Dw[i][j] = max(-lo_, up_); side[('w', i, j)] = rng.choice([lo_, up_])
        Dv = [beta if rng.random() < 0.5 else ZERO, ZERO]
        if Dv[0]:
            side[('v', 0)] = rng.choice([-beta, beta])
        D0 = (Dw, Dv, [ZERO] * 4, [ZERO] * 2, [0, 0])
        obs = run(P, st0, seq, u, setting, side); tau = run(P, st0, seq, u, setting, {})
        z_obs = {t: [obs[t]['vt'][j] + rq(-rv, rv, 256) for j in range(2)] for t in range(T_len)}
        z_act = {t: [act[t]['vt'][j] + rq(-rv, rv, 256) for j in range(2)] for t in range(T_len)}
        g_obs = {t: obs[t]['g'] for t in range(T_len)}
        S['n'] += 1
        t1 = next((t for t in range(u, T_len) if obs[t]['g'][0] != act[t]['g'][0]), None)
        if t1 is None or t1 + lag > T_len - 1:
            continue
        t2 = t1 + lag
        Dvt_all, _ = prop_bounds(P, seq, u, t1, g_obs, D0)
        if not margins_ok(P, u, t1, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dvt_all):
            continue
        shift = Umd * (int(obs[t1]['g'][0]) - int(act[t1]['g'][0]))
        cross = f1_cross(P['theta'][1], z_act[t2][1], rv, shift)
        flipped = tau[t2]['g'][1] != act[t2]['g'][1]
        if cross is None:
            # crossing not guaranteed: only when there is exactly no crossing (θ not inside the shift interval), count whether the DAN stays put
            exact_cross = (act[t2]['vt'][1] >= P['theta'][1]) != (act[t2]['vt'][1] + shift >= P['theta'][1])
            if not exact_cross:
                S['nocross'] += 1
                S['nocross_flip'] += flipped
            continue
        S['cond'] += 1
        S['flip_ok'] += flipped and (tau[t2]['g'][1] == (cross == 'up'))
        evs = [('YIN', 1), ('RESET', 1)] + [(k, i, 0) for k in ('PLUS', 'MINUS') for i in range(4)]
        for e in evs:
            S['ev'] += 1
            pred = f1_event_change(P, e[0], e[1] if len(e) == 3 else 0, seq[t2][0])
            S['ev_ok'] += pred == changed(tau[t2]['ev'][e], act[t2]['ev'][e])
        Dvt2, _ = prop_bounds(P, seq, u, t2, g_obs, D0)
        if margins_ok(P, u, t2, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dvt2):
            S['margin_t2'] += 1
            for e in evs:
                v_ = m1_certificate(P, seq, u, t2, z_obs, rv, g_obs, D0, e, obs[t2]['ev'][e], act[t2]['ev'][e])[0]
                if v_ == 1:
                    S['cert'] += 1
                    S['cert_bad'] += not changed(tau[t2]['ev'][e], act[t2]['ev'][e])
for mode in ('delay', 'dag'):
    S = stat[mode]
    say(f'F2 F-1 (5) (6), {"𝒦_PA^δ (t₂ = t₁ + 1)" if mode == "delay" else "𝒦_PA^≺ (t₂ = t₁)"}: {S["n"]} instances; MBON first flip with margins holding on [u, t₁] and '
        f'the robust crossing condition holding: {S["cond"]} cases, DAN flips at t₂ in the indicated direction in {S["flip_ok"]}; t₂ events declared {S["ev"]} times, agreeing with the actual change {S["ev_ok"]} times; '
        f'θ_D not inside the shift interval (negative control) {S["nocross"]} cases, DAN flips in {S["nocross_flip"]}; margins also hold on [u, t₂] in {S["margin_t2"]} cases, M-1 gives 1 {S["cert"]} times, violations {S["cert_bad"]}')
    assert S['flip_ok'] == S['cond'] and S['ev_ok'] == S['ev'] and S['nocross_flip'] == 0 and S['cert_bad'] == 0
say()
# F3 κ threshold
Pk = {'lam': [F(1, 2)], 'theta': [F(1)], 'vres': [F(0)], 'dx': F(1, 2), 'dy': F(1, 2), 'Ap': F(0), 'Am': F(0), 'rho': F(0),
      'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': False, 'bap': False, 'b': [[F(0)]], 'gate_of': [0], 'mode': 'g'}
sqk = [([1], [F(0)])]
unit_w = ([[F(1)]], [F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0)], [0])
none_D = ([[F(0)]], [F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0)], [0])
cases = [('zero denominator, all satisfied (no positive denominator)', {0: [F(3, 2)]}, {0: [True]}, none_D),
         ('zero denominator, negative headroom on the firing side', {0: [F(9, 10)]}, {0: [True]}, none_D),
         ('zero denominator, zero headroom on the non-firing side', {0: [F(1)]}, {0: [False]}, none_D),
         ('positive denominator, firing side κ ≤ m/D̂', {0: [F(3, 2)]}, {0: [True]}, unit_w),
         ('positive denominator, non-firing side κ < m/D̂', {0: [F(1, 2)]}, {0: [False]}, unit_w),
         ('positive denominator, negative headroom on the firing side', {0: [F(9, 10)]}, {0: [True]}, unit_w),
         ('positive denominator, zero headroom on the non-firing side', {0: [F(1)]}, {0: [False]}, unit_w)]
say('F3 κ threshold branches (single input, single output, r_v = 0):')
for name, z, g, Dh in cases:
    say(f'   {name}: {kappa_read(Pk, sqk, 0, 0, z, ZERO, g, Dh)}')
ok_n = ok_good = none_n = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    nin, nout = 3, 2
    P = {'lam': [rq(F(0), F(1)) for _ in range(nout)], 'theta': [rq(F(1, 2), F(2)) for _ in range(nout)], 'vres': [F(0)] * nout,
         'dx': rq(F(0), F(1)), 'dy': rq(F(0), F(1)), 'Ap': rq(F(0), F(1, 2)), 'Am': rq(F(0), F(1, 2)), 'rho': rq(F(0), F(1)),
         'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': rng.random() < 0.5, 'bap': rng.random() < 0.5,
         'b': [[F(1, 4)] * nout for _ in range(nin)], 'gate_of': [0, 1], 'mode': 'g'}
    T_len = 5
    seq = [([1 if rng.random() < 0.6 else 0 for _ in range(nin)], [rq(F(-1, 2), F(1)) for _ in range(nout)]) for _ in range(T_len)]
    st0 = ([[rq(F(0), F(2), 16) for _ in range(nout)] for _ in range(nin)], [rq(F(0), F(1)) for _ in range(nout)],
           [rq(F(0), F(1)) for _ in range(nin)], [rq(F(0), F(1)) for _ in range(nout)], [0] * nout)
    u = rng.randint(0, 2); te = rng.randint(u, T_len - 1)
    setting = {(0, 0): rq(F(1, 4), F(2), 16)}
    tau = run(P, st0, seq, u, setting, {})
    zeta = rq(F(0), F(1, 16), 64); rv = rq(F(0), F(1, 32), 64)
    vt_pred = {t: [tau[t]['vt'][j] + rq(-zeta, zeta, 256) for j in range(nout)] for t in range(T_len)}
    g_pred = {t: tau[t]['g'] for t in range(T_len)}
    st_u = tau[u]['pre']
    Dhat = ([[F(1) if (i, j) != (0, 0) and rng.random() < 0.5 else ZERO for j in range(nout)] for i in range(nin)],
            [F(1) if rng.random() < 0.5 else ZERO for _ in range(nout)], [ZERO] * nin, [ZERO] * nout, [0] * nout)
    status, k, strict = kappa_design(P, seq, u, te, vt_pred, zeta, rv, g_pred, Dhat)
    if status == 'none':
        none_n += 1
        continue
    kap = (k * rq(F(0), F(1), 16)) if status == 'bound' else F(1, 4)
    if status == 'bound' and strict and kap == k:
        kap = k / 2
    # actual side effects: drawn within the unit profile × κ (for w, kept inside the valid box)
    side = {}
    Dact = ([[ZERO] * nout for _ in range(nin)], [ZERO] * nout, [ZERO] * nin, [ZERO] * nout, [0] * nout)
    for i in range(nin):
        for j in range(nout):
            if Dhat[0][i][j]:
                lo_, up_ = max(-kap, P['lo'] - st_u[0][i][j]), min(kap, P['hi'] - st_u[0][i][j])
                side[('w', i, j)] = rng.choice([lo_, up_])
    for j in range(nout):
        if Dhat[1][j]:
            side[('v', j)] = rng.choice([-kap, kap])
    obs = run(P, st0, seq, u, setting, side)
    z = {t: [obs[t]['vt'][j] + rq(-rv, rv, 256) for j in range(nout)] for t in range(T_len)}
    g = {t: obs[t]['g'] for t in range(T_len)}
    D0 = ([[kap * Dhat[0][i][j] for j in range(nout)] for i in range(nin)], [kap * x for x in Dhat[1]], [ZERO] * nin, [ZERO] * nout, [0] * nout)
    Dvt, _ = prop_bounds(P, seq, u, te, g, D0)
    ok_n += 1
    ok_good += margins_ok(P, u, te, z, rv, g, Dvt) and all(g[t] == g_pred[t] for t in range(u, te + 1))
say(f'   a priori design threshold: of 3000 random instances, threshold nonempty in {ok_n} (empty {none_n}); with κ inside the threshold and readings random within the radius, the setting-experiment gating equals the prediction and the margins hold in {ok_good}/{ok_n}')
assert ok_good == ok_n
say()
say('Run finished.')
~~~

A.11  run\_f1\_branch.py  Run entry: separating the three levels of conclusion of F-1 (8.1)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Material Field Concretization: separating the three levels of conclusion of Proposition F-1, F4–F5 (random seed 20261108; calls only t13_gate and t13_f1).
F4 RESET boundary witnesses (one each for 𝒦_PA^δ and 𝒦_PA^≺): the MBON flips and the margins hold on [u, t₂]; RESET_D is given 1 under M-1 (it occurs in both runs, with different components),
   but the DAN does not flip, YIN_D is not given 1, the robust crossing condition fails and PLUS/MINUS are unchanged — a positive event-level verdict does not imply a DAN flip, and (6) is not invoked
F5 random verification separating the two branches: every event-level M-1 verdict 1 is a true FD witness; count the cases in which the DAN does not flip at t₂ (the event-level branch is nonempty);
   whenever YIN_D@t₂ is given 1 under M-1, the DAN flips and the declarations of (6) agree with the actual changes
F0–F3: see run_f1.py.
"""
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import random
from fractions import Fraction as F
from t13_gate import run, validate, prop_bounds, margins_ok, m1_certificate, changed
from t13_f1 import f1_cross, f1_event_change
rng = random.Random(20261108)
ZERO = F(0)

def rq(lo, hi, den=8):
    return F(rng.randint(int(lo * den), int(hi * den)), den)

def say(x=''):
    print(x)

EVS = [('YIN', 1), ('RESET', 1), ('PLUS', 0, 0), ('MINUS', 0, 0)]
# F4
for mode in ('delay', 'dag'):
    P = {'lam': [F(0), F(0)], 'theta': [F(1, 2), F(1, 8)], 'vres': [F(0), F(0)], 'dx': F(0), 'dy': F(0), 'Ap': F(1, 8), 'Am': F(1, 8),
         'rho': F(0), 'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': True, 'bap': True, 'b': [[F(0), F(0)]], 'gate_of': [1, 1], 'mode': mode,
         'U': [[F(0), F(1)], [F(0), F(0)]]}
    if mode == 'dag':
        P['order'] = [0, 1]
    assert validate(P, 1, 2)[0]
    st = ([[F(1, 4), F(1, 4)]], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [0, 0])
    seq = [([1], [F(0), F(0)])] * 2
    act = run(P, st, seq); tau = run(P, st, seq, 0, {(0, 0): F(3, 4)}, {})
    t2 = 1 if mode == 'delay' else 0
    zero = ([[F(0), F(0)]], [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0), F(0)], [0, 0])
    z = {t: r['vt'] for t, r in enumerate(tau)}; g = {t: r['g'] for t, r in enumerate(tau)}
    dv, _ = prop_bounds(P, seq, 0, t2, g, zero)
    ok = margins_ok(P, 0, t2, z, ZERO, g, dv)
    cert = {e[0]: m1_certificate(P, seq, 0, t2, z, ZERO, g, zero, e, tau[t2]['ev'][e], act[t2]['ev'][e])[0] for e in EVS}
    ch = {e[0]: changed(tau[t2]['ev'][e], act[t2]['ev'][e]) for e in EVS}
    cross = f1_cross(P['theta'][1], act[t2]['vt'][1], ZERO, F(1))
    say(f'F4 RESET boundary witness ({"𝒦_PA^δ, t₂ = 1" if mode == "delay" else "𝒦_PA^≺, t₂ = 0"}): margins {ok}; MBON beat 0 {int(act[0]["g"][0])}→{int(tau[0]["g"][0])}; '
        f'DAN vt {act[t2]["vt"][1]}→{tau[t2]["vt"][1]}, firing {act[t2]["g"][1]}→{tau[t2]["g"][1]}; robust crossing {cross}; '
        f'RESET_D {act[t2]["ev"][("RESET", 1)]}→{tau[t2]["ev"][("RESET", 1)]}')
    say('   M-1 verdict / actual change: ' + ', '.join(f'{k} {cert[k]}/{ch[k]}' for k in cert))
    assert ok and act[0]['g'][0] != tau[0]['g'][0] and act[t2]['g'][1] and tau[t2]['g'][1] and cross is None
    assert cert['RESET'] == 1 and cert['YIN'] is None and not ch['PLUS'] and not ch['MINUS'] and cert['PLUS'] is None and cert['MINUS'] is None
say('   Conclusion: an event-level M-1 verdict 1 implies only FD = 1 for that event; it does not imply a DAN flip, and (6) is not invoked.')
say()
# F5
ALLC = [(False, False), (False, True), (True, False), (True, True)]
for mode in ('delay', 'dag'):
    lag = 1 if mode == 'delay' else 0
    n = ev_cert = ev_bad = ev_noflip = yin_cert = yin_flip = yin_ev = yin_ev_ok = 0
    for _ in range(3000):
        Umd = rq(F(1, 4), F(3, 2)) * (1 if rng.random() < 0.7 else -1)
        Am = F(0) if rng.random() < 0.2 else rq(F(1, 8), F(1, 2))
        combo = rng.choice(ALLC)
        P = {'lam': [F(1, 2), F(1, 2)], 'theta': [F(1), F(5, 4)], 'vres': [F(0), F(0)], 'dx': F(1, 2), 'dy': F(1, 2),
             'Ap': F(1, 8), 'Am': Am, 'rho': F(1, 8), 'lo': F(0), 'hi': F(2), 'plastic': combo[0], 'bap': combo[1],
             'b': [[F(1, 2), F(1, 4)] for _ in range(4)], 'gate_of': [1, 1], 'mode': mode, 'U': [[F(0), Umd], [F(0), F(0)]]}
        if mode == 'dag':
            P['order'] = [0, 1]
        T_len = 8
        seq = [([1 if rng.random() < 0.5 else 0 for _ in range(4)], [rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2)), rq(F(-1, 2), F(1, 2))]) for _ in range(T_len)]
        st0 = ([[rq(F(1, 4), F(3, 2), 16), rq(F(0), F(1), 16)] for _ in range(4)], [rq(F(0), F(1, 2)), rq(F(0), F(1, 2))],
               [rq(F(0), F(1)) for _ in range(4)], [rq(F(0), F(1)), rq(F(0), F(1))], [0, 0])
        u = rng.randint(0, 3)
        setting = {(0, 0): rq(F(0), F(2), 16), (1, 0): rq(F(0), F(2), 16)}
        rv = rq(F(0), F(1, 32), 64)
        act = run(P, st0, seq); tau = run(P, st0, seq, u, setting, {})
        z = {t: [tau[t]['vt'][j] + rq(-rv, rv, 256) for j in range(2)] for t in range(T_len)}
        g = {t: tau[t]['g'] for t in range(T_len)}
        t1 = next((t for t in range(u, T_len) if tau[t]['g'][0] != act[t]['g'][0]), None)
        if t1 is None or t1 + lag > T_len - 1:
            continue
        t2 = t1 + lag
        zero = ([[ZERO, ZERO] for _ in range(4)], [ZERO, ZERO], [ZERO] * 4, [ZERO] * 2, [0, 0])
        n += 1
        flip = tau[t2]['g'][1] != act[t2]['g'][1]
        evs = [('YIN', 1), ('RESET', 1), ('LEAK', 1)] + [(k, i, j) for k in ('PLUS', 'MINUS') for i in range(4) for j in range(2)]
        for e in evs:
            ra = act[t2]['ev'][e]
            ro = tau[t2]['ev'][e]
            ro_read = (ro[0], ro[1] + (rq(-rv, rv, 256) if rv else ZERO))
            v_ = m1_certificate(P, seq, u, t2, z, rv, g, zero, e, ro_read, ra, rv)[0]
            if v_ != 1:
                continue
            ev_cert += 1
            ev_bad += not changed(ro, ra)
            ev_noflip += not flip
            if e == ('YIN', 1):
                yin_cert += 1
                yin_flip += flip
                for e2 in [('YIN', 1), ('RESET', 1)] + [(k, i, 0) for k in ('PLUS', 'MINUS') for i in range(4)]:
                    yin_ev += 1
                    yin_ev_ok += f1_event_change(P, e2[0], e2[1] if len(e2) == 3 else 0, seq[t2][0]) == changed(tau[t2]['ev'][e2], act[t2]['ev'][e2])
    say(f'F5 {"𝒦_PA^δ" if mode == "delay" else "𝒦_PA^≺"}: MBON first-flip instances {n}; at t₂, M-1 verdicts 1 on DAN and KC→MBON/DAN column events total {ev_cert}, violations {ev_bad}, '
        f'of which {ev_noflip} with the DAN not flipped (event-level branch, (6) not invoked); YIN_D@t₂ is given 1 {yin_cert} times, DAN flips {yin_flip} times, '
        f'declarations of (6) invoked on this basis {yin_ev} times, agreeing with the actual changes {yin_ev_ok} times')
    assert ev_bad == 0 and yin_flip == yin_cert and yin_ev_ok == yin_ev and ev_noflip > 0
say()
say('Run finished.')
~~~
